> **Current focus:** **Community phase (C1–C5) kicked off; C1 Foundation spec locked, awaiting Chris's review.** Studio section is done; this phase turns `/community` from the H1-era hive-activity-feed placeholder into the social-media-hub layer of the app (Discover = explore strangers' writing; Community = your circle + group activity). Phase decomposes into 5 independent sub-projects following the H1–H5 precedent: **C1 Foundation** (friends + /community shell + activity feed, spec landed today), **C2 Sparks refresh** (port richer features from beehive-books-online prompts), **C3 Reading Lists** (net-new), **C4 Book Clubs** (net-new), **C5 Polish + net-new social features** (mentions, notification prefs, friend-feed prioritization, final UI pass via Claude Design). Phase overview at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-community-phase-overview.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-community-phase-overview.md) locks cross-cutting commitments that constrain every later phase: (1) friend model is Friends + Follows (both — mutual ACCEPTED `friendships` rows gate FRIENDS-tier content; one-way `follows` rows drive low-friction discovery); (2) no friend groups, flat list (Hives + Book Clubs serve named-subgroup niche); (3) activity feed runs on a new `social_activity` append-only event store mirroring `hive_activity` pattern; (4) privacy gates use `areFriends()` + `isBlocked()` helpers, no parallel gate implementations; (5) blocks are global + cascading (delete friendship + follows in both directions atomically); (6) /community is the hub — sub-systems live at own routes (`/friends`, `/hives`, `/sparks`, `/reading-lists`, `/clubs`); (7) visual design deferred to a Claude Design handoff after C1 ships. **C1 spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-c1-community-foundation-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-c1-community-foundation-design.md)** is the full design — locked 11 decisions from brainstorm (friend model B; no groups A; discovery A+B+C+D incl. invite-by-link tokens; management B with block+mute+cancel; IA C hybrid hub with horizontal section rail + lean attention sidebar; feed scope ii friends+followed merged; feed events F1-F7 minus F8-noisy-followed; profile P1+P2+P3+P4+P6; FRIENDS gate A strict-mutual; notifications N1+N2+N4 only; nav user-avatar dropdown gains View profile + Friends + Settings + Sign out). **Data model**: 4 new tables (`social_activity` append-only event store with `(actor_id, created_at DESC)` cursor-pagination index + `(subject_type, subject_id)` cleanup index; `user_blocks` global hard cut with bidirectional read-time enforcement; `user_mutes` viewer-side feed hide; `friend_invites` 14-day one-shot tokens) + 1 new enum `social_activity_type` (9 values for C1, additive enum extensions in C2-C5) + 2 new `notification_type` enum values (`FRIEND_REQUEST`, `FRIEND_ACCEPTED`). Existing `friendships` (PENDING/ACCEPTED) + `follows` tables unchanged — blocks live in their own table because they're global, not a friendship state. **Helpers**: `areFriends()` + `isBlocked()` + `getMutualFriends()` + `recordSocialActivityTx()` (mirrors `recordHiveActivityTx` pattern; called in source tx; privacy-gate at write time — `book_published` only fires if PUBLIC+discoverable, FRIENDS-tier content NEVER flows into feed because feed redistributes to follows which aren't friends). Per-actor-per-target dedupe at 6h window for high-cardinality events (`book_liked`). **Server actions**: 4 new files (`blocks.actions.ts`, `mutes.actions.ts`, `friend-invites.actions.ts`, expanded `friendships.actions.ts`) + replaced `community.actions.ts` with `getCommunityFeedAction({ cursor, limit })` single-SELECT cursor-paginated on the event store filtered by `actor_id IN (friends + follows) MINUS blocked MINUS muted`. Activity event hooks added to 7 source actions (publishBook, saveChapter only when status flips into REVISED/FINAL, toggleBookLike, addComment, submitSparkEntry, createHive, acceptHiveInvite + spark winner-resolution paths). New `searchUsersAction({ query })` for /friends Find-a-friend popover. **Privacy wired**: `canReadBook` extended to call `areFriends` for FRIENDS-tier books and masquerade blocks as `NOT_FOUND` (never reveal block existence). Hive FRIENDS visibility filter added to Discover. **Page-level work**: full rewrites of `/community` (Comfortaa h1 + horizontal section rail of 5 module tiles + 2-col feed/sidebar) and `/friends` (4-tab strip Friends/Requests/Sent/Suggested + username search popover + Invite-by-link Dialog). Profile page `/u/[username]` extended with status pill + smart CTA + mutual-friends count+avatars + friends/followers split + block-aware fetch that renders "This profile is unavailable" for either-direction block + ⋯ kebab with Mute/Block/Report-stub. Nav user-avatar gains DropdownMenu (View profile + Friends + Settings + Sign out — closes the "no way to see your own profile" gap Chris flagged). New `/friend-invite/[token]` route mirrors hive invite-accept (sign-up redirect with `?next=`, atomic claim, sonner toast). **Notifications**: `FRIEND_REQUEST` + `FRIEND_ACCEPTED` types added; writes skipped when either party blocks the other; existing bell list picks up new types via message map. **Test posture**: unit tests for `areFriends` / `isBlocked` / `getMutualFriends` / `recordSocialActivityTx` dedupe; surface-shape tests for 4 new server-action files mirroring `reading-actions.test.ts` + `create-hive-action.test.ts`; manual smoke per AGENTS.md convention. **Implementation phasing**: spec proposes 16-task subagent-driven decomposition (T1 migration → T2-T4 helpers + dedupe → T5-T8 server actions + event hooks → T9-T15 page rewrites + profile + nav + invite route + notifications + Discover hive filter → T16 smoke + AGENTS.md + ship). **Out of scope (deferred to later C-phases or never)**: @-mentions (C5), friends-list visible on profile P5 (C5), per-friend feed prioritization (C5), feed ranking beyond chronological (C5), live "X new posts" indicator (C5), Restrict-Instagram-style (never), Report+moderation queue (Phase 10+), reading-list/book-club event types (C3/C4 extend enum additively), Settings → Blocked/Muted pages (C5 polish — action layer ships in C1), mass-email invite (out — invite-link is the supported path). **23-scenario carry-forward smoke checklist** baked into the spec for post-ship verification (friend request happy path, mutual friends, silent rejection, cancel sent, unfriend, block-deletes-friendship+follows, unblock-doesn't-restore, mute-hides-feed, invite-by-link new-user signup, invite expired/self-invite errors, FRIENDS book access flip, FRIENDS hive Discover surface, feed-event flow for 5 actions, feed dedupe per-book per-6h, feed scope friends+followed, FRIENDS-content-NOT-leaked-to-followers, feed empty state, nav dropdown routes, profile self-view no-CTA, section rail tile routing, notification rendering).

> **Wave 5 UI deviations + design decisions worth knowing:**
> - **T9 community page**: section rail Hives tile routes to `/studio` (Hives section lives there per H1) — defensible but unobvious; smoke target. Real `getUserHivesView()` takes no args (uses requireAuth internally) — plan was wrong about `getUserHivesView(viewerId)`. `listPendingFriendRequestsAction` returns `{ incoming, outgoing }` not `{ received, sent }` — plan was wrong. No `<HiveInvitesCard>` exists in codebase — plan referenced it as "existing"; sidebar instead mounts `<RequestsCard>` + existing `MyHivesPanel` + existing `ActiveSparksPanel`. 5 verb-mapped event types covering all 9 `social_activity_type` values.
> - **T10 friends page**: All consumers use real action names. `sendFriendRequestAction({ recipientId })` (NOT `{ recipientUsername }` from plan). Added `_components/shared.tsx` scope-local helper module for Avatar + EmptyState + cardStyle + relTime (avoids duplicating across 5 tab files). Deleted legacy `friends-tabs.tsx` (3-tab monolith, −268 LOC). InviteLinkDialog mirrors `<ShareBookDialog>` Copy UX with 14-day expiry note.
> - **T11 profile page**: **Complementary approach to existing `<FriendButton>`** — existing button (in profile header) keeps owning Add Friend / Cancel / Accept+Decline / Unfriend. New `<FriendStatusSection>` shows status pill + mutual-friends cluster + a separate ⋯ kebab providing Mute / Block / View-on-Friends only. Unfriend stays on the existing FriendButton dropdown. Avoids two competing primary CTAs. **Block-aware masquerade lands early** (before any data fetch that could leak existence). Stats row gains `friendsCount`. Direct DB query for friendship row id (since `getFriendshipStatusAction` doesn't return it) needed for accept/cancel CTAs.
> - **T12 nav dropdown**: New 4-item menu (View profile gated on username / Friends / Settings / Sign out) replaces the prior popover with Studio/Friends/Billing/SignOut. **Studio and Billing dropped from the menu** — Studio reachable via top-nav main links; Billing reachable as `/settings/billing` child route. Spec §6.4 listed the 4-item shape but didn't explicitly authorize the Studio drop — smoke target.
> - **T13 invite route**: `safeNextPath` (at `lib/utils.ts`) accepts any value starting with `/` not starting with `//` not containing `://` — `/en/friend-invite/<token>` satisfies. Server page bounces unauthed to `/sign-up?next=...` with URL-encoded path, claims on auth, redirects success-with-username to `/u/{inviterUsername}?invite_claimed=1`. 6 error codes + 2 success variants each with tailored copy.

>
> **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase (from C1 end-to-end):**
> 1. **`areFriends` + `isBlocked` are the ONLY canonical privacy helpers** — never write parallel implementations. Both React `cache()`-wrapped for per-request memoization. Used by `canReadBook` (T4), profile-page block masquerade (T11), feed actor filter (T7), Discover hive filter (T15), and any future visibility surface.
> 2. **`recordSocialActivityTx(tx, opts)` is the canonical feed-event writer.** Callers MUST pass their source-action's tx so the activity row commits atomically with the source change. Future social-feed actions (C2 Sparks refresh, C3 Reading Lists, C4 Book Clubs) extend `social_activity_type` enum and write via this helper.
> 3. **Notification writes use `actorId + resourceType + resourceId`** shape — NEVER add a `payload` field (column doesn't exist). Plan's `payload: { requesterId }` was wrong for this schema; canonical write pattern from `social.actions.ts:32-38` is the source of truth.
> 4. **Block tx cascades to delete friendships + follows in both directions.** Future "social tie" tables (e.g. C3 reading-list-follows, C4 club memberships) must extend this cascade in `blockUserAction` if cross-relationship blocking is needed.
> 5. **Subagent-driven parallel waves work cleanly** when file scopes don't overlap — git index lock serializes commits, zero race conditions across the 5 waves of this phase.
> 6. **Status-transition-gated hooks** need the priorStatus comparison INSIDE the same tx (fetch prior row via `tx.query.<table>.findFirst` before the update, capture the status field, then compare after update). T8's `updateChapterStatusAction` is the canonical example.
> 7. **Spec corrections by implementers are right** — when plan and reality diverge (e.g. saveChapterAction vs updateChapterStatusAction; getFriendsAction vs listFriendsAction; `recipientUsername` vs `recipientId`; `{ received, sent }` vs `{ incoming, outgoing }`), trust reality.
> 8. **Block masquerade**: blocked viewers see `NOT_FOUND` (books) or `<ProfileUnavailable>` (profiles) — never reveal block existence. Same pattern applies to any future visibility-aware surface.
> 9. **Spark winner-resolution event actor** is the WINNER, not the spark creator (the actor of a "@x won the Spark" event in @x's followers' feeds is @x).
> 10. **Bell-list copy-map adapter pattern**: when the `NotificationRow.actor` shape lacks a field your click-through wants (e.g. username), route to a less-precise but-still-functional target rather than widening the actor type. Widening is a follow-up.
> 11. **Per-actor-per-target dedupe at 6h window** for high-cardinality events (currently only `book_liked`). Add types to `DEDUPE_ELIGIBLE` in `lib/social/types.ts` if a future event type needs the same protection.
> 12. **Sub-system surfaces live at their own routes** (`/friends`, `/hives` via `/studio`, `/sparks` via `/discover`, future `/reading-lists`, `/clubs`). `/community` stays a lean hub — never grows into a giant index of everything.

Each wave: implementer → spec + code-quality reviews → checkpoint. Net new tests: +37 (438 baseline → 475).

> **Wave 4 SHAs:** T7 [dc608a4](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/dc608a4) `getCommunityFeedAction` rewrite — single cursor-paginated SELECT on `social_activity` filtered by `actor_id IN (friends + follows) MINUS blocked (bidirectional) MINUS muted`, cursor format `(createdAt DESC, id DESC)` base64url JSON tuple, subject hydration via `inArray` grouped by `subjectType` for `book`/`chapter`/`hive` (spark_entry + comment subjects render with raw id — C1 launch deferral, T9 UI handles fallback), `isFriend: friendIds.has(actorId)` in row projection for the subtle brand-yellow left-edge UX. 6 surface-shape tests added. `getSuggestedWritersAction` + `getMyActiveSparksAction` preserved verbatim. `getMyHivesAction` already absent from this file (H1-T10 cleanup). T8 [254d941](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/254d941) 9 `recordSocialActivityTx` hook sites across 5 source-action files (`book.actions.ts`, `chapter.actions.ts`, `social.actions.ts`, `sparks.actions.ts`, `hive.actions.ts`) — 6 of the 9 source actions needed tx conversion (were bare `db.insert`/`update` before). **Hook list with gates:** publishBookAction → `book_published` (discoverable check), `updateChapterStatusAction` (NOT `saveChapterAction` — meaningful spec correction by implementer because `saveChapterAction` only takes content, no status field; updateChapterStatusAction is where status transitions actually happen) → `chapter_posted` (priorStatus check + new status is REVISED/FINAL + book PUBLIC+discoverable), `toggleBookLikeAction` insert branch only → `book_liked` (PUBLIC+discoverable + auto 6h dedupe), `addCommentAction` → `book_commented` (PUBLIC+discoverable + 120-char excerpt payload), `submitSparkEntryAction` → `spark_entry_submitted` (sparks have no privacy field — always fires), `setCreatorChoiceAction` + `getSparkAction` lazy-finalize → `spark_won_creator_choice` / `spark_won_community` (actor = WINNER, not spark creator), `createHiveAction` + `acceptHiveInviteAction` + `joinHiveByLinkAction` (2 paths) → `hive_created` / `hive_joined` (PUBLIC+discoverable). Spec §4.6 lists 9 sites; plan's "7 source actions" count was loose. T14 [77288af](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/77288af) drizzle `notificationTypeEnum` array literal extended with `FRIEND_REQUEST` + `FRIEND_ACCEPTED` at line 26 of `db/schema/social.ts`; `notifications-bell.tsx` got 2 LABELS entries + 2 click-handler branches routing FRIEND_REQUEST → `/en/friends?tab=requests` and FRIEND_ACCEPTED → `/en/friends` (NotificationRow.actor exposes `{ name, image }` only — no username field — so `/u/{username}` routing would require widening the actor type, deferred; `/en/friends` is the right adaptation). Hardcoded `/en/` locale matches existing HIVE_INVITE pattern (pre-existing tech debt, not introduced here). T15 [9c48d96](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/9c48d96) `getDiscoverableHivesAction` in `lib/actions/hive.actions.ts` (NOT `discover.actions.ts`) extended with Strategy B (pre-fetch friend ids via single `friendships` query, hives query gets OR clause `eq(visibility, 'PUBLIC') OR (eq(visibility, 'FRIENDS') AND inArray(ownerId, friendIds))`); when friendIds is empty the FRIENDS branch is omitted via `sql\`false\`` so PUBLIC-only is the fallback. Action is `requireAuth`-gated so anonymous viewers never reach it — "anonymous sees only PUBLIC" spec line is moot. **More patterns now load-bearing from Wave 4:** (1) New social-feed actions (Reading Lists, Book Clubs, future) MUST extend the source-action hook pattern from T8 — write the activity row inside the source tx, gate at write time per privacy rule, never write FRIENDS-tier content into the feed because feed redistributes to follows. (2) Status-transition-gated hooks need the priorStatus comparison INSIDE the same tx — fetch prior row via `tx.query.<table>.findFirst` before the update, capture the status field, then compare after update. T8's `updateChapterStatusAction` is the canonical example. (3) When the plan and reality diverge on action names (e.g. saveChapterAction vs updateChapterStatusAction), trust reality — the implementer's spec correction is the right move. (4) Spark winner-resolution event actor is the WINNER, not the spark creator (the actor of a "@x won the Spark" event in @x's followers' feeds is @x). (5) `recordSocialActivityTx` callers in pluggable source actions can use `tx.query.socialActivity.findFirst` for the dedupe check since the same tx is passed through. (6) Bell-list copy map adapter pattern: when the `NotificationRow.actor` shape lacks a field your click-through wants (e.g. username), route to a less-precise but-still-functional target (e.g. /friends instead of /u/{username}) rather than widening the actor type across the surface. Widening is a follow-up.

Migrated legacy `lib/friendships/are-friends.ts` consumer to new `@/lib/social/are-friends` (legacy file left in place with zero consumers — cleanup deferred). T5 [29aa292](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/29aa292) `friendships.actions.ts` AUGMENTED (not rewritten): `sendFriendRequestAction({ recipientId })` adds `isBlocked` gate + wraps insert path AND auto-accept path in `db.transaction` with paired `FRIEND_REQUEST` notification to recipient (insert path) or `FRIEND_ACCEPTED` to original requester (auto-accept path); `acceptFriendRequestAction` wraps status flip + `FRIEND_ACCEPTED` notification in tx; reject/cancel/unfriend stay silent per spec. NEW actions: `getFriendCountAction(targetUserId)` public-readable count via `sql<number>` count; `searchUsersAction({ query, limit? })` auth-required ILIKE on username + displayName with 3× overscan + self-filter + bidirectional-block filter via `userBlocks` IN-list. Existing action names KEPT (`listFriendsAction`, `listPendingFriendRequestsAction`) — plan's "getFriendsAction" / "getPendingRequestsAction" was wrong about existing reality. Extended `lib/actions/__tests__/friendships.test.ts` mocks (added `db.transaction`, `userBlocks.findFirst`, `.returning()`) to keep 5 prior behavior tests green under the new tx wrapping; assertions untouched. T6 [40625b9](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/40625b9) 3 new action files: `blocks.actions.ts` (`blockUserAction` tx wipes friendships + follows in BOTH directions via `onConflictDoNothing` for idempotency; `unblockUserAction` does NOT restore — must re-request; `getBlockedUsersAction`), `mutes.actions.ts` (mute/unmute/getMuted), `friend-invites.actions.ts` (`createFriendInviteAction` generates `randomBytes(24).toString('base64url')` = 32-char tokens + 14d TTL; `claimFriendInviteAction` error ladder TOKEN_NOT_FOUND → TOKEN_ALREADY_CLAIMED → TOKEN_EXPIRED → SELF_INVITE → BLOCKED → existing-friendship short-circuit). New `lib/validations/social.ts` with `userTargetSchema` + `claimInviteSchema`. **Pattern caught + documented**: notifications table has NO `payload` column — plan's `payload: { requesterId }` was wrong for this schema; canonical write pattern is `{ userId, type, actorId, resourceType, resourceId }` (mirrors `social.actions.ts:32-38` precedent). T5 + T6 both use this corrected shape. **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase from this wave**: (1) `areFriends` + `isBlocked` are the ONLY canonical privacy helpers — never write parallel implementations. Both `cache()`-wrapped for per-request memoization. (2) `recordSocialActivityTx(tx, opts)` is the canonical feed-event writer; callers MUST pass their source-action's tx so the event commits atomically. (3) Notification writes use `actorId + resourceType + resourceId` shape — NEVER add a `payload` field (column doesn't exist). (4) Block tx in `blockUserAction` cascades to delete friendships + follows in both directions; future "social tie" tables (e.g. C3 reading-list-follows, C4 club memberships) must extend this cascade if cross-relationship blocking is needed. (5) Subagent-driven parallel waves work cleanly when file scopes don't overlap — git index lock serializes commits.

> **T1 SHIPPED ([8b22053](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/8b22053)):** schema migration + idempotent runner. 438/438 tests green, tsc clean, migration ran twice cleanly proving idempotency. Subagent-driven cadence executed: implementer → spec review (verdict: spec-compliant, one non-blocking flag) → code-quality review (verdict: no blockers, ship it). Implementation: appended to `db/schema/social.ts` — `socialActivityTypeEnum` (9 values: book_published, chapter_posted, book_liked, book_commented, spark_entry_submitted, spark_won_community, spark_won_creator_choice, hive_created, hive_joined), 4 new tables (`socialActivity` with `(actorId, createdAt DESC)` + `(subjectType, subjectId)` indexes, `userBlocks` composite PK + reverse `blockedId` index, `userMutes` composite PK, `friendInvites` with token PK + inviter index + 14-day TTL semantics), and 2 new values appended to `notificationTypeEnum` (`FRIEND_REQUEST`, `FRIEND_ACCEPTED`). New `scripts/migrate-c1.ts` (7-step idempotent runner mirroring migrate-h4.ts shape). Schema-shape unit test from plan Step 1 skipped (no precedent in codebase; drizzle's tsc catches missing exports at consumer sites). **Known non-blocking flags from reviews (worth addressing or accepting):** (a) **schema/migration `.desc()` drift** — drizzle declaration is `.on(t.actorId, t.createdAt)` without `.desc()`; live DB has DESC ordering correctly via migration raw SQL; matters only if someone regenerates SQL from drizzle schema. (b) Neon HTTP driver auto-commits per-statement so `ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE` works in T1 — but if a future task batches statements via `sql.transaction([...])`, an enum ADD inside will fail. (c) `friend_invites.token` has no length/format constraint — T6's `createFriendInviteAction` must enforce entropy at app layer (24-byte base64url = 32 chars, plan already specifies). (d) `user_mutes` lacks a `muted_idx` mirror of `user_blocks_blocked_idx` — T7's feed query may need it; add if `EXPLAIN` shows seq scan. (e) `social_activity.subjectType` is free-text not enum (intentional extensibility; `SubjectType` TS union in `lib/social/types.ts` prevents typo drift across T8 writers). **Code-quality reviewer called out as GOOD patterns:** typed `jsonb('payload').$type<Record<string, unknown>>()`, composite-PK + reverse-direction index on `user_blocks` for bidirectional lookups, `friend_invites.claimedBy` ON DELETE SET NULL preserves audit trail.

> **C2 Sparks refresh spec landed ([f407d47](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/f407d47)):** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-c2-sparks-refresh-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-04-c2-sparks-refresh-design.md). C1 smoke PASSED end-to-end (all 23 scenarios + 3 Wave-4 UX targets green); kicked off the C2 brainstorm. Locked decisions: (Q1) optional entry titles with first-line-of-content fallback via shared `deriveTitle` helper (Buzz precedent); (Q2) one-level threaded comments via `parent_id` column on `spark_entry_comments` + `REPLY_DEPTH_EXCEEDED` predicate (Hive Discussions H3 precedent); (Q3) PUBLIC/FRIENDS/PRIVATE three-tier visibility + `discoverable` boolean (book + hive precedent + forward-compat for C4 clubs); (Q4) stored `spark_status` enum (`OPEN | VOTING | CLOSED`) + explicit `voting_ends_at` timestamp + lazy sweep on list/read (`sweepSparkStatuses` helper mirrors H4 word-goal auto-archive); (Q5) entries inherit parent spark visibility (no per-entry opt-down — simpler model); (Q6) canonical routes move to `/[locale]/(public)/sparks/*` with 308 `permanentRedirect` shims from `/discover/spark/*` (SP-A precedent). Additional locked items: entry `like_count` denorm bumped atomically in `voteSparkEntryAction` tx (H4 buzz_posts precedent); C1's 3 spark activity hooks (`spark_entry_submitted`, `spark_won_community`, `spark_won_creator_choice`) gate-tightened to `if (spark.visibility === 'PUBLIC')` so FRIENDS/PRIVATE sparks don't leak into the feed. **No new `social_activity_type` enum values needed** — C1 already covers the 3 spark feed events. **Data model**: 4 columns added to `sparks` (visibility/discoverable/status/voting_ends_at), 2 to `spark_entries` (title/like_count), 1 to `spark_entry_comments` (parent_id self-FK CASCADE); 2 new pgEnums (`spark_visibility`, `spark_status`). Idempotent `scripts/migrate-c2.ts` runner mirrors `migrate-h4.ts` shape — 7 steps including backfills for `like_count` (COUNT from existing votes) + `voting_ends_at` (deadline + 48h for existing rows with non-null deadline). **New helpers** (`lib/sparks/`): `canViewSpark` / `canEnterSpark` / `canVoteSpark` predicates in `predicates.ts` (mirrors `lib/hive/permissions.ts` layout), `deriveTitle` pure helper, `sweepSparkStatuses` lazy-sweep function. **Server actions modified**: `createSparkAction` (3 new Zod fields + computed `votingEndsAt`), `getSparksAction`/`getSparkAction` (sweep + `canViewSpark` + block masquerade), `submitSparkEntryAction` (title + `canEnterSpark` + tightened hook gate), `updateSparkEntryAction` (title), `voteSparkEntryAction` (tx wraps vote + `like_count` ±1 atomically + `canVoteSpark`), `getSparkEntriesAction` (return rows include title + likeCount, sort by `likeCount DESC` when VOTING/CLOSED), `getSparkEntryAction` (return title). **New action**: `replyToSparkCommentAction({ entryId, parentId, content })` with one-level enforcement + block check + `canViewSpark` on parent's spark. **UI changes**: 3 new pages at `/sparks/*` (index with Active/Voting/Past sections + detail + entry reader — server logic ported from existing `/discover/spark/*` with status/visibility pills + countdown to `voting_ends_at` added); `<CreateSparkModal>` gains 3-card visibility picker (`<VisibilityPicker>` reuses `<SharingControls>` shape) + discoverable checkbox with 3-layer defense + voting-window segmented control (24h/48h/72h/1 week, default 48); `<SparkSubmitPanel>` gains optional title input; `<SparkEntryCard>` displays `deriveTitle(...)` as Comfortaa headline + visibility pill; `<SparkEntryComments>` rewritten as 2-level threaded with Reply button on top-level only + indented `ml-8 border-l` reply block; spark detail header gains alpha-tinted status pill (gold OPEN / brand-yellow VOTING / muted CLOSED via `<HivePill>`-shape) + visibility pill + countdown when VOTING; `<SparkCard>` gains visibility + status pills; `/community` section rail Sparks tile flips href from `/discover?tab=sparks` to `/sparks`. **Privacy + block enforcement** wired through every surface (index/detail/reader/submit/vote/comment/reply) — NOT_FOUND masquerade everywhere. **Out of scope (deferred)**: per-entry visibility (Q5 — never unless asked), per-comment likes (C5), @-mentions (C5), `/sparks` search (C5), visual chrome refresh (future Claude Design handoff). **16-task implementation phasing** outlined in spec §8. Test posture: unit tests for `deriveTitle`/predicates/sweep + surface-shape tests for new + modified actions + manual smoke per AGENTS.md convention.
>
> **C2 plan landed ([20822df](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/20822df)):** 16-task plan at [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-04-c2-sparks-refresh.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-04-c2-sparks-refresh.md). T1 schema migration (4 columns on sparks + 2 on spark_entries + 1 on spark_entry_comments + 2 new pgEnums) with `scripts/migrate-c2.ts` idempotent runner including like_count and voting_ends_at backfills. T4 `getSparksAction` + `getSparkAction` (sweep + canViewSpark + block masquerade). T5 `submitSparkEntryAction` + `updateSparkEntryAction` (optional title + canEnterSpark gate + hook gate `if spark.visibility === 'PUBLIC'`). T6 `voteSparkEntryAction` (tx wraps vote + like_count ±1 atomically + canVoteSpark) + `getSparkEntriesAction` (sort by likeCount when VOTING/CLOSED). T7 new `replyToSparkCommentAction` with one-level enforcement via parent.parentId check + REPLY_DEPTH_EXCEEDED. T8 spark winner hooks (setCreatorChoiceAction + lazy-finalize in getSparkAction) gate-tightened to `if spark.visibility === 'PUBLIC'`. T9 three new pages at `/sparks/*` (index with Active/Voting/Past sections + detail + entry reader — server logic ported from /discover/spark/* with status/visibility pills + countdown added). T10 308 redirect shims + internal href audit (6 known consumers). T11 `<CreateSparkModal>` form additions (new `<VisibilityPicker>` reuses SharingControls shape + discoverable checkbox with useEffect force-clear + 24/48/72/168h segmented control). T12 `<SparkSubmitPanel>` optional title input + `<SparkEntryCard>` displays `deriveTitle(...)`. T13 `<SparkEntryComments>` threaded rewrite (Map-grouped replies + Reply button on top-level only + indented `ml-8 border-l` reply block). T14 three new components (`<StatusPill>` alpha-tinted via `oklch(from var(--token) l c h / 0.14)` + `<VisibilityPill>` with Globe/Users/Lock + `<Countdown>` 60s-interval client component). T15 `<SparkCard>` pills + `/community` section rail Sparks tile href flips to `/sparks`. T16 17-scenario smoke + AGENTS.md + ship. Self-review confirmed full spec-section coverage (every §2-§5 requirement maps to a task), no placeholders, type consistency (`SparkVisibility`/`SparkStatus` flow from schema → predicates → form state → pills; `deriveTitle` signature stable; `SparkLike` shape matches all callers). Suggested 6-wave execution: W1=T1 alone, W2=T2 alone, W3=T3+T4+T5+T6+T7+T8 parallel (6-way), W4=T9 alone, W5=T10+T11+T12+T13+T14+T15 parallel (6-way), W6=T16.

> **C2 T1 SHIPPED ([068e231](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/068e231)):** schema migration + idempotent runner. Combined review verdict: ✅ ship it, no blockers. 475/475 tests, tsc clean, migration ran twice cleanly proving idempotency. Implementation: appended to `db/schema/social.ts` — `sparkVisibilityEnum` (3 values: PUBLIC/FRIENDS/PRIVATE), `sparkStatusEnum` (3 values: OPEN/VOTING/CLOSED), 4 new columns on `sparks` (visibility/discoverable/status/votingEndsAt), 2 new columns on `spark_entries` (title/likeCount), 1 new self-FK column on `spark_entry_comments` (parentId with ON DELETE CASCADE matching spec). `scripts/migrate-c2.ts` is 7-step idempotent runner mirroring `scripts/migrate-c1.ts` + `scripts/migrate-h4.ts` shape: (1) enum creation via `DO $$ EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object`, (2-4) `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, (5) `like_count` backfill from `spark_votes` count guarded by `WHERE like_count = 0`, (6) `voting_ends_at` backfill = `deadline + 48h` guarded by `WHERE voting_ends_at IS NULL AND deadline IS NOT NULL`, (7) verification row counts. TS types exported as `SparkVisibility` / `SparkStatus` via `enumValues[number]` indexed access.

> **C2 Wave 3 SHIPPED ([7bb6e3e](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/7bb6e3e) + privacy-leak fix [afab389](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/afab389)):** T3-T8 server actions landed in ONE combined commit (Chris's pick B — single subagent doing all 6 modifications to `lib/actions/sparks.actions.ts` instead of 6 parallel implementers racing on the same file). 502/502 tests, tsc clean. **T3** `createSparkAction` inline Zod gains `visibility`/`discoverable`/`votingDurationHours` with `.transform()` 3-layer defense; computes `votingEndsAt = deadline + duration*3600s`. **T4** `getSparksAction` + `getSparkAction` both call `sweepSparkStatuses()` at top + use stored `status` column (legacy `computeStatus(deadline)` kept as fallback but unused on read path); per-row `canViewSpark` parallel `Promise.all` filter in list; NOT_FOUND masquerade in detail. `SparkSummary` widened with `visibility` + `votingEndsAt`. **T5** `submitSparkEntryAction` + `updateSparkEntryAction` accept optional `title` (Zod max 120, trim, nullable); `canEnterSpark` gate replaces explicit OPEN check; `recordSocialActivityTx` for `spark_entry_submitted` wrapped `if (spark.visibility === 'PUBLIC')`. **T6** `voteSparkEntryAction` gets `canVoteSpark` gate front-and-center; vote toggle + `likeCount ±1` wrapped in `db.transaction` with `sql\`${col} ± 1\``. `getSparkEntriesAction` sort branches on `parent.status`: OPEN chronological; VOTING/CLOSED by `likeCount desc, createdAt desc` — caller passes `sort='top'` (UI must opt-in on VOTING/CLOSED views — flag for T15). **T7** NEW `replyToSparkCommentAction` with error ladder PARENT_NOT_FOUND → REPLY_DEPTH_EXCEEDED → PARENT_MISMATCH → BLOCKED → NOT_ALLOWED (matches H3 discussion-pattern: depth check before block check so unauthenticated-by-block can't differentiate reply-to-reply from missing parent). `getSparkEntryCommentsAction` projection widened with `parentId` + `authorUserId`; `EntryComment` type matches. **T8** both winner hooks (`spark_won_creator_choice` via `setCreatorChoiceAction` + `spark_won_community` via `getSparkAction` lazy-finalize) wrapped in PUBLIC visibility gate. SPARK_WIN personal notification NOT gated (winner deserves to know even on FRIENDS/PRIVATE sparks — only feed events are gated). Local `SparkStatus` type at line 31 removed and re-imported from `@/db/schema/social` + re-exported via `export type { ... }` so existing external consumers don't break. **Privacy leak caught in review + fixed [afab389]:** `getProfileSparksAction` in `lib/actions/user-profile.actions.ts` got the `SparkSummary` schema widening but no `canViewSpark` gate — would have leaked FRIENDS/PRIVATE sparks on profile pages to anonymous/non-friend viewers. Fix mirrors T4 pattern: `getOptionalUserId` + parallel per-row `canViewSpark` filter + projection extends with `creatorId` + `status` so the predicate has the fields it needs. **Known non-blocking flags from review (deferred):** (1) T4 pagination off-by-canViewSpark — `limit(PAGE_SIZE + 1)` fetches up to 21 rows; if N rows get masqueraded, `hasMore` falsely reports no more pages; over-fetch a bigger window if it bites in production. (2) T7 `PARENT_MISMATCH` precedes `BLOCKED` — blocked user who guesses a parentId still learns it exists; tighten ordering for stricter info-hiding. (3) T6 sort default — UI in T15 must pass `sort='top'` on VOTING/CLOSED views or chronological ordering shows even on closed sparks. **Patterns now load-bearing for the rest of C2:** (1) Privacy gates on read actions follow the parallel `canViewSpark` per-row + post-fetch filter pattern (T4 canon, replicated in profile fix); always extend projection with `creatorId` + `visibility` + `status` so the predicate has what it needs. (2) Activity hook gates on FRIENDS/PRIVATE sparks ALWAYS wrap `recordSocialActivityTx` in `if (spark.visibility === 'PUBLIC')` — never leak feed events for non-public content. (3) Personal notifications (`SPARK_WIN`) stay un-gated — recipient is the data subject, they should know regardless of public-feed visibility. (4) `votes + likeCount` atomic tx pattern with `sql\`${col} +/- 1\`` mirrors H4 buzz_likes precedent — any future denorm counter follows the same shape.
>

Mock pattern uses top-level `vi.mock` + static imports per [ca51b28] lesson. Net new tests +27 (475 → 502 baseline). tsc clean.

>
> **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase (from C2 end-to-end):**
> 1. **Sweep-on-read for status machines.** `sweepSparkStatuses()` called at top of `getSparksAction` + `getSparkAction` does two UPDATEs (OPEN→VOTING when deadline past; VOTING→CLOSED when voting_ends_at past). No cron needed. Mirrors H4 word-goal precedent. Reusable for any future state machine where transitions are time-bounded.
> 2. **Privacy gates as composable predicates.** `canViewSpark` / `canEnterSpark` / `canVoteSpark` built on C1's `areFriends` + `isBlocked`. Pattern: each predicate takes `viewerId | null` + a `SparkLike` shape `{ creatorId, visibility, status? }`. Filter rows via `Promise.all(rows.map(r => canViewSpark(...) ? r : null))` post-fetch. Used by C2 list/detail/profile (T4 + T13's privacy fix mirror).
> 3. **`deriveTitle(explicit, content)` is the canonical optional-title-with-fallback helper.** Trims explicit title or falls back to first line of content truncated at 80 chars + `…`. Reusable for Reading Lists/Book Clubs entries in C3/C4.
> 4. **`<VisibilityPicker>` 3-card radio shape is reusable for C3/C4** (mirrors `<SharingControls>` for books). Component lives at `app/[locale]/(public)/discover/_components/visibility-picker.tsx`.
> 5. **Alpha-tinted pills via `oklch(from var(--token) l c h / 0.14)`** is the canonical status-badge pattern (T14's StatusPill). Token-based, theme-aware, no hardcoded colors. Reusable for any future status indicator.
> 6. **Countdown component** (`Countdown to={Date} prefix=""`) uses 60s `setInterval` + `useState` re-render — cheap, no animation frame churn. Reusable for any future deadline display.
> 7. **One-level threaded comments**: H3 hive_discussion_posts precedent + C2 sparks both use `parentId text REFERENCES self ON DELETE CASCADE` + `REPLY_DEPTH_EXCEEDED` server check (parent.parentId must be null) + UI gates Reply button to top-level only. Any future threaded-comment surface follows this triple-defense.
> 8. **Atomic counter denorms with `sql\`${col} ± 1\`` inside `db.transaction`** — vote insert/delete paired with `likeCount` update commits atomically. H4 buzz_likes precedent; C2 sparkVotes; any future denormalized counter follows.
> 9. **Status-transition activity hooks always check** `if (spark.visibility === 'PUBLIC')` before `recordSocialActivityTx` — feed redistributes to follows (not friends) so FRIENDS-tier content must NOT leak via the feed. Same posture as C1 books.
> 10. **Personal notifications stay un-gated** (SPARK_WIN to winner fires for FRIENDS/PRIVATE sparks — winner deserves to know even when feed events don't fire). Distinction: feed events are public broadcasts; notifications are direct messages to the data subject.
>
> **Known follow-ups (non-blocking, named so they don't get forgotten):**
> 1. **T4 pagination off-by-canViewSpark** — `getSparksAction` fetches `PAGE_SIZE + 1` then filters; if many rows get masqueraded, `hasMore` falsely reports no more pages. Over-fetch a bigger window if it bites in production.
> 2. **T7 `PARENT_MISMATCH` precedes `BLOCKED`** in `replyToSparkCommentAction` error ladder — blocked user who guesses a parentId still learns it exists; tighten ordering for stricter info-hiding.
> 3. **T6 sort default** — `getSparkEntriesAction` requires caller to pass `sort='top'` for likeCount ordering on VOTING/CLOSED views. T9 + T13's UI may need a sanity check that `sort='top'` is passed on those statuses.
> 4. **Legacy `STATUS_STYLES` map** in `<SparkCard>` had emoji badges — T15 removed them. If any other consumer still uses the old emoji shape, audit.
> 5. **"+ New Spark" CTA on /sparks header** — flagged in T9 review as missing. T11 modified the modal but didn't mount a trigger button on the /sparks index page. Small follow-up to add the trigger + import the modal at /sparks.
> 6. **T11+T12 commit fusion** — `git add -A` collision swept T12's work into T11's commit `b7a32ce`. Wizard-redesign fusion pattern from project history. Functional but history-collapsed. **Future avoidance**: brief subagents to use explicit `git add <file1> <file2>` for their own files instead of `-A`.
> 7. **`getProfileSparksAction` review-caught privacy leak** — fixed at [afab389] but only after Wave 3 ship. Pattern reminder: when widening a return type, audit all callers for privacy implications, not just type compatibility.
>
> **Build fix [43f5f5e](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/43f5f5e):** Chris hit a Next.js 16 Turbopack build error on `npm run dev` — `Export SparkStatus doesn't exist in target module` originating from the server-action bundler in `.next-internal/server/app/[locale]/(public)/sparks/page/actions.js`. Root cause: Wave 3 added `export type { SparkStatus, SparkVisibility }` at the top of `lib/actions/sparks.actions.ts` for backward-compat. Turbopack's `'use server'` bundler treats every export as a runtime-callable async fn and fails on type-only re-exports. Fix removed the re-export from the actions file; two consumers (`spark-entry-card.tsx`, `spark-vote-button.tsx`) updated to import `SparkStatus` directly from `@/db/schema/social`, matching the pattern already used by 4 other consumers (`create-spark-modal.tsx`, `status-pill.tsx`, `visibility-picker.tsx`, the actions file's own import). **Pattern note now load-bearing**: `'use server'` modules must export ONLY async functions. Type definitions live alongside the schema; consumers import from `@/db/schema/...` directly. If a future C-phase action file needs a type re-export for ergonomic consumer imports, move it into a sibling non-`'use server'` module (the same trick T3 used for `lib/tiptap-extensions/patch-mark.ts` to avoid the H3-era `'use server'` restriction).
>
> **Last commit:** [43f5f5e](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/43f5f5e) — fix(c2/sparks): drop type re-export from 'use server' module.
>

> **Prior next step (resolved):** Chris answered the **Wave 3 strategy checkpoint** — the plan said "T3+T4+T5+T6+T7+T8 in 6-way parallel" but ALL SIX tasks modify the same file `lib/actions/sparks.actions.ts`, so 6 parallel implementers would race on the git tree (last committer's tree wouldn't include others' work). Three real options: **(A) Sequential T3→T8** (safe, 6 round-trips); **(B) One combined "all server actions" subagent** doing all 6 modifications in one shot (faster than A, still safe — recommended); **(C) Worktree isolation per subagent + manual merge** (same-file merge conflicts still likely → not actually safer than A). Recommendation: B because the 6 tasks are mechanical (plan has exact code), all touch the same file, and one implementer keeping mental state across all 6 changes is more reliable than 6 separate implementers each loading the file fresh. > **Prior focus:** **Hive routes unified UI design spec landed ([45bb927](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/45bb927)) — awaiting Chris's review before plan writing.** Two-phase plan locked: (1) fix-pass to make the hive routes internally consistent, (2) produce a Claude Design handoff prompt for future visual recreation cycles. Brainstorm session (visual companion at .superpowers/brainstorm/45647-1780523115/) walked 6 load-bearing visual decisions and 4 textual decisions; spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-hive-routes-unified-ui-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-hive-routes-unified-ui-design.md). **Locked decisions:** (§1) **page anatomy** — back link above panel as lucide ArrowLeft 12px + mono uppercase tracking-wider "Back to {parent}" label; h1 Comfortaa bold 28px brand-yellow INSIDE panel header; subtitle plain Geist 13px muted under h1 (kills the Chapters/Outline/Discussions mono-subtitle drift); top-right slot holds ONE OF {primary brand-pill CTA, 2-button group for Submission review, status/role pill for Chapter view}; gated CTAs are omitted not disabled+tooltipped; dashboard "HIVE" eyebrow removed. (§2) **max-width 2 tiers** — `max-w-3xl` (768px) for 13 surfaces (Dashboard, Buzz, Word Goals, Chapters, Members, Settings, Submissions list+composer+review+read, Suggestions); `max-w-5xl` (1024px) for 5 (Outline index+detail, Discussions list+thread, Wiki shell, Hive chapter view). `p-6` outer padding everywhere (kills Suggestions `px-2 py-2` outlier). (§3) **list chrome** — universal panel wrapper, two shapes inside: **forum-table** (column-header strip on `--canvas-dark-100` bg with `--br-card` borders + mono uppercase 10px labels + `divide-y` `<ul>` rows + `hover:bg-[var(--canvas-dark-300)]` only — kills Chapters' `hover:translate-y-[-1px]` jitter) for pages with parallel meta columns (Outline index, Discussions list, Members, Submissions list, Chapters index, Suggestions per-chapter groups), **card-stack** (tile-gradient cards stacked with `gap-3` inside `px-6 pb-6`) for pages with self-contained posts (Buzz, Word Goals sections, Suggestions card pile). (§4) **NO sub-panels** — every page is exactly ONE panel; internal sections divided by `border-t border-[var(--br-card)]/50` + mono uppercase 10px tracking-wider muted label. Word Goals goes panel > 4 hairline sections (ACTIVE GOALS / CONTRIBUTORS / RECENT ACTIVITY / HISTORY); Settings goes panel > BASICS / VISIBILITY / DISCOVERABILITY / DANGER ZONE. Word Goals History stays inline (not collapsible accordion). (§5) **pills** — universal alpha-tint shape (`oklch(from <token> l c h / 0.14)` bg + accent ink + 1px border at 0.3 alpha + 999px radius + uppercase tracking-wider 11px); 6 token families (chapter status `--status-*` existing, submission status `--status-idea/warning/success/error` existing, discussion topic `TOPIC_META` existing, annotation layer `LAYER_META` existing, **role `--role-*` NEW** with warm gold owner / slate blue moderator / mint contributor / cool gray reader, **goal `--goal-*` NEW** with gold daily / mint weekly / lilac monthly / gray custom). Kills the two semantic-mismatch bugs where role piggybacked on `--status-*` and goal-type used chapter-status tokens. (§6) **specific fixes** — Chapters rows get constant height via fixed 180px activity badge slot with invisible placeholder when count=0; Buzz gains derived-title treatment (first line of body → Comfortaa bold 16px title, rest → `line-clamp-2` excerpt) matching Discussions; Suggestions outer padding `p-6`; `disabled={type !== 'TOTAL' && false}` always-false bug in new-goal-modal.tsx swept. **Implementation phasing:** 14-task subagent-driven decomposition (T1 token additions in globals.css → T2 shared `<HivePageShell>` → T3 `<HivePill>` → T4 `<HiveSectionDivider>` → T5-T12 per-page apply → T13 modal bug sweep → T14 smoke). **Out of scope:** new features, hive layout shell + sidebar (already correct per H1), light-mode chrome variant, studio editor surfaces (cream paper stays studio-only), mobile/tablet responsive. **Appendix A** is a Claude Design handoff prompt scaffold to be filled out AFTER the fix pass ships and the codebase is internally consistent — that's the deliverable for Phase 2 of the user's two-phase plan.

Word Goals 4 hairline sections + `--goal-*` token migration via `GOAL_TOKEN` map; GoalHistory rendered inline NOT accordion; CTAs as brand pills in headerSlot via small `<BuzzHeaderCTA>` / `<NewGoalHeaderCTA>` client wrappers that own modal state). · T8 [b618da2](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/b618da2) Submissions all 4 modes (list with 3 role-scoped hairline sections + forum-table rows with status pills via `<HivePill>` + `--status-*` tokens; composer auto-save → router.replace dance preserved verbatim; review has Approve/Reject 2-button group in headerSlot (Approve brand pill, Reject tile-gradient with `--status-error` tint); read mode has status pill in headerSlot). · T9 [e34974a](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/e34974a) Suggestions (outer `p-6` kills `px-2 py-2` outlier; per-chapter hairline sections with card-stack inside; custom Reject Dialog with reviewNote textarea preserved — NOT shared ConfirmDialog). · T10 [40fa5a4](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/40fa5a4) Chapters (index gets column-header strip + flat divide-y rows + invisible `—` placeholder in `<ChapterActivityBadges>` for constant row heights; chapter view role pill in headerSlot at wide width; metadata as inline hairline section). · T11 [082694d](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/082694d) Discussions (list h1 moves INSIDE panel — kills outlier; FILTER + Threads hairline sections; new `<NewDiscussionCTA>` client wrapper for headerSlot CTA). · T12 [d7180bf](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/d7180bf) Outline + Wiki (Outline index h1 moves INSIDE panel — kills second outlier; Wiki shell takes ownership of `HivePageShell` since `selectedEntryId` is client state; wiki entry-view back button stays inside editor as Option A — URL-only back prop doesn't fit client-state navigation). · T13 [2ba3fba](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/2ba3fba) new-goal-modal end-date disabled bug fixed — `disabled={type !== 'TOTAL' && false}` (always false) corrected to `disabled={type !== 'TOTAL'}`. Investigation revealed intent was clear from surrounding code: lines 83-86 auto-compute end-date from start for non-TOTAL types (DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY), so the end-date input SHOULD be disabled for those types to prevent manual edits that would break the contract. The `&& false` was a debug or temporary disable that got committed. The codebase's `WordGoalType` is `DAILY | WEEKLY | MONTHLY | TOTAL` (not `CUSTOM` as the spec assumed when drafting the plan — verified during T7 implementation).

> **Two deferred trade-offs from T10 (worth a future follow-up):** (a) Chapters index "Updated" column omitted because `getHiveChapterListAction` return shape doesn't include `updatedAt`; (b) inline chapter status pill in title row omitted because the same projection lacks `status`. Both would need extending the server action's projection — left for future polish pass rather than gold-plating this refactor.
>
> **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
> 1. **`<HivePageShell>` is the canonical hive-route page wrapper.** Every hive page (existing or future) consumes it. Children render directly under the panel header without their own panel chrome. Top-level pages omit `back`; detail pages set `back={{ href, label }}` for the mono breadcrumb above the panel.
> 2. **`<HivePill>` is the canonical alpha-tint categorical tag.** Takes a CSS custom property name as `token` prop and composes `oklch(from var(<token>) l c h / 0.14)` bg + accent ink + 0.3-alpha border. Server+client safe. Any new role/status/topic/goal/layer tag uses it.
> 3. **`<HiveSectionDivider>` is the canonical sub-panel replacement.** Hairline top border + mono uppercase label. `hideTopBorder` for first sections. `tone="danger"` for destructive groups. NEVER nest panel chrome inside a panel.
> 4. **Modal trigger CTAs that need client state get a tiny header-CTA wrapper.** Pattern: `<{Surface}HeaderCTA>` client component owns the modal state and renders the brand-pill button. Shipped instances: `<BuzzHeaderCTA>`, `<NewDiscussionCTA>`, and the inline equivalents in Word Goals + Submissions list + Wiki shell. Reusable for any future surface where the page-level headerSlot CTA needs to open a Dialog.
> 5. **Token semantic correctness matters.** Don't piggyback role pills on chapter-status tokens. Don't piggyback goal-type pills on chapter-status tokens. Don't reference token names that don't exist (CSS will silently fall back). The token-correctness audit (T1) caught a silent bug where every submission status pill was rendering brand-yellow because three `--status-*` tokens didn't exist.
> 6. **`oklch(from <color> l c h / <alpha>)` syntax** is the standard tint mechanism in this codebase — works in Tailwind v4 + modern browsers. Used by `<HivePill>`, `<HiveSectionDivider>` border, and many existing surfaces.
> 7. **Forum-table rows are flat (no per-row tile chrome).** Column-header strip with `bg: var(--canvas-dark-100)` + `border-y: var(--br-card)` + mono `text-[10px]` uppercase tracking-wider labels. Rows use `divide-y divide-[var(--canvas-dark-300)]/40` + `hover:bg-[var(--canvas-dark-300)]` (NO `translate-y` jitter).
> 8. **Card-stack rows are tile-gradient.** Used where each row is a self-contained post or card with no parallel meta columns (Buzz, Suggestions cards, Word Goals contributors/activity). Chrome: `linear-gradient(180deg, var(--canvas-dark-350), var(--canvas-dark-300))` + `var(--sh-tile)` + `var(--r-row)`.
>

Contains project context, all 33 design tokens verbatim from `globals.css`, universal page shell DOM, section divider + pill convention + forum-table/card-stack snippets, the 18-page specification table mirroring spec §6, and hard constraints (no pure black, brand-yellow restraint, cream-paper-is-studio-only, dark mode only, desktop only). Phase 2 of the two-phase plan delivered — the fix pass made the codebase internally consistent; this prompt now lets future visual cycles iterate purely on look-and-feel without drifting on layout or feature scope. Existing `<HivePageShell>` + `<HivePill>` + `<HiveSectionDivider>` React components already match the prompt's structure so a future mechanical import is a CSS-token + per-page apply pass with no architecture rework.

Spec catalogs: (a) 9 net-new tokens needed in `globals.css` (`--brand-hover/-active/-soft`, `--canvas-dark-ink-faint`, 5 `--layer-*` promoting inline collab-gutter values), (b) viewport-fill min-height rule needed on `<HivePageShell>` (outer `min-h-screen flex flex-col` + panel `flex-1`), (c) 5 structural deviations from the locked unified-UI spec that need Chris sign-off (Wiki drops 3-tab view switch + adds category drill-down route, Chapters index drops activity badges for "Last edit", Dashboard gains Recent activity feed, Submissions adopts 3-tier tonal hierarchy, OrphanSection dead-code cleanup), (d) per-page deltas for all 18 routes with explicit "do not regress" call-outs (auto-save dances, optimistic mutations, permission gates), (e) 4-phase implementation roadmap (A foundation / B 12 visual polish / C 5 structural / D 1 new feature). Cross-cutting constraint flagged: AppNav must stay; cream paper stays studio-editor-only; sidebar `<HiveSidebar>` already correct.

Standalone-hive fallback preserved.
>
> **Architectural decisions worth knowing:**
> 1. **`<HivePageShell>` is now viewport-fill** via outer `flex flex-col min-h-screen` + panel `flex-1` (Phase A2). Short-content pages fill the viewport; tall-content pages scroll naturally. All 18 hive pages inherit identically.
> 2. **`<HiveSubSectionHeader>` is the new sibling of `<HiveSectionDivider>`** (Phase C3). Use for surfaces that need chunky section bars with title + count caption + optional right slot (currently only Submissions list, but reusable).
> 3. **`<CollaborationGutter>` has a new `inline` prop** (Phase C2). `inline={true}` renders without panel chrome (320px right rail with left border + sticky top-16); without it renders the existing toggleable studio-editor variant. Hive chapter view uses inline; studio editor uses the toggle path. Both consume the same `useCollabData` hook.
> 4. **Wiki has 3 routes now** (Phase C4+C5): `/wiki` (category landing), `/wiki/category/[category]` (drill-down), `/wiki/entry/[entryId]` (editor). Entry editor's URL-based back goes to `/wiki` landing for simplicity (doesn't carry source category). Existing `WikiCategoryPicker` modal preserved for the landing's "+ New Entry" flow; drill-down skips it since category is already locked.
> 5. **Character UNION removed from wiki** per Q2=B. `getHiveWikiView` no longer surfaces `binder_items.type === 'character'` rows as wiki entries. Characters stay studio-binder-only. If a future feature needs them in the wiki, restore the UNION coercion.
> 6. **`createHiveOutlineAction` is the new server action** for outline creation (Phase B1). Mirrors `createBinderItemAction` pattern with `requireHiveMember` + `canEditOutline` gate. Inserts an `outline` binder item with empty beats. `<NewOutlineCTA>` client wrapper renders the brand-pill trigger gated on `canEditOutline(viewerRole)`.
> 7. **9 token additions in `globals.css`** (Phase A1): 5 `--layer-*` net-new (brand variants + ink-faint already existed pre-A1). The mockup's `--brand-soft` value `oklch(0.85 0.18 90 / 0.16)` differs from the pre-existing `0.18` by a small opacity tick — left untouched.
>

> **Smoke fix [ed1aefe](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ed1aefe):** wiki entry editor at `/hive/[hiveId]/wiki/entry/[entryId]` had duplicate header chrome — Chris smoke-caught the page rendering "New Character / Character / Back to wiki / Edited by @x · ● Saved" all stacked. The shell wrapper from C4+C5 was passing `title` + `subtitle` + `back` to `HivePageShell` AND the inner `HiveWikiEntryEditor` had its own header with a second "Back to wiki" button + edited line + save badge. Chris wanted ONLY the inner edited-by + saved cluster preserved. Fix: (1) `HivePageShell.title` made optional + entire `<header>` block conditionally rendered (skipped when no title, subtitle, or headerSlot — preserves panel chrome + viewport-fill); (2) `HiveWikiEntryEditorPage` drops `title`/`subtitle`/`back` props from its `HivePageShell` call; (3) `HiveWikiEntryEditor`'s inner header drops the "Back to wiki" button + unused `ChevronLeft` import, switches to `justify-end` so only the edited+saved cluster sits at the right; (4) entry-page server component drops now-unused `title` + `categoryLabel` + `CATEGORY_TEMPLATE_MAP` import. Browser back handles navigation. tsc clean. Resolves the "double back affordance" known-follow-up flagged at ship.

Now renders the mono breadcrumb ABOVE the panel (the canonical pattern from `HivePageShell`'s back prop). Title + subtitle stay omitted; the inner editor's "Back to wiki" button stays removed. Net result: above the panel, "← BACK TO WIKI" breadcrumb; top of panel content, just the edited-by + saved cluster.

Chris flagged it as visual noise — the forum-table's own column-header strip + top hairline are enough visual separation from the Filter section above. 6-line delete in `discussions-list.tsx`.

Three changes: (a) dropped the outer `px-6` wrapper from `OutlineIndex` so content can extend full-width; (b) pulled the Outlines section out of `<HiveSectionDivider>` — replaced with a manual `<section>` that owns its own hairline + label rendering, label stays inset at `px-6` but the column-header strip below is edge-to-edge; (c) removed `borderRadius: 'var(--r-row) var(--r-row) 0 0'` from the strip so it's rectangular with top+bottom hairlines. List rows bumped from `px-5` to `px-6` to align with the new strip edges.

Three changes: (a) title + subtitle dropped from `HivePageShell` call in `outline/[outlineId]/page.tsx`, replaced by a contenteditable h1 inside `HiveOutlineSurfaceInner`. New `commitTitle` handler calls `updateBinderItemAction(outline.id, { title })` with rollback on failure; Enter blurs to commit; BETA_READER sees non-editable cursor. (b) "Last edited by @x · 2h ago" subtitle renders below the editable title; `SaveStatusBadge` moves inline to top-right of the new header row. (c) `BeatLabelBadge` styling swapped from `oklch(from --outline-ink-strong l c h / 0.08)` faint alpha bg → solid `--canvas-dark-100` bg + `--canvas-dark-ink-strong` near-white text + `--sh-inset` recessed shadow. Applies app-wide (studio + hive); on studio cream paper the dark badge reads as a high-contrast tag chip.

> **Reader theme + accessibility font-size stepper shipped ([3ea8e70](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/3ea8e70)):** ported a Claude Design handoff bundle (`design_handoff_reader_theme/`) into the public chapter reader at `/[locale]/(public)/books/[bookId]/read/[chapterId]/`. End-to-end: top bar now carries a Kindle-style 3-tile theme toggle (**Dark / White / Cream**) + a tighter `[− Font Size] [+ Font Size]` stepper headed by a mono uppercase "ACCESSIBILITY" eyebrow + hairline separator + theme tiles. Both controls persist to `localStorage` (keys `beehive:reader-theme` + `beehive:reader-font-size`) so the choice rides across every chapter and book. Theme toggle has 3 options; font-size stepper has 5 discrete steps (`15px → 16.5px → 18px → 20px → 22px`, default Medium = 18px). Buttons disable + grey out at boundary states. **Architecture:** server `page.tsx` slimmed to data-fetch + gate (everything preserved verbatim from before — `canReadBook` access guard / `AccessDenied` + `notFound()` branches / drizzle book + allChapters + chapter queries / `isChapterReaderVisible` → `LockedChapterPlaceholder` / `markChapterReadAction(bookId, current.binderItemId)` side-effect / `tiptapToHtml(chapter.content)` + `.prose-chapter` + `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` / `showContributionByline` + `<ChapterContributionByline>` props); new client component `_components/reader-surface.tsx` owns the top bar (back-to-book + chapter context + accessibility cluster + theme cluster), the prose panel (carrying `data-reader-theme={theme}` on the `<article>`), and the nav row (Prev / Back / Next paper-stack cards with mid-hover lift + brand-tinted border via `onMouseEnter/Leave` style mutation). Two new hooks at `lib/hooks/use-reader-theme.ts` + `lib/hooks/use-reader-font-size.ts` — SSR-safe pattern (default value on first render, read `localStorage` in `useEffect` after mount, write on change). Font-size hook returns `{ size, setSize, increment, decrement, canIncrement, canDecrement }` + a `READER_FONT_SIZE_LABELS` map for tooltips/aria. `globals.css` gained three `[data-reader-theme="dark|white|cream"]` token blocks driving `--prose-bg / -bg-elevated / -ink / -ink-strong / -ink-muted / -rule / -accent`; `.prose-chapter` retokenized to consume those so the body flips with the wrapper. `.prose-chapter h1/h2/h3` converted from absolute px (28/24/18) to `em` (1.56 / 1.33 / 1) so headings scale with the body font-size (which is set via inline `style={{ fontSize: size }}` on the `.prose-chapter` div). `<hr>` glyph scaled to 0.9em. **Omitted per README** (no backing data layer): collection eyebrow + brand-yellow collection labels, author's note callout, comment composer + threads — those are separate features. **Page bg uses literal `#232425`** (one tick darker than `--canvas-dark-100 = #262728`) per Chris's smoke; AGENTS.md's "do not modify `--canvas-dark-*`" rule means the override is page-scoped (outer reader wrapper + sticky topbar background + `LockedChapterPlaceholder` outer bg). **Patterns now load-bearing:** (1) `useReaderTheme` + `useReaderFontSize` share the same SSR-safe shape (default value first render → `useEffect` localStorage read → write on change). Any future "reader preference" hook should follow this pattern; (2) `ChapterContributionByline` mounts OUTSIDE the prose panel (on dark chrome between top-bar and panel) — its muted-foreground text would land unreadable on white/cream prose if mounted inside the theme-flipping panel; (3) `data-reader-theme` lives on the prose `<article>` wrapper, NEVER on `<html>` or `<body>` — app chrome stays dark, only the prose surface flips; (4) hover effects on paper-stack nav cards use `onMouseEnter`/`Leave` inline-style mutation (Tailwind can't cleanly combine `translateY` + box-shadow on hover when shadows are CSS-var-derived — same load-bearing pattern noted in the Reader Page Claude Design port section).
>
> **Studio Continue Writing hero tightened ([d5c443f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/d5c443f)):** four targeted trims on `_components/continue-writing-hero.tsx` per Chris's smoke. (1) Status badge ("Drafting" / "Revised" / "Published" pill) removed from the meta strip — now reads `● Continue writing · Last edited Xh ago`. The pill duplicated info already on the book card below. `statusToken()` helper deleted along with its only consumers. (2) Confusing `<em>Chapter N</em>{genre ?? '—'}` subtitle (rendered as "Chapter 1 —" for a fresh book with one chapter and no genre) replaced by a new `bookSubtitle()` helper rendering `1 chapter` / `12 chapters · Fantasy` with proper pluralization and no orphan em-dashes. (3) Entire fake word-goal block removed — `WORD_GOAL = 80_000` const, `pct` math, words/percent readout, progress track + fill. No system exists for per-book word goals yet, so the hardcoded `80,000` was surfacing fiction. If real goals get added later, the block can come back driven by actual data. (4) Secondary `+ New book` button moved out of the hero's right action column and integrated into `_components/library-grid.tsx`'s controls bar as the rightmost element (after the Sort dropdown). Hero right column now holds only the brand-pill `Open editor →` CTA aligned to the bottom-right of the panel. Empty-state branch (no books but hives exist) deliberately untouched — when there's no library to integrate the button into, the hero IS the only path. 438/438 tests, tsc clean.
>

> **Prior focus:** **Hive collab UX overhaul shipped + Chris confirmed pass.** Single commit [302468f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/302468f) covers seven coordinated changes resolving every issue Chris raised over the session. (1) **Orphaned tab + section deleted entirely.** Every annotation/suggestion stays in the main Collaboration list until manually approved/rejected — the prior orphan detection was misclassifying freshly-created rows under multi-paragraph edit races and confusing users. `OrphanSection` mount + `orphanAnnotationIds`/`orphanSuggestionIds` consumers removed from the gutter; server still returns `orphanRowIds` for backward compat but nothing reads them. (2) **X reject button alongside the approve check** on every unresolved annotation card. Both buttons currently call `mutate.resolveAnnotation` (no schema disposition field — UX-only distinction; add `outcome` column on `hive_annotations` if real differentiation needed later). Per-button busy state (`approving` / `rejecting`) so the spinner sits on whichever button was actually clicked, with a combined `busy = approving || rejecting` disabling both. (3) **Flow-layout spacing.** Ripped out the absolute-position prose-anchoring system (no more `useLayoutEffect` + `coordsAtPos` + `positions` state + `canAnchor` + `resolvedTops` collision avoidance). Single `flex flex-col p-2` with `gap: 10px` — cards stack consistently regardless of how sparsely the annotations sit in the prose. (4) **Resolved is now a view-mode toggle**, not an additive flag. Predicate flipped to `if (filter.showResolved ? !a.resolved : a.resolved) return false` — Resolved ON shows ONLY resolved, OFF shows ONLY unresolved. The All pill (layer filter) stays orthogonal. (5) **Highlight stripping on resolve/reject.** New pure helper `lib/tiptap-extensions/strip-mark.ts` walks PM JSON and removes every instance of `markName` whose `attrs[attrKey] === id` while preserving sibling marks (bold/italic). Server-side persistence: `resolveAnnotationAction` (only on transitioning to `resolved=true`) and `rejectSuggestionAction` (in-tx alongside the suggestion update) strip the matching mark from `chapters.content` so every reader surface stops rendering it on next load. Client-side instant visual: `collaboration-gutter.tsx` wraps `mutate` into `wrappedMutate` — on resolve/reject success, dispatches a single ProseMirror transaction (`stripMarkInstancesById`) to remove the live mark, then `flushPendingSave` so the editor's stripped content lands in DB before the 2s autosave debounce. `unresolveAnnotationAction` deliberately leaves content untouched — restoring the mark would require knowing the original range, and unresolve is rare. Accept-suggestion needs no strip (`applySuggestionToDoc` already replaces the marked text run). (6) **Gutter closed by default on chapter open.** `maybeAutoOpenGutter` neutered to a no-op — toolbar badge is the surface for "you have unresolved items"; user opens it when ready. (7) **Toolbar badge stays accurate.** Provider gained `liveCollabCounts: { annotations, suggestions } | null` state seeded from chapter data on every load path (cache hit + cache miss + `reloadActiveChapter`). The gutter pushes live counts up via `onCountsChange` while it's open AND triggers a chapter reload via `onMutated` after every mutation, so when the user closes and reopens the gutter the seeded counts match reality. Toolbar reads `liveCollabCounts ?? fallback`, where fallback is the server-fetched chapter counts (covers the brief chapter-switch window). **Root-cause server fix:** `getChapterAction` (chapter.actions.ts) AND `getHiveChapterListAction` (hive-content.actions.ts) were both counting top-level annotations WITHOUT a resolved filter — the suggestion queries had `eq(hiveSuggestions.resolved, false)` but the annotation queries didn't. Both fixed with `eq(hiveAnnotations.resolved, false)`. This was the root cause of badge bouncing back to 1 after refresh / chapter switch even though the gutter showed "No annotations or suggestions yet." 438/438 tests, tsc clean.
>
> **Sub-thread diagnosis along the way (resolved):** A long detour where Chris reported the selection popover wasn't appearing turned out to be a misunderstanding rather than a code regression — the SelectionPopover gates on `bookHive` truthy, which means the book must have a `hives` row pointing to its `bookId`. The book Chris was testing on (`skfa5bcvm553uci3aeflqp0b`) wasn't linked to a hive at the moment of testing. **Annotations + suggestions are hive-only features by design** — `bookHive` gates BOTH the selection popover AND the collaboration gutter. Non-hive books deliberately don't expose collab UI. To work on collab features, the book must be linked to a hive via the binder footer's "+ Create Hive" → link-existing-book path. AGENTS.md note: any future "popover/gutter not appearing" report should check `bookHive` truthy first.
>
> **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
> 1. **Server-side row count queries for collab badges must include the `resolved = false` filter on BOTH annotations and suggestions.** Annotation rows persist across resolve (we only flip a flag, never delete), so counting top-level + parentId-null is NOT sufficient — must also filter out resolved. Same shape on both `getChapterAction` and `getHiveChapterListAction`. Future per-chapter count queries should follow this 3-clause pattern.
> 2. **Highlight stripping is a two-layer concern.** Server-side strip via `stripMarkById` in the action's tx is the authoritative path (every reader surface picks it up on next fetch). Client-side strip via `stripMarkInstancesById` in the gutter is instant-visual sugar only; without it the user has to wait for a fetch to see the mark disappear. Both layers should ship together for any new "resolve/reject removes a mark" flow.
> 3. **`'use server'` action modules cannot export sync helpers** — pure JSON walkers must live in a sibling non-'use server' module (`lib/tiptap-extensions/` is the convention). `stripMarkById` follows the pattern set by `patchDocWithMark`, `applySuggestionToDoc`, and `findMarkRanges`.
> 4. **Optimistic counts that the user can mutate need both a live source AND a re-seed on refresh.** Pattern: provider state seeded from server data on every chapter load → gutter pushes live updates while mounted → `onMutated` triggers a chapter reload so the cached server data matches the new reality on next gutter open. Without both legs, the badge bounces back to stale values on refresh/switch.
> 5. **`bookHive` truthy is the universal gate for hive collab UI.** Any new collab-adjacent feature (popover, gutter, mark commands, status indicators) must gate on it. Non-hive books are first-class but lack collab affordances by design.
