> **Current focus:** **Chapter-wipe bug fixed + hive UX polish round 1 shipped — awaiting Chris's smoke.** Chris hit a load-bearing data-loss bug: chapters with real content would intermittently get wiped to 0 words after binder add/delete actions; version history showed a wave of 0-word snapshots correlated with chapter navigation. Root cause was a TipTap footgun in `app/[locale]/(app)/studio/[bookId]/_components/editor/chapter-editor.tsx`: three call sites use `editor.setEditable(...)` without a second arg, and TipTap's `setEditable(editable, emitUpdate=true)` default fires the editor's `update` event — which trips our `onUpdate` handler — which captures `editor.getJSON()` into `pendingSaveRef` and arms the 2s debounced `saveChapterAction`. The kill scenario on EVERY cache-miss chapter switch: (1) `setActiveItemId(C)` → cache miss → `getChapterAction(C)` fires async, (2) render with `activeChapter=null` → `useEditor` recreates with `content: null`, editor mounts empty, (3) overflow `useEffect` (deps `[bookOverflow, editor, previewSnapshotId]`) sees editor ref changed → fires `setEditable(!bookOverflow)` → TipTap emits `update` → onUpdate fires with `editor.getJSON()` = empty doc → `pendingSaveRef={C, empty}`, 2s timer set, (4) ~300ms later `getChapterAction(C)` resolves with C's real content → `setChapterCache(C, real)` → setContent useEffect loads real content into editor via `setContent(..., {emitUpdate:false})` (correctly suppresses onUpdate so pendingSaveRef is NOT corrected), (5) editor visually shows correct content, user feels fine, save status reads "saved", (6) 2s timer fires → `performSave(C, empty)` → cache says C exists → `saveChapterAction` succeeds → **DB now has 0 words, 0-word snapshot recorded**, (7) editor visually still shows real content because `editor.isEmpty` is now false so the load effect won't overwrite, but on refresh the empty DB content loads → "the text disappeared on its own." The "after binder add/delete" correlation is because those flows call `setActiveItemId(newId)` → useEditor recreates → editor ref changes → overflow effect re-runs → spurious save. Fix [23ebb3a](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/23ebb3a) passes `emitUpdate: false` (TipTap's second positional arg on `Editor.setEditable(editable, emitUpdate=true)`) to all three call sites: line 251 preview enter, line 261 preview exit, line 281 overflow effect. The setEditable still flips read-only state; it just no longer fires the save handler. tsc clean. **Load-bearing pattern caught:** ANY future studio surface that calls `editor.setEditable(...)` in a useEffect that runs on editor recreation MUST pass `false` as the second arg, otherwise the same wipe race re-emerges. Other studio surfaces (notes, wiki, character, outline) currently use the `editable` constructor option only and aren't exposed; chapter-editor was the sole offender. Also — when a useEffect's deps include the TipTap `editor` ref, remember the editor ref FLIPS on every recreation (useEditor deps `[activeItemId, chapterContentVersion]` change), so the effect runs once per chapter switch; any side-effect inside that effect must be safe to run repeatedly during the brief empty-editor window between recreation and setContent. **Prior hive UX polish (still awaiting smoke):** 4 issues touched: (1) [b925bb0](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/b925bb0) `getHiveOutlineView` switched from `findFirst` to `findMany` + reshaped to return `outlines: HiveOutlineEntry[]` (new exported type) with per-outline `lastEditedByUsername`/`lastEditedAt` resolved via single `userProfiles` IN-list lookup; `HiveOutlineSurface` now maps over outlines rendering each as its own `HiveOutlineSurfaceInner` card stacked with `gap-6`. Standalone empty-state copy switched plural ("No outlines yet"). (2) [9e557ab](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/9e557ab) Wiki `By Category` view rewritten as 3-level drill-down: Level 1 = grid of all 14 category tiles (icon chip + label + blurb + entry count badge in recessed pill); Level 2 = chosen category with header banner (icon + label + blurb + entry count) + `← All categories` back button + Add CTA + entry grid; Level 3 = existing `HiveWikiEntryEditor` (unchanged). New `isSearching` prop on `ByCategoryView` bypasses the drill-down: when search is active, view flattens to a single cross-category grid of matching entries. `useState<WikiCategory | null>` is local to the view (resets on tab switch, not persisted). Folder + Notes tabs untouched. (3) [c44c58d](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c44c58d) Discussions reskinned to web-forum/message-board layout. List page widened to `max-w-5xl`, threads now render inside ONE unified panel (panel chrome on the wrapper, not per-row) with a column-header strip (`Thread | Replies | Last activity`) styled as tile-gradient over `--canvas-dark-300` border-bottom; rows use `divide-y` `<ul>` with 3-col grid `[1fr 90px 130px]` — left is avatar (9×9, recessed) + topic pill + bold title + 1-line excerpt + "started by @x" mono byline, middle is big bold reply count with `replies` mono label below, right is `MessageSquare` + relTime right-aligned. Filter chips bumped to show per-topic counts. Thread page also widened to `max-w-5xl`. Top is a panel-chrome "thread header banner" (topic pill + `Thread · N posts` mono eyebrow + brand-yellow title + starter byline). Posts now render as 2-col `[180px 1fr]` cards: left rail is a tinted column (`oklch(from var(--canvas-dark-100) l c h / 0.4)` with right divider) containing a 14×14 avatar, @username, and an `Original Poster` badge for the OP; right body has its own header strip (`#N` post-number pill in recessed chip + relTime, Reply button + kebab) above the rendered prose. OP card gets a 3px `var(--brand)` left border, replies get `var(--canvas-dark-300)`. Empty-replies state moved inline as a recessed-inset block. Quick-reply composer relocated into its own panel-chrome card below posts with a `Reply` icon heading. (4) [387bf30](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/387bf30) hive sidebar title centered (header now `flex justify-center`; inner `<div className="min-w-0">` wrapper dropped; `<h2>` gets `text-center`). **Patterns now load-bearing:** (a) hive view actions that surface binder rows should `findMany` not `findFirst` when the same binder type can appear more than once per book — codebase precedent now exists for `outline` type (T9's `getHiveOutlineView` was the last `findFirst` holdout); (b) when a view component owns drill-down state (category → entries) and the parent shell owns search, pass `isSearching: boolean` so the view knows to bypass its own drill-down and render flat results — pattern lives in `ByCategoryView`'s new contract; (c) forum-style list = panel-chrome on the wrapper + column-header strip + `divide-y <ul>` + 3-col grid per row, NOT per-row tile cards; (d) forum-style thread post = 2-col `[180px 1fr]` grid with tinted author rail + body header strip carrying `#N` post number — reusable for any future "ordered, attributed posts" surface. **Light-mode outline polish ([0bc8aec](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/0bc8aec)):** `--outline-act-cap-bg` in light mode resolved to `var(--paper-200)` — the same value as `--outline-canvas` — so each act header ("No Act / N beats / Add beat") blended into the page and was hard to scan. Bumped to `oklch(0.905 0.032 70)`, a warm tan band that sits between `--paper-200` and `--paper-300` with a touch more chroma. Dark mode untouched. Follow-up [6994be8](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/6994be8) darkened the `{N} beat(s)` count in the same header from `--outline-ink-muted` to `--outline-ink-strong` + weight 600 so it reads cleanly against the new tan band. **Outline beat dialog + hive index spec landed ([df26454](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/df26454)):** brainstorm session locked a 7-decision spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-outline-beat-dialog-and-hive-index-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-outline-beat-dialog-and-hive-index-design.md). Replaces the click-to-cycle status model on outline beats with a popup dialog (title + description + curated 8-swatch color palette + single-select label from a fixed list of 8). Legacy `status` field on existing beats maps to a derived color at read time (idea→yellow, drafting→orange, done→lime) inside `readContent()`, then drops off JSON on next save. No DB migration (jsonb on `binder_items.content`). Scope: beat dialog ships to BOTH studio + hive surfaces; the index page is HIVE-ONLY (studio binder remains the studio-side index). New hive routes `/hive/[hiveId]/outline` (forum-style table list mirroring Discussions reskin) + `/hive/[hiveId]/outline/[outlineId]` (single-outline detail, hosts the existing beat-sheet surface). New shared `beat-dialog.tsx` component built on shadcn Dialog primitive (chrome inherited); auto-focus title on open, Enter saves, Esc cancels, edit-mode Delete opens ConfirmDialog. Beat row UI: inline-edit title is REMOVED (click title → opens dialog), color dot is no longer a status cycler (click → opens dialog), new `<BeatLabelBadge>` between title and meta column (non-interactive `<span>`, hidden when null). Linked-chapter affordance UNTOUCHED on the row. Drag/sortable behavior UNTOUCHED. BETA_READER read-only mode collapses dialog footer to a single Close button. Index page list: search by outline title only (no cross-outline beat search v1), sort by Recent/A→Z/Most beats, color-dot strip per row showing up to 6 deduplicated unique `--beat-*` colors in first-appearance order. New server action `getHiveOutlineByIdAction` with cross-hive escape guard. Hive surface simplifies — the `outlines: HiveOutlineEntry[]` mapping wrapper from [b925bb0](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/b925bb0) reverts to single-outline shape since detail route now drives navigation. Out of scope (deferred): free-form colors, multi-select labels, cross-outline beat search, label/color filter chips on index, studio index, custom labels. Test posture: `readContent()` migration unit tests + `getHiveOutlineByIdAction` surface-shape test; everything else manual smoke per AGENTS.md preference. Spec self-review caught 3 ambiguities (fixed inline): label badge non-interactive, tab order excludes badge, color-dot strip in first-appearance order. Chris approved the spec, picked subagent-driven execution, and instructed to proceed without per-task checkpoints. **All 9 implementation tasks shipped end-to-end ([28ae781](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/28ae781) → [1b71fe5](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/1b71fe5)) in one session via subagent-driven flow (implementer → spec review → code-quality review → fix loop if needed per task).** 9 commits, 435/435 tests green throughout, tsc clean across the run. The new flow: any "+ Add a beat" affordance on studio outline OR hive outline detail page opens the shared `BeatDialog` (title + description + curated 8-swatch color + single-select label from 8 fixed values + auto-focus title + Enter saves + Esc cancels via shadcn Dialog primitive). Clicking a beat title OR its color dot opens the dialog in edit mode (delete button moves inside dialog footer with ConfirmDialog destructive variant). Inline contenteditable title and status-cycle dot click handlers are gone — the dialog is the single mutation surface. Legacy beats with `status` field migrate at read time via `lib/outline/migrate-beat-status.ts` (idea→yellow, drafting→orange, done→lime); status drops off JSON on next save. `<BeatLabelBadge>` renders between title and meta column on every beat row (shared component, non-interactive `<span>`, hidden when label null). Hive surface lost its multi-outline mapping wrapper from [b925bb0](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/b925bb0) — `HiveOutlineSurface` now takes a single-entry shape since the new `/hive/[hiveId]/outline` index drives per-outline navigation through `/hive/[hiveId]/outline/[outlineId]` detail route. Forum-style index mirrors Discussions reskin [c44c58d](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c44c58d) with panel-chrome wrapper + column-header strip (`Outline | Beats | Last edit`) + `divide-y <ul>` rows + 3-col grid (title + color-dot strip showing up to 6 deduplicated colors in first-appearance order + beat count + relTime); search by outline title only (200ms debounced); sort Recent/A→Z/Most beats. Cross-hive escape via `getHiveOutlineByIdAction`'s `eq(binderItems.bookId, hive.bookId)` guard matches T13 chapter-view precedent.

dialog state reset added to item.id-flip block; dead helpers `addBeat`/`deleteBeat`/`cycleStatus`/`nextStatus`/`STATUS_CYCLE`/`STATUS_COLOR`/`STATUS_LABEL` swept) · T6 [ed9d9ec](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ed9d9ec) `getHiveOutlineByIdAction(hiveId, outlineId)` with cross-hive escape guard `eq(binderItems.bookId, hive.bookId)` (T13 precedent) + 2-test surface-shape test using static top-level `import * as actions` pattern from [ca51b28](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ca51b28) · T7 [c33f78b](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c33f78b) hive surface revert to single-entry shape (`outlines: HiveOutlineEntry[]` mapping wrapper from b925bb0 unwinds), HiveOutlineSurfaceInner gets own tagged-union DialogState mirroring OutlineBoard, all 5 `addBeat(...)` callsites rewired to `openCreate(...)`, OutlineBeatRow gets onEditClick, BeatDialog mounted with commit(Beat[]) signature (this surface's signature differs from studio), interim page bridge added to keep tsc green by passing first outline to single-entry shape, dead `STATUS_CYCLE`/`nextStatus`/`addBeat`/`deleteBeat`/`cycleStatus`/`BeatStatus` import/unused `Link` import swept · T8 [4a6264c](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/4a6264c) dynamic detail route `/hive/[hiveId]/outline/[outlineId]/page.tsx` (server component, calls T6 action, `notFound()` on failure, "← Back to outlines" link, 42 LOC) · T9 [1b71fe5](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/1b71fe5) forum-style index page replaces T7 interim bridge — new `OutlineIndex` client component with title-search + 3-way sort (Recent default / A→Z / Most beats) + panel-chrome wrapper + column-header strip + `divide-y <ul>` 3-col grid rows showing title + up to 6 deduplicated `--beat-*` color dots in first-appearance order + beat count + relTime; empty states distinguish "No outlines yet" vs "No matches"; shared `readContent` + `Beat` + `BeatColor` imports from studio outline-board (no duplication).
>
> **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
> 1. **Beat data shape additivity.** `Beat` type's `color` and `label` are optional + nullable. `status?` stays as `@deprecated` for legacy reads. New code never writes `status`. The migration runs at read time inside `readContent()` and is idempotent (re-running on already-migrated beats is a no-op because the `color !== undefined` guard short-circuits).
> 2. **`commit()` signature differs between studio and hive surfaces.** Studio's `commit(Partial<OutlineContent>)` takes a partial object; hive's `commit(Beat[])` takes the new beats array directly. Both route through their respective debounce. Any future shared mutation logic must respect this divergence or normalize one of them.
> 3. **Tagged-union DialogState** (`{mode: 'closed'} | {mode: 'create', defaultAct} | {mode: 'edit', beatId}`) is the canonical pattern for dialog state in this codebase. Avoids nullable beatId/act sprawl across separate useState slots. Mirrored exactly between OutlineBoard and HiveOutlineSurfaceInner.
> 4. **`editingBeat` derivation via `beats.find(b => b.id === dialogState.beatId)`** rather than capturing the beat at click-time defends against stale-reference bugs if `beats` shifts under an open dialog (concurrent commit). This is the correct pattern for any future dialog wired to a list.
> 5. **`BeatDialog`'s `useEffect` re-syncs form state from `initial` on every open.** Without it, the prior beat's values leak into the next dialog session because `useState`'s lazy initializer only fires on first render. Future dialog components with `initial`/`mode` props should follow this pattern.
> 6. **Two-color-system architecture for outline beats.** Dark beat-* tokens live in `globals.css :root` (globally available — needed for BeatDialog which sits outside the outline scoped block). Light beat-* overrides live inside outline-board.tsx's scoped `[data-editor-theme="light"] [data-slot="outline-pane"]` block (cream-paper only). Cross-references in the comments help future Claude find the paired override.
> 7. **`getHiveOutlineByIdAction`'s `eq(binderItems.bookId, hive.bookId)` filter** is the cross-hive escape guard — same posture as T13 chapter view. Any future hive-side single-item-by-id action must include this filter to prevent cross-hive data leakage.
> 8. **Surface-shape tests for new server actions** use static top-level `import * as actions` after `vi.mock` (`ca51b28` lesson). 2 tests max per action (export + arity); behavior is gated by manual smoke per AGENTS.md preference.
>
> **Test count:** 435/435 (433 pre-epic baseline + 6 migrate-beat-status + 2 get-hive-outline-by-id = 441 net additions, but a few prior surface-shape tests got consolidated, netting 435). tsc clean throughout.
>
> **Plan landed ([1dea82f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/1dea82f)):** 10-task implementation plan at [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-03-outline-beat-dialog-and-hive-index.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-03-outline-beat-dialog-and-hive-index.md). OutlineBoard plumbs `DialogState` tagged union, "+ Add a beat" buttons call `openCreate(act)`, dialog's onSave appends/maps beats and routes through existing `commit()` debounce so 2e8311b state-isolation pattern is preserved) · T6 `getHiveOutlineByIdAction(hiveId, outlineId)` server action + 2-test surface-shape test (mirrors `lib/actions/__tests__/reading-actions.test.ts` posture — `vi.mock` require-auth + permissions + db, static top-level import); `HiveOutlineSurfaceInner` gains own DialogState tagged union mirroring OutlineBoard, readOnly path (BETA_READER) gates openCreate as no-op + onSave bails + onDelete undefined · T8 new dynamic route `/hive/[hiveId]/outline/[outlineId]/page.tsx` (server component, calls T6 action, `notFound()` on failure, `← Back to outlines` link) · T9 rewrite `/hive/[hiveId]/outline/page.tsx` as forum-style index list mirroring Discussions reskin from [c44c58d](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c44c58d) (panel-chrome wrapper + column-header strip `Outline | Beats | Last edit` + `divide-y <ul>` rows; each row title + up to 6 deduplicated unique `--beat-*` color dots in first-appearance order + beat count + relTime; search by outline title only debounced 200ms; sort Recent/A→Z/Most beats; empty states for zero outlines vs zero matches) · T10 AGENTS.md update + 10-scenario smoke checklist. Self-review confirmed every spec section maps to a task, types stay consistent (`BeatColor`/`BeatLabel`/`HiveOutlineEntry`), no placeholders, per-task commits honor Chris's per-task verification preference. **Awaiting Chris's execution choice:** subagent-driven (recommended, fresh subagent per task with per-task checkpoint — matches reader-page redesign cadence) vs inline execution via executing-plans. **Post-ship fix [3f7bc73](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/3f7bc73):** smoke caught the BeatDialog's 8 color swatches rendering with no fill (only the `--sh-tile` boxShadow visible — 8 pale circles in a row). Root cause: BeatDialog portals to `<body>` via the shadcn Dialog primitive, OUTSIDE `[data-slot="outline-pane"]`. The light-mode `--beat-*` token overrides live scoped INSIDE that outline-pane block in outline-board.tsx, so they don't cascade into the portaled dialog; and in practice the `:root` dark fallbacks also weren't resolving cleanly through the portal context. Fix inlines the 8 oklch swatch values directly in beat-dialog.tsx via a local `COLOR_FILL: Record<BeatColor, string>` map. Removes the cascade dependency for the dialog entirely. Beat-row dots stay on `var(--beat-{color})` since they live inside the outline pane and need the theme-flip. tsc clean. **Patterns now load-bearing:** any future component built on the shadcn Dialog primitive (or any Radix Portal in general) cannot rely on CSS custom properties scoped to specific `[data-slot]` blocks in the React tree — portals lift the content outside that DOM scope. Inline the values OR define them in `:root` globally. **Smoke trim [54e2da1](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/54e2da1):** removed the redundant top-right "+ Add beat" header button on the hive outline detail page. Per-act "+ Add beat" buttons inside each act header and the bottom-of-list dashed button still cover beat creation, so the header CTA was duplicative. `Plus` import stays in `hive-outline-surface.tsx` — still used by the other affordances. **Header polish [c422ea4](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c422ea4):** dropped the literal "Outline" eyebrow from the hive outline detail page header and promoted the outline title itself to the brand-yellow h1 (Comfortaa bold, `text-4xl`, truncated). The route already lives at `/outline/[id]` so the eyebrow word was redundant context. Follow-up [26b33cb](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/26b33cb) shrank the new h1 from `text-4xl` to `text-2xl` per Chris's smoke — the 4xl was too dominant. **Wiki category UX polish [1b4ef85](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/1b4ef85):** four trims on `/hive/[hiveId]/wiki`. (1) Removed the 3px colored left border on level-1 category cards — the icon chip's accent tint already carries the category cue. (2) Brightened level-1 card text — label + count flipped from `--canvas-dark-ink-strong` to literal `#ffffff`; blurb to `oklch(1 0 0 / 0.78)` for high contrast on the tile gradient. (3) Killed the picker re-roundtrip: `ByCategoryView`'s `onAddEntry()` prop became `onAddEntryInCategory(category)` so the level-2 "Add a {label}" + "Add the first {label}" buttons jump straight into create-with-category, skipping the all-categories picker. (4) Removed the redundant top-right "+ New Entry" button from the shell header — the level-2 add affordances cover creation. Side cleanups: `WikiCategoryPicker` import + mount + `pickerOpen` state removed from the shell (no callers remained), `Plus` icon import dropped, `pending` flag from `useTransition` dropped. **Pattern note:** when a parent owns category selection (the category card click in level 1), the "Add" affordance inside the drill-down view should call back with the implicit category rather than re-opening the parent's category-picker dialog. **Character-category fix [3fe1799](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/3fe1799):** opening a character on `/hive/[hiveId]/wiki` showed the wrong badge ("Other") and the wrong blurb ("Doesn't fit a category — that's fine.") because characters are stored as `type='character'` binder items with `content: null` (the dedicated character renderer owns its own shape on the studio side), so `HiveWikiEntryEditor`'s `readContent()` fell back to `category: 'OTHER'`. Match the list-side coercion in `getHiveWikiView`: derive `displayCategory: WikiCategory = item.type === 'character' ? 'CHARACTER' : content.category` and look up `CATEGORY_TEMPLATE_MAP` via that override. `content.category` is left untouched on disk (character renderer doesn't care). **Pattern note:** the character/wiki-entry split is intentional — characters keep their own first-class binder type so the studio's dedicated character profile renderer can drive them, but the hive wiki surface union-coerces them back to CHARACTER for UX parity. Any future hive-side wiki UI that reads raw binder-item content must respect this same coercion. **Annotate/Suggest popover focus fix [5e7a9a9](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/5e7a9a9):** Chris smoke-caught that selecting text in the studio editor + clicking Annotate or Suggest Edit did nothing — the buttons looked clickable but no modal opened. The same buttons worked fine on `/hive/[hiveId]/chapters/[chapterId]`. Root cause: the popover sits in a fixed-position portal outside the editor's DOM tree. Mousedown on any popover button shifts focus off the (editable) TipTap editor → editor emits `blur` → `useSelectionPopover`'s blur listener fires `setState(null)` → by the time the click handler resolves, `openAnnotate`/`openSuggest` read `sel.{from,to,text}` as null and early-return. The hive chapter view's editor is `editable: false` (read-only), so its blur doesn't fire the same way and selection state survives the click — that's why the same component worked there. Fix adds `onMouseDown={e => e.preventDefault()}` on the popover toolbar `<div>`. The toolbar (and all child buttons) no longer steal focus on mousedown, so the editor keeps focus, the selection stays live, and the click handler captures it correctly. **Pattern note for future floating menus over editable contenteditable surfaces:** ALWAYS preventDefault on mousedown to preserve focus + selection state. Without it, even a brief focus shift will clear the selection before onClick reads it. This is the canonical pattern for toolbars over TipTap / ProseMirror editors. **Hive collab UX rework spec landed ([01f90f6](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/01f90f6)):** brainstorm session locked an 8-decision design at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-hive-collab-ux-rework-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-hive-collab-ux-rework-design.md) addressing three concerns Chris flagged: (1) annotations User B creates on hive side aren't visible in studio editor unless User A knows to toggle the gutter; (2) chapter Status/Synopsis/Scene Planner only show in studio metadata panel, not for hive members; (3) `/hive/.../annotations`, `/hive/.../suggestions`, and `/hive/.../chapters` overlap so users don't know which page to use. Decisions: (a) annotations live with the prose (Google-Docs-style mental model); (b) studio editor's gutter auto-opens on chapter load when `annotationCount + pendingSuggestionCount > 0` (fire-once per chapter switch, tracked in a ref to defeat React 19 strict-mode double-fire); (c) toolbar gutter button gains numeric badge `N` when gutter is closed AND total > 0; (d) Notes stays author-private (label + placeholder unchanged) — only Status + Synopsis + Scene Planner shared with hive; (e) hive chapter view gains a header strip above prose with status pill + line-clamp-3 synopsis + collapsible `<details>` scene planner, hidden sections when their data is empty; (f) `/hive/.../annotations` page deleted entirely (hive sidebar drops from 11 entries to 10), `lib/actions/hive-annotations.actions.ts` untouched (still consumed by gutter); (g) `/hive/.../suggestions` kept unchanged as reviewer queue (canReviewSuggestion gate stays); (h) `/hive/.../chapters` index gains per-row activity badges `N annotations` (neutral) + `M suggestions` (brand-yellow when viewer can review), hidden when count is 0, both counts via single GROUP BY query per kind stitched in JS via Map. Hive chapter view's gutter stays always-mounted (no toggle, no auto-open, no badge) — intentional asymmetry since the surface's only purpose is collab. Out of scope: real-time updates, per-user read/unread tracking, cross-chapter annotation digest, making Notes shared, renaming /suggestions. 14-scenario smoke checklist baked into the spec for post-impl verification. **Hive collab UX rework plan landed ([d0fa6ae](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/d0fa6ae)):** 11-task implementation plan at [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-03-hive-collab-ux-rework.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-03-hive-collab-ux-rework.md). Same commit amended the spec — caught during plan-writing that Scene Planner is actually three text fields (sceneGoal/sceneConflict/sceneOutcome) per chapter on `binder_items.content`, NOT a scenes array as the original spec assumed (a real defect — the studio metadata-panel implements it that way). Spec + plan now use `scenePlanner: { goal, conflict, outcome }` shape consistently. **Task SHA layout:** T1 extend `getChapterAction` with `annotationCount` + `pendingSuggestionCount` (top-level + open only; skip queries when book has no hive via `getBookHive` cache gate) · T2 extend `getHiveChapterView` with status + synopsis + scenePlanner (no counts here — hive gutter is always-mounted, doesn't need them) · T3 extend `getHiveChapterListAction` with per-row counts via two GROUP BY queries stitched in JS via Map (matches `listBuzzPostsAction`/`listHiveSubmissionsAction` precedent — no correlated-subquery pattern in the codebase) + surface-shape test using static `import * as actions` after `vi.mock` (ca51b28 lesson) · T4 studio editor auto-opens gutter on chapter load via `maybeAutoOpenGutter(itemId, data)` helper called from cache-miss, cache-hit, AND `reloadActiveChapter` paths; `autoOpenedForItemRef` tracks the active item id so React 19 strict-mode double-fire + manual closes don't reopen; mutually exclusive with `historyOpen` · T5 toolbar gutter button gains brand-yellow numeric pill badge when `gutterOpen === false && total > 0`, caps at "99+" · T6 new `<ChapterMetadataHeader>` (status pill via `--status-{level}` tokens with `oklch(from ... l c h / 0.18)` tint + line-clamp-3 italic synopsis + collapsible `<details>` scene planner with three stanzas) · T7 mount on hive chapter surface between byline and prose · T8 new `<ChapterActivityBadges>` (renders nothing when both counts 0; suggestion badge gets brand-yellow accent when `canReview` is true) · T9 mount on chapter index rows, server component pre-computes `canReview` via `canReviewSuggestion(viewerRole)` once and threads as prop · T10 `git rm -r` the `annotations/` directory + drop `Annotations` from sidebar `NAV_ITEMS` + drop the now-unused `StickyNote` lucide import; `lib/actions/hive-annotations.actions.ts` UNTOUCHED (still consumed by gutter) · T11 AGENTS.md update + ship. Self-review confirmed every spec section maps to a task, types are consistent (`ChapterStatus` from `@/lib/books/is-chapter-reader-visible`, `scenePlanner` shape identical across T2/T6/T7, count field names identical across T1/T3/T4/T5/T9), no placeholders, and the per-task fragile spots (provider's actual setter names, action's row field name, surface's prop path) all carry explicit "check the existing code" guidance for the implementer. **All 10 implementation tasks shipped end-to-end** via subagent-driven flow (implementer → spec review → code-quality review per task, reviews compressed to inline checks for the smallest atomic tasks). 437/437 tests green throughout, tsc clean across the run. **Task SHAs:** T1 [f1f54a2](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/f1f54a2) extend `getChapterAction` with `annotationCount` + `pendingSuggestionCount` (top-level + open only; skip queries when book has no hive via `getBookHive` React-cached gate) · T2 [ce62821](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ce62821) extend `getHiveChapterView` with status + synopsis + scenePlanner via new `safeString()` helper (treats blank-after-trim as null); extends `chapters.findFirst` columns with `status` and `binderItems.findFirst` columns with `content` · T3 [18244d2](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/18244d2) extend `getHiveChapterListAction` with per-row counts via two GROUP BY queries stitched in JS via Map (matches `listBuzzPostsAction`/`listHiveSubmissionsAction` precedent — codebase has no correlated-subquery pattern). Implementer correctly renamed local `topLevel` → `topLevelRows` and built `chapterIds` from top-level rows only (sub-chapters with non-null parentId don't drive badges) + filtered null `chapterId` values out of the `inArray` list to defend against binderItems-without-chapters-row edge case · T4 [5533871](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/5533871) `BookEditorProvider` auto-opens gutter via `maybeAutoOpenGutter(itemId, data)` helper called from cache-miss, cache-hit AND `reloadActiveChapter` paths; `autoOpenedForItemRef` tracks the active item id so React 19 strict-mode double-fire + manual closes don't reopen; mutually exclusive with `historyOpen` (raw `setGutterOpen` and `setHistoryOpen` setters were already at lines 114-115 — no surfacing needed) · T5 [d1c0354](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/d1c0354) gutter toolbar button gains brand-yellow numeric pill badge when `gutterOpen === false && total > 0`, caps at "99+", `pointer-events: none` so it doesn't intercept clicks. Implementer inlined the gutter button (skipped `ToolbarButton` wrapper) to add `position: relative` and corner badge — sound tactical decision since threading a `badge` prop through the 20+ shared `ToolbarButton` consumers would have polluted an API surface 19 of them don't need; parity with the wrapper is faithful via the same `tbtnClass`/`tbtnStyle`/`tbtnHoverEnter`/`tbtnHoverLeave` helpers · T6 [5f1f8cb](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/5f1f8cb) new `<ChapterMetadataHeader>` presentational component (status pill via `--status-{level}` tokens with `oklch(from ... l c h / 0.18)` tint + line-clamp-3 italic synopsis + collapsible `<details>` Scene planner with three labeled stanzas) · T7 [a1961a9](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/a1961a9) mount on hive chapter surface between byline and prose. Implementer caught that the action's return places status/synopsis/scenePlanner at the TOP LEVEL of `HiveChapterViewData` (alongside `chapter`/`book`/`author`/`hive`), not nested under `data.chapter` as the plan's example assumed — uses `data.status`, `data.synopsis`, `data.scenePlanner` · T8 [e3215ad](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/e3215ad) new `<ChapterActivityBadges>` (`pluralize` helper for 1 vs N; suggestion badge gets brand-yellow accent when `canReview` is true) · T9 [3205a43](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/3205a43) mount on chapter index rows. Server component calls `requireHiveMember(hiveId, userId)` to get role + computes `canReview = canReviewSuggestion(role)` once; the existing client `Chapter` type widens with the count fields and the row title slot restructures into a `flex-1 min-w-0` column wrapper so badges sit directly beneath the truncated title without breaking the existing num/title/chevron horizontal layout. Note: `requireHiveMember` runs twice per request (once in page, once inside action) — cheap and acceptable for v1, could be optimized later by widening the action's return with `viewerRole` · T10 [c0df081](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c0df081) `git rm -r` the `annotations/` directory + drop `Annotations` from sidebar `NAV_ITEMS` (11→10 → linter later removed `Chapters` then `Wiki` rebalance? — sidebar settled at 11 entries) + drop the now-unused `StickyNote` lucide import. `lib/actions/hive-annotations.actions.ts` UNTOUCHED — still consumed by the chapter-view gutter. **Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:** (1) `getBookHive` React-cached gate is the canonical pattern for skipping hive-specific queries when a book isn't in a hive; any future studio-side action that needs hive data should follow it. (2) Auto-open ref pattern (`autoOpenedForItemRef = useRef<string | null>(null)` + helper that checks `ref.current === id` early-return) is the canonical pattern for one-shot-per-item side effects in the load-bearing `BookEditorProvider`. (3) When extending shared `ToolbarButton` would touch 20+ consumers for a feature only one needs, inline the consumer locally — drift risk is bounded by routing the inline version through the same style helpers (`tbtnClass`/`tbtnStyle`). (4) Module-scope `safeString(v: unknown): string | null` helper coerces non-string + blank-after-trim to null — reusable for any future `binder_items.content` jsonb read. (5) The hive chapter view's `HiveChapterViewData` shape places top-level fields (status/synopsis/scenePlanner) alongside `chapter`/`book`/`author`/`hive` — NOT nested under `chapter`. Future hive-side chapter actions should follow the same flat shape. **Test count:** 437/437 (435 baseline + 2 new surface-shape tests in `get-hive-chapter-list-action.test.ts`). tsc clean throughout. **Smoke fixes round 1 ([287d31f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/287d31f) + [088b232](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/088b232)):** Chris smoke-tested the new gutter and flagged two bugs. (1) `defaultFilterState()` set `showSuggestions: true`, so on first open the "Suggestions" pill rendered active alongside "All"; users wanting annotations-only had to deselect every time. Flipped the default to `false` (and the localStorage hydration fallback too) so on first open only "All" highlights — users opt INTO suggestions by clicking the pill. (2) **Load-bearing bug:** every annotation/suggestion Chris created immediately showed up in the Orphaned tab. Root cause: `useSelectionPopover` captures `from`/`to` from `editor.state.selection` (ProseMirror positions), the modal passes them through to `createAnnotationAction({selectionStart, selectionEnd})`, the action calls `patchDocWithMark(doc, ..., selectionStart, selectionEnd)` — but `patchDocWithMark` (and its sibling `findMarkRanges`) walks **text-character offsets**, NOT ProseMirror positions. Each block boundary adds 2 to PM positions but 0 to text offsets. For multi-paragraph documents, PM > text by 2 × (paragraph count); the mark either lands in the wrong range or — when PM exceeds total text — silently skips entirely. With no mark in the doc, the next `findOrphanMarks` walk flags the DB row as orphaned and the gutter shows it under Orphaned. New helper `lib/tiptap-extensions/pm-pos.ts::pmPosToTextOffset(doc, pos)` walks the doc tracking PM and text cursors simultaneously and returns the matching text offset. Both `createAnnotationAction` and `createSuggestionAction` now convert before calling `patchDocWithMark`. **DB still stores PM positions on the row** (unchanged) — `findMarkRanges` locates marks by attribute id, not by stored range, so the row's selection fields are display-only metadata and don't need re-conversion. **Pattern note for future hive collab actions:** any code path that takes `selectionStart`/`selectionEnd` from `editor.state.selection.{from,to}` and feeds them into the doc-walker helpers in `lib/tiptap-extensions/` MUST go through `pmPosToTextOffset` first. The two coordinate systems look interchangeable (both are non-negative integers) but they diverge by 2 × block-nesting depth and the failure mode (orphaned-immediately) is silent. **Last commit:** [088b232](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/088b232) — fix(hive/collab): default filter state no longer pre-selects Suggestions. **Next concrete step:** Chris runs the 14-scenario smoke checklist from the spec ([docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-hive-collab-ux-rework-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-03-hive-collab-ux-rework-design.md) "Carry-forward smoke checklist for Chris"): (1) author no-activity chapter in `/studio` → gutter closed, no badge; (2) User B annotates from hive chapter view → status pill + synopsis + collapsed Scene Planner visible above prose, gutter card appears; (3) User A opens same chapter in `/studio` → gutter auto-opens with new annotation visible, refresh still auto-opens; (4) close gutter manually → toolbar button gains `1` badge, click to reopen clears badge; (5) `/hive/[hiveId]/chapters` → that chapter shows `1 annotation` badge, zero-count chapters show no badges; (6) User B suggests edit on different chapter → row shows `1 suggestion` badge (brand-yellow for OWNER/MOD); (7) `/hive/[hiveId]/suggestions` as OWNER/MOD shows pending list, as BETA_READER shows permission-denied; (8) hive sidebar has no Annotations row; (9) `/hive/[hiveId]/annotations` returns 404; (10) studio metadata-panel still shows "Notes" with "Private notes — only you can see these"; (11) chapter without synopsis / without scenes on hive side → header omits those subsections cleanly; (12) chapter without a hive → studio opens normally, no counts/badge/auto-open; (13) light + dark mode on hive metadata header — status pill, synopsis, scene list all legible; (14) chapter with 5 annotations + 3 suggestions → toolbar badge shows `8`, chapter index row shows both badges. That can't support the spec's checkbox UI (mark arbitrary chapters, unmark a middle chapter). > **Execution in flight (subagent-driven, per-task checkpoint with Chris):** T1 + T2 shipped, 11 tasks remain. **T1 [1983e7e](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/1983e7e)** — `chapter_reads` table added to `db/schema/social.ts` (composite PK `userId + chapterBinderItemId`, denorm `bookId` with `chapter_reads_user_book_idx`, `readAt timestamp`, all FKs cascade); new idempotent runner `scripts/migrate-reader-redesign.ts` (`IF NOT EXISTS` on table + index, matches sibling `migrate-h4.ts` shape with `neon(DATABASE_URL!)` + `npx dotenv -e .env.local -- tsx`); ran twice on dev DB to prove idempotency; `readingProgress` untouched. **T2 [0d7aa71](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/0d7aa71)** — `lib/actions/reading.actions.ts` rewritten: `markChapterReadAction(bookId, chapterBinderItemId)` (second arg renamed; resolves `chapters.id` via `chapters.binderItemId` for the cursor upsert; inserts into `chapter_reads` ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING targeting the composite PK; still upserts `readingProgress.chapterId` so "Continue Reading" + auto-mark from `/read/[chapterId]` keep working; cursor upsert skipped if the `chapters.id` lookup is empty — read intent recorded regardless); new `unmarkChapterReadAction(bookId, chapterBinderItemId)` (DELETE from `chapter_reads` only, NEVER touches `readingProgress`); `getReadingProgressAction` return shape unchanged but `readChapterBinderItemIds` now sourced from `chapter_reads` instead of the linear at-or-before-cursor derivation (the old `binderItems`/`asc` walk + `binderItems`/`asc` imports are gone). Auto-mark call site at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/[bookId]/read/[chapterId]/page.tsx:93` updated to pass `current.binderItemId` (reused from the existing `allChapters` projection — no schema lookup added). New test file `lib/actions/__tests__/reading-actions.test.ts` with 3 surface-shape tests (export + arity) mocked via `vi.mock` of `@/lib/require-auth`, `@/lib/books/can-read`, `@/db` (same pattern as `create-hive-action.test.ts`). 427/427 tests, tsc clean across both ships.
>
> **Patterns now load-bearing (carry forward to remaining tasks + future read-tracking work):**
> 1. **`chapter_reads` is the per-chapter read truth.** Never derive reads from `readingProgress.chapterId` linearly — that table is the cursor only. Any new read-related query should join `chapter_reads`, not walk binder item order.
> 2. **`markChapterReadAction` is the ONE place that bridges read-set + cursor.** Both writes happen in the same call so the auto-mark from `/read/[chapterId]` keeps populating both tables atomically (from the caller's perspective). Don't add a separate "set cursor" action; if a new caller needs cursor-only behavior, it goes through `markChapterReadAction` and the read insert is the cheap idempotent byproduct.
> 3. **Server actions take `chapterBinderItemId` everywhere.** The chapters PK (`chapters.id`) is now exclusively a `readingProgress` cursor implementation detail. UI + new actions must pass `binderItems.id`. The auto-mark page demonstrated the pattern: pull the binder item id from a separately-loaded projection.
> 4. **Surface-shape tests are the codebase norm for reshape commits.** `lib/actions/__tests__/reading-actions.test.ts` mirrors `create-hive-action.test.ts` (mock `@/lib/require-auth` + `@/lib/books/can-read` + `@/db`). Behavior tests are gated by manual smoke per AGENTS.md preference.
>
> **T3 [78bb164](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/78bb164)** — `<ShareBookDialog>` shipped at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/_components/share-book-dialog.tsx`. Client component wraps shadcn `Dialog` (chrome inherited from the primitive — no new styling work). Props: `{ url, visibility: 'PUBLIC'|'FRIENDS'|'PRIVATE', trigger: ReactNode }`. Title `Share this book`, read-only URL `<input>` with focus-select-all + `aria-label="Share URL"`, brand-yellow `Copy` button that calls `navigator.clipboard.writeText` → flips to `Copied ✓` for 2s + sonner `toast.success('Link copied')`; catch path fires `toast.error('Could not copy link')`. Trigger via `<DialogTrigger asChild>` so the consumer (T4 BookHero) supplies a styled button. Privacy note below URL: `Lock` icon + "Only people you invite can open this link." for PRIVATE; `Users` icon + "Only your friends can open this link." for FRIENDS; no note for PUBLIC. `aria-live="polite"` on the Copy button. Token usage matches the recessed-input pattern from `create-hive-modal.tsx` (`--canvas-dark-100` bg + `--sh-inset` boxShadow + `--r-row` radius for input; `--brand` + `--brand-ink` + `--r-btn` for CTA). Three code-review nits flagged as optional: (a) `setTimeout` after unmount is a noop in React 19 so no cleanup needed today, (b) `privacyNote.Icon` dot-notation JSX works fine just less conventional than a capitalized local, (c) no `cn()` since classes are static — fine until a consumer needs `className` override.

> **Carry-forward fix [ca51b28](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ca51b28)** — `lib/actions/__tests__/reading-actions.test.ts` was using `await import('@/lib/actions/reading.actions')` inside each `it()` block. Under full-suite parallel load the module-graph parse exceeded the 5s vitest default and timed out; solo runs (~2.7s) masked the issue, so T2 reported 427/427 green when it shouldn't have. Hoisted to a single top-level `import * as readingActions` after the `vi.mock` declarations. **Pattern note for future test files in this repo:** any test file that needs to mock a module then import a consumer must keep the `vi.mock` calls above the `import` statement (vitest hoists them) — but the consumer import itself should be top-level static, not dynamic-per-test. Dynamic-per-test only makes sense if you need to vary the mock between `it()` blocks via `vi.resetModules()` (we don't here).

Panel-gradient card with `[grid-template-columns:200px_1fr]` on sm+ (mobile stacks): left = 200px 2:3 cover img (or fallback gradient), right = title (Comfortaa bold 28px brand-yellow) + author row (24px avatar + `by` + `<Link>` to `/${locale}/u/${authorUsername}` with `displayName ?? @username`) + series line (mono uppercase, both `Book N of <italic Series>` and `Part of <italic Series>` branches) + pill row (genre conditional · privacy pill always with `Globe`/`Lock`/`Users` lucide icon via `VISIBILITY_META` record · `tags?.slice(0, 5)`) + 4-stat row (`Chapters` shows `${readCount} / ${totalChapters}` only when `isAuthenticated && totalChapters > 0`, else just total; `Words` humanized to `Xk` for ≥1000; `Likes` reads live `likeCount` state; `Comments` from prop) + `line-clamp-3 max-w-xl` synopsis + CTA row (left to right: brand-pill `Start Reading →` or `Continue Reading →` Link using `continueReadingHref ?? startReadingHref` precedence with label conditional on continue truthiness, renders nothing when both null; `Favorite` tile button with Heart icon + `aria-pressed` + optimistic flip of both `liked` AND `likeCount` with full rollback on action failure + sonner error toast; Share button as `<ShareBookDialog>` trigger via `asChild`; `Bookmark` tile button same optimistic shape as Favorite but no count tracking; `Edit in studio →` text link to `/${locale}/studio/${book.id}` gated on `isAuthor`). Guest clicks on Favorite/Bookmark fire `toast.info('Sign in to favorite this book')` / `'Sign in to bookmark this book'` and early-return without invoking the server action — intentional UX shift from the legacy `social-actions.tsx` which hid the buttons entirely. Heart/Bookmark active-state coloring is `fill-current text-[var(--brand)]` (lucide pattern: `fill-current` ties SVG fill to `currentColor`, sibling text color sets it to brand-yellow). Props widen `PublicBook & { visibility: 'PUBLIC'|'FRIENDS'|'PRIVATE'; commentCount: number }` because `getPublicBookAction` doesn't return those two — T11 page orchestrator will fill via sibling `books`/`count(bookComments)` fetches. Verified `toggleBookLikeAction(bookId)` + `toggleBookmarkAction(bookId)` signatures + `ActionResult` shape.

None of these are queued for follow-up unless a smoke pass surfaces a real bug.
>
> **Pattern for future stacked-section components in this epic:** inline `style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, var(--canvas-dark-250), var(--canvas-dark-200))', boxShadow: 'var(--sh-card)', borderTop: '1px solid var(--br-card)' }}` is the canonical panel chrome — T5+ chrome-bearing components copy this shape. Tiles inside panels use `linear-gradient(180deg, var(--canvas-dark-350), var(--canvas-dark-300))` + `var(--sh-tile)`. T4 stashes the tile gradient in a `tileStyle` const and reuses it across 3 buttons (Favorite, Share trigger, Bookmark) — same trick is valid for T5-T10 when a component renders ≥2 tile elements.
>
> **T5 [70a7f5f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/70a7f5f)** — `<BookTabStrip>` shipped at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/_components/book-tab-strip.tsx` (~60 LOC client component, no props). `<nav aria-label="Book sections">` with three `<a>` pills (Chapters/Comments/About) — `position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 10` with the same T4 panel chrome inline style (panel gradient + `--sh-card` + `--br-card`) so content doesn't bleed through when scrolled past the hero. IntersectionObserver with `rootMargin: '-80px 0px -60% 0px'` and threshold `[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1]` watches `#chapters`/`#comments`/`#about` section roots by id — most-intersecting (highest ratio) sets the active pill, ties broken by ratio sort. Active pill: `bg-[var(--brand)] text-[var(--brand-ink)] font-semibold` + `aria-current="page"`. Inactive: `text-[var(--canvas-dark-ink-muted)]` with hover `text-[var(--canvas-dark-ink-strong)]`. Click handler calls `e.preventDefault()` then `getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })`. `TabId` type derived via `(typeof TABS)[number]['id']` so the const tabs list + the runtime narrowing stay in sync. Effect cleanup calls `observer.disconnect()`. The `sectionEls.filter((el): el is HTMLElement => el !== null)` predicate makes the component resilient to T6/T7/T8 sections rendering in any order or being absent.
>
> **Load-bearing assumption (T5 → T11):** all three section roots must be mounted **synchronously** in the same render tree. T11's page orchestrator does this — it renders `<ChaptersPanel>`/`<CommentsPanel>`/`<AboutSection>` statically below the tab strip without Suspense boundaries between them. If a future change wraps any of the three in a Suspense boundary or lazy-mounts them, that section's pill will never light up because the effect runs once with `[]` deps and never re-attaches. If that bites: add a `MutationObserver` on `document.body`, or accept a `sectionIds: string[]` prop and re-run the effect on prop change.
>
> **Code-review nits flagged as optional (none blocking):** (a) `backdrop-blur` on the nav is a no-op over the fully-opaque panel gradient — drop or swap to alpha-channel gradient stops if a frosted effect is desired later, (b) `z-10` is fine for the current flat reader page but a future modal/drawer/overlay at the same z-index will occlude the sticky strip mid-scroll, (c) `behavior: 'smooth'` doesn't branch on `prefers-reduced-motion` — the platform mostly handles this but explicit branching would be more correct.
>
> **T6 [d7e1983](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/d7e1983) + fix [e1c6797](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/e1c6797)** — `<ChaptersPanel>` shipped at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/_components/chapters-panel.tsx` (~150 LOC client component). Root: `<section id="chapters" className="scroll-mt-20 ..." style={panel chrome}>` so T5 `<BookTabStrip>`'s IntersectionObserver finds it and the scroll-jump clears the sticky strip. Header: Comfortaa bold brand-yellow `Chapters` heading + `{readCount} / {totalCount} read` subtitle (gated on `isAuthenticated && totalCount > 0`); thin `h-1 rounded-full` progress bar below on inset rail (`var(--canvas-dark-100)` bg + `var(--sh-inset)`) with brand-yellow inner fill at `width: ${progressPct}%` + `transition-all`, same gate. Author sees all chapters; non-author filters via `isChapterReaderVisible(status)` (REVISED or FINAL only). Empty state: italic muted "No chapters yet." when `visibleChapters.length === 0`. Rows are tile-gradient `<li>`s with left-to-right: mark-as-read toggle (rendered only for authed+reader-visible; `h-5 w-5 shrink-0` spacer span otherwise to preserve column alignment), `<button aria-pressed={isRead} aria-label="Mark X as read/unread" className="shrink-0">` with `<CheckCircle2 text-[var(--brand)]>` when read or `<Circle text-[var(--canvas-dark-ink-muted)]>` when not; 1-based index `{i+1}`; title cell (`<Link>` to `${readerBasePath}/read/${chapterId}` when reader-visible, italic muted `<span>` when locked); right meta (`Updated Mon DD` via `toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })` when reader-visible, `Draft — coming soon` uppercase tracking-wider when locked). NO per-row word count (book total lives in hero stats). `onReadSetChange?: (next: Set<string>) => void` prop fires on every toggle (optimistic flip AND rollback) so T11's `<ReaderPageShell>` can lift the read-set into the hero's `Chapters X/Y read` stat. Guest click on toggle fires `toast.info('Sign in to track your progress')` and does NOT call the server action.
>
> **Bug caught + fixed in code review** (the load-bearing pattern for future optimistic state in this codebase): the first cut captured `readSet` by closure for rollback (`setReadSet(readSet)` inside the action's catch branch). Walking the scenario: user clicks A (snapshot `{}`, optimistic `{A}`) → user clicks B before A resolves (snapshot `{A}`, optimistic `{A, B}`) → A's action fails → rollback fires `setReadSet({})` and silently wipes B's successful flip. The fix in [e1c6797](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/e1c6797) switches BOTH the optimistic flip AND the rollback to functional updaters `(prev) => ...`. Rollback inverts only `binderItemId`'s flip against `prev` instead of stomping the entire set. `wasRead` is still captured at click-time because it represents the user's INTENT at the click, not global state — it can't drift. **Pattern note for any future optimistic toggle with a parent-side stat lift:** never capture state-at-call-time for rollback; always do `setX((prev) => invert(prev, theOneRowYouChanged))`. The `onReadSetChange?.(set)` invocation lives inside the updater body so the parent always sees the same value the state lands on — pragmatic violation of React's "updater must be pure" guideline, accepted for the live-stat-sync contract.
>
> **Other code-review nits flagged as optional (none blocking):** (a) `isPending` from `useTransition` is discarded; wiring `disabled={isPending}` + `aria-busy` on the toggle buttons would give visual double-click defense, (b) toggle button has no `type="button"` — defensive habit not strictly required outside a `<form>`. Legacy chapter-list's `expanded`/show-first-5 affordance + `currentChapterId` "Reading" highlight are intentionally dropped — the new IA puts the full list inside the Chapters section card and "what to read next" is the Continue/Start CTA in the hero.
>
> **T7 [8d8c45f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/8d8c45f)** — `<CommentsPanel>` colocated copy shipped at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/_components/comments-panel.tsx` (~150 LOC client component). Legacy at `app/[locale]/(public)/_components/comments-panel.tsx` UNTOUCHED — T12 audits callers + decides delete. Root: `<section id="comments" className="scroll-mt-20 ..." style={panel chrome}>` so T5 `<BookTabStrip>` finds the anchor. Header: Comfortaa bold brand-yellow `Comments` heading + count subtitle in muted small text. Composer (authed only): viewer avatar + recessed `<textarea>` (`--canvas-dark-100` bg + `--sh-inset` boxShadow + `--r-row` radius, `placeholder="Add a comment…"`, `rows={2}`, `maxLength={1000}`) + right-aligned brand-yellow `Post` button with `disabled={isPending || !draft.trim()}` + `type="button"`. Guest fallback: muted line with `<Link>` to `/${locale}/sign-in` (brand-yellow `Sign in` + plain `to leave a comment.`). Empty state: italic muted `No comments yet.` (rendered after composer/sign-in prompt). Comment rows: avatar + clickable `@username` (or `displayName` fallback, or `Anonymous` if both null) `<Link>` to `/${locale}/u/${authorUsername}` + `relTime` muted + body with `whitespace-pre-wrap` so line breaks render. `Load more` tile-button (`type="button"`, `disabled={isPending}`) calls `getBookCommentsAction(bookId, page+1)` inside `startTransition`, appends results via `setComments((prev) => [...prev, ...result.data.comments])`, updates `hasMore` + increments local `page`. Post flow is optimistic: `setDraft('')` clears immediately, on action failure `setDraft(trimmed)` restores + `toast.error('Could not post comment')`; on success prepends `result.data` + bumps `count`. Avatars use raw `<img>` (consistent with the codebase pattern, not `next/image`). `BookComment` type from `lib/actions/discover.actions.ts` consumed as-is (`{ id, content, createdAt, authorUsername, authorDisplayName, authorAvatarUrl }`).
>
> **Code-review nits flagged as optional (all carry-forward from legacy or codebase convention, none blocking):** (a) draft-restore race — if a user types "hello" + Posts, then types "world" before the action resolves and fails, the rollback restores "hello" and overwrites "world"; mitigation is the rarity (sub-1s action window + need for failure + need for new keystrokes during it); legacy has the identical bug; fix-if-it-bites is `setDraft((current) => current || trimmed)` to only restore when the field is still empty, (b) `relTime` doesn't auto-update — a fresh "just now" stays "just now" until next render; codebase-wide convention, not worth a `setInterval` here, (c) `count` has no decrement path — fine until delete-comment ships, (d) `loadMore` reads `page+1` from closure; safe today because `useTransition` serializes the action with the `setPage(nextPage)` call but switch to functional `setPage((p) => p+1)` if a future change parallelizes loads.
>
> **T8 [cf4ca5a](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/cf4ca5a)** — `<AboutSection>` shipped at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/_components/about-section.tsx` (~95 LOC pure SERVER component, no `'use client'`). Root: `<section id="about" className="scroll-mt-20 ..." style={panel chrome}>` so T5 `<BookTabStrip>` finds the anchor. Heading: Comfortaa bold brand-yellow `About this book`. Three blocks: (1) Synopsis — non-null splits on `/\n\n+/` into `<p>` paragraphs inside `max-w-prose space-y-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[var(--canvas-dark-ink)]`; null renders italic muted `The author hasn't written a description yet.` (apostrophe HTML-escaped as `&apos;` per JSX). (2) Metadata grid — 2-col `<dl>` with two `<div>` children, uppercase tracking-wider muted `text-[10px]` labels (`First published` / `Last updated`), strong-ink `<dd>` values formatted via `fmt(d: Date | string)` helper using `toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })` so output reads `March 14, 2026`. Helper accepts string OR Date to survive Next.js's server→client Date serialization (matches the `formatUpdatedLabel` precedent from the legacy chapter-list). (3) Author card — tile-gradient (`linear-gradient(180deg, var(--canvas-dark-350), var(--canvas-dark-300))` + `var(--sh-tile)`) rounded `--r-row` panel with `p-4 flex items-center gap-4` containing 48px (`h-12 w-12`) avatar img (or muted `--canvas-dark-200` circle fallback) + identity column (`min-w-0 flex-1`) with truncate Comfortaa bold display name (`displayName ?? username ?? 'Unknown'` fallback chain) + truncate mono muted `@username` line (only when `username` set) + brand-yellow `View profile →` `<Link>` to `/${locale}/u/${username}` with lucide `ArrowRight h-3.5 w-3.5` (only when `username` set so a null-username profile never produces a broken `/u/null` href).
>
> **Code-review nits flagged as optional (all carry-forward from codebase convention, none blocking):** (a) raw `<img>` instead of `next/image` for the author avatar — matches the codebase pattern set by T4 BookHero + T7 CommentsPanel + legacy components; flag for a future avatar-component consolidation pass, (b) `alt=""` on author avatar is technically correct since name is adjacent and would otherwise double-announce, defensible but `alt={displayName ?? username ?? ''}` would survive a future refactor that moves the name out of the row, (c) missing `flex-shrink-0` on the `View profile` link is theoretical at expected panel widths.
>
> **Public Book Reader Page Redesign ✅ CODE-COMPLETE.** All 13 plan tasks shipped end-to-end in one session via subagent-driven execution (per-task implementer → spec review → code-quality review → fix loop if needed → checkpoint before next task). 16 commits, 427/427 tests green, tsc clean. Full task-by-task breakdown lives under "What Has Been Built" → **Public Book Reader Page Redesign**. The new reader at `/[locale]/books/[bookId]` is a centered `max-w-5xl` stacked-section page on `bg-[#262728]` with 6 new colocated components (`<BookHero>` rich cover + metadata + CTA row · sticky `<BookTabStrip>` with IntersectionObserver scroll-spy · `<ChaptersPanel>` with manual mark-as-read circles + brand-yellow progress bar · `<CommentsPanel>` flat composer + Load more · `<AboutSection>` description + dates + author card · `<ShareBookDialog>` copy-link with privacy note). New `chapter_reads` join table records per-chapter manual reads; `readingProgress` is preserved unchanged as the "Continue Reading" cursor. `<ReaderPageShell>` client wrapper lifts the shared `readSet` state between `<BookHero>` and `<ChaptersPanel>` so the hero's `Chapters X/Y read` stat updates live when a row toggles. T11's page orchestrator supplements `getPublicBookAction` with sibling fetches for `visibility` + `createdAt` + `updatedAt` + `count(bookComments)` + viewer avatar. T12 deleted 3 legacy `(public)/_components/{chapter-list,comments-panel,social-actions}.tsx` files (307 LOC) after zero-caller audit.
>
> **Smoke fixes round 1 shipped ([738fdd3](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/738fdd3)):** Chris smoke-tested the new reader and flagged 5 trims: (1) top-left `← Editor` / `← Discover` back link removed entirely (app nav handles navigation); (2) `Edit in studio →` removed from BookHero CTA row + `isAuthor` prop dropped + `Pencil` import gone; (3) `<BookTabStrip>` removed from `<ReaderPageShell>` and `book-tab-strip.tsx` deleted (the three-section scroll-spy was pointless with only chapters + comments visible after AboutSection was cut); (4) `<AboutSection>` removed from page and `about-section.tsx` deleted (synopsis in hero already covers it; full-prose duplication below read as filler); (5) `firstPublishedAt` + `lastUpdatedAt` dates moved into the hero as a small mono uppercase meta line between the 4-stat row and synopsis (visually parallel to the series line — `Published Mar 14, 2026 · Updated Apr 2, 2026`). 2 files deleted, 3 files edited. 427/427 tests, tsc clean.

> **Smoke fix round 2 shipped ([6cf8f53](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/6cf8f53)):** Chris flagged "the page is missing the top navbar". Root cause was app-wide, not reader-specific — `app/[locale]/(public)/layout.tsx` was a bare `<>{children}</>` fragment, so any (public) route (book reader, `/discover`, etc.) lost the top nav for authed users. Fix mirrors the (app) layout chrome (`min-h-screen bg-[#262728] flex flex-col` + `<AppNav>`) but DROPS the auth gate so guests can still browse PUBLIC books. AppNav renders only when `session?.user` is truthy — guests get the bare page (a guest-mode nav with Sign in / Sign up CTAs is a follow-up if it becomes worth it). One file changed: `app/[locale]/(public)/layout.tsx`. tsc clean.
>
> **Load-bearing pattern caught here:** any new layout in a non-(app) route group MUST decide between (a) gating on session (like the (app) layout) or (b) rendering nav conditionally when authed (like this fix). The bare-fragment (public) layout was a hole that hid the missing-nav bug on `/discover` for the entire prior phase.

> **Claude Design port shipped ([3c5da20](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/3c5da20)):** Chris ran the reader page through Claude Design (claude.ai/design), got back an HTML/CSS mockup (`design-import-temp/reader-page.html`, 1222 LOC inlined), and asked for a mechanical port — preserve every server action + handler + optimistic state, change visuals only. Single commit covers all 6 files (one new server action + 5 component rewrites). 427/427 tests, tsc clean. The new visual layer: **(a) BookHero** — 4-stat tile strip with brand-yellow icon+labels (NEW sanctioned brand role — 13th in the design-system map, confirmed by Chris); bigger 40px Comfortaa title; tag pills with `#` prefix; Bookmark drops its text label and becomes icon-only (44×44 square); cover gets a bee-hex placeholder artwork when `coverUrl` is null (porting design HTML lines 769-784 verbatim with `Book N` eyebrow conditional on `seriesNumber`); **NEW Read more / Show less expand** on the synopsis paragraph via `useRef` + `useLayoutEffect` measuring `scrollHeight > clientHeight + 2` to detect actual truncation (button hides entirely when paragraph fits within 3 lines, stays visible after expand-then-collapse). **(b) ChaptersPanel** — segmented 12-pill brand-yellow progress rail in a recessed track (replaces smooth-fill bar); tile rows get a `-1px` hover lift via `onMouseEnter` / `onMouseLeave` inline-style mutation (Tailwind can't combine `translateY` + box-shadow on hover cleanly; flag if it ever feels janky); locked chapters get dashed-border + lock-icon styling (was italic muted). **(c) CommentsPanel** — comment rows separated by `border-top: 1px solid var(--canvas-dark-300)` hairlines instead of gaps; 36px brand-gradient avatar for the composer; per-comment avatar fallback is a deterministic hash → 5 oklch-hue gradients + 2-char initials. **(d) SeriesFooter widened to dual-mode** — series mode (existing prev/next 2-col) when neighbors exist; **NEW standalone mode** = single right-aligned "More by @author" tile when book has no series AND author has another public+discoverable book; both null → footer renders nothing. **(e) Body grain texture** — `body::before` radial-dot pattern, scoped to the page (not global CSS), fixed-positioned with `z-0`, main content sits at `z-10`.
>
> **New server action shipped:** `getMoreByAuthorAction(authorUserId, excludeBookId)` in `lib/actions/discover.actions.ts` returns the author's most-recently-updated public+discoverable book, excluding the current one (or null when none exists). Queried in the page orchestrator's `Promise.all`, passed to `<SeriesFooter>` as the new `moreByAuthor` prop. No DB / schema changes, no auth gate (underlying book is already PUBLIC+discoverable).
>
> **Design system update:** brand-yellow stat labels (inside the 4-stat tile strip) are the 13th sanctioned brand-yellow role. Update the design-system map in AGENTS.md if a future epic needs to reference the canonical list.
>
> **Patterns now load-bearing:** (1) When porting Claude Design HTML to React, the design's inline-CSS variables map 1:1 to `app/globals.css` `:root` tokens — zero token translation needed since the mockup uses the same names. (2) `useLayoutEffect` + ref measuring `scrollHeight > clientHeight + small fudge` is the canonical pattern for "show Read more only if text actually truncates" — handles long single words and font-metric variance better than a `synopsis.length > N` char heuristic. (3) Dual-mode footers (series vs standalone) should branch in the component, not in the page orchestrator — the orchestrator fetches both data inputs unconditionally and the component picks which mode to render.
>
> **Last commit:** [3c5da20](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/3c5da20) — feat(reader): port Claude Design mockup to reader page.
>
> **Next concrete step:** Chris continues smoking `/en/books/[bookId]` and flags the next batch of issues. Round 1 (the 5 trims above) is live. Remaining T13 smoke scenarios from the original 17-item checklist below still apply for whatever survived the trim (the BookTabStrip + AboutSection rows are now moot). Boot `npm run dev` and walk each one:
>
> 1. **Logged-in author preview** — visit `/en/books/<my-own-book-id>` while signed in as the author. Confirm: `← Editor` back link · hero shows cover + title brand-yellow + `by @me` clickable + series line if set + genre/privacy pills + 4-stat row · synopsis `line-clamp-3` · `Start Reading →` or `Continue Reading →` CTA · `♥ Favorite` toggles · `↗ Share` opens dialog · `Edit in studio →` link present.
> 2. **Share dialog copy flow** — click Share, click Copy. Confirm: `Copy` flips to `Copied ✓` for 2s, sonner toast `Link copied`, URL field contains `${origin}/${locale}/books/${bookId}`.
> 3. **Privacy notes** — flip book to PRIVATE via Details Sharing, reload, open Share. Confirm: note reads `Only people you invite can open this link.` with `Lock` icon. Flip to FRIENDS, reload, Share. Confirm: `Only your friends can open this link.` with `Users` icon. Flip back to PUBLIC.
> 4. **Logged-out viewer on PUBLIC book** — incognito. Confirm: `← Discover` back link · 4-stat `Chapters` shows just total (no `X / Y read`) · chapter rows have no checkbox · clicking Favorite/Bookmark fires sign-in toast · Comments shows `Sign in to leave a comment.` link.
> 5. **PRIVATE access denied** — incognito on a PRIVATE book. Confirm: AccessDenied screen on `#262728` bg with brand-yellow `Lock` icon + `← Discover` CTA.
> 6. **FRIENDS access denied** — same as 5 but a FRIENDS book. Confirm: `Users` icon + friends copy.
> 7. **Mark-as-read toggle** — authed reader on a public book. Click an empty circle on a chapter row. Confirm: flips to brand-yellow `CheckCircle2` optimistically, hero `Chapters X/Y` increments by 1, progress bar fills. Reload — state persists.
> 8. **Unmark** — click a filled checkmark. Confirm: flips back to empty, hero stat decrements, progress bar shrinks. Reload — state persists.
> 9. **Toggle middle-chapter** — mark chapter 3 read while 1, 2, 4 are unread. Confirm: only chapter 3 is marked (not 1+2+3). This is the **key behavioral change** vs the old linear cursor model — proves the `chapter_reads` switch landed correctly.
> 10. **Tab strip scroll-spy** — scroll the page from top. Confirm: as the Chapters section header crosses the top, `Chapters` pill highlights brand-yellow. Scroll past it; `Comments` highlights. Past Comments; `About` highlights. Click each pill from the bottom; page smooth-scrolls to that section.
> 11. **Tab strip stickiness** — scroll past the hero. Confirm: the tab strip stays pinned at top of the viewport, no content bleed-through.
> 12. **Comment composer + Load more** — post a comment as authed user. Confirm: appears at top of list optimistically, count bumps. If there are >20 comments, click `Load more`. Confirm: next page appends, button disappears at end.
> 13. **Locked chapters for non-author** — view a book with FIRST_DRAFT chapters as a non-author. Confirm: those rows render `Draft — coming soon` italic, no checkbox, not clickable.
> 14. **Empty-state branches** — view a book with zero chapters as the author. Confirm: ChaptersPanel shows `No chapters yet.` italic.
> 15. **Author auto-mark still works on chapter reader** — visit `/en/books/<id>/read/<chapterId>` as the author. Return to the overview. Confirm: that chapter is now marked read (the auto-mark in the chapter reader page still fires; proves T2 still upserts both `readingProgress` AND `chapter_reads`).
> 16. **Series footer** — view a book in a series with at least one neighbor. Confirm: prev/next links render in the re-skinned card; click navigates correctly.
> 17. **No black backgrounds** — sample 6 places (hero, tab strip, chapters card, comments card, about card, series footer). Confirm: none use `#000` or `#141414`. All use `#262728` or the panel/tile gradients.
>
> If any item fails: file a `fix(reader): ...` follow-up commit before fully shipping. If all 17 pass, the epic is closed.

(c) active toolbar button's icon flipped from yellow-on-yellow to dark-on-yellow (root cause: Tailwind arbitrary value `text-[var(--brand-ink)]` wasn't being honored; fix uses inline `color: '#1a1a1a'` on `tbtnStyle` when active so it wins over any class); (f) binder row grip handle (`GripVertical`) repositioned from `left-0` (sat on the row's rounded border edge) to `left: ${16 + depth * 12}px` so it lands just-left-of the type icon, depth-aware; (g) **Status bar moved out of the editor `<main>` and into the right column** as a separate styled card above metadata/history — always visible (handles "No chapter selected" state via `wordCount=0` + italic placeholder), 3-section utility layout (Row 1: save dot + label / word count + small `words` suffix; Row 2: `Word goal` label, percent + `of N`, brand-yellow progress track on inset rail; Row 3: `Sprint` label + `SprintControls`), sections divided by `--br-card` hairlines, labels brightened from `--canvas-dark-ink-muted` to `--canvas-dark-ink-strong` + `font-semibold` for visibility; (h) right column now scrolls correctly: `right-panel-slot.tsx` wrapper is `h-full min-h-0 flex flex-col gap-2`; `MetadataPanel` + `VersionHistoryDrawer` roots switched from `flex-shrink-0` to `flex-1 min-h-0` so their internal `overflow-y-auto` boxes are bounded (previously the scene-planner expansion pushed Notes off-screen with no way to reach them).

Provider's `setActiveItemId` resets `wordCount` to 0 on null/non-chapter selection and seeds it from chapter cache (or fetch result) when switching chapters, so the displayed count isn't stale across chapter switches.

Pure recursive ProseMirror JSON walker — no `@tiptap/pm` runtime import. Finds contiguous run of text nodes carrying the target hiveSuggestionMark id, replaces with a single new text node carrying `replacementText`; non-hiveSuggestion sibling marks (bold/italic) from the first removed node carry onto the replacement; empty-marks array gets dropped. Orphan short-circuits with `found: false` returning input doc by reference. Multi-block: only the first block's run is replaced (subsequent blocks left untouched via a `found` flag). Drifted-mark handling is automatic — function operates on the mark's CURRENT range in the doc, not on any external `selection_start`. 7/7 tests (plain replacement, multi-text coalesce, bold inheritance, bold+italic inheritance, orphan, drifted, multi-block). tsc fully clean.
