No tests (presentational components, will be exercised E2E by T12/T13). **NOT yet wired into chapter-editor.tsx** — T12 does that. **T11 collaboration gutter shipped (`3c14f1e`):** 7 files under `components/hive/collab/` + `lib/hooks/use-collab-data.ts` (plus an inline `rel-time.ts` helper — codebase had no shared module). `useCollabData({ chapterId, hiveId })` Promise.alls `getChapterAnnotationsAction` + `getChapterSuggestionsAction`, exposes `refresh` + `mutate.{replyToAnnotation, resolveAnnotation, unresolveAnnotation, replyToSuggestion, acceptSuggestion, rejectSuggestion}`. `<CollaborationGutter>` is 320px expanded / 32px collapsed (controlled by parent via `collapsed` + `onToggleCollapse` props). Cards positioned absolutely via `editor.view.coordsAtPos(row.selectionStart)` inside a `useLayoutEffect` (recomputed on update/selectionUpdate/resize/scroll, try/catch around the call, gutter-rect normalization, ESTIMATED_HEIGHT=84px collision-avoidance heuristic for dense ranges). Flow-layout fallback when editor is null. `<GutterFilterStrip>` 8 pills (All + 5 layers + Suggestions + Resolved), multi-select, localStorage key `collab:filter:<chapterId>` (auth-scoped page so no userId needed). AnnotationCard: paper-100 chrome + per-layer left-edge border (inline-oklch from T3); collapsed shows 2-line line-clamped body + reply count chip; expanded shows full body + chronological replies + reply input; resolve check button visible when `canResolveAnnotation(annotation, role, viewerId, bookOwnerId)`. SuggestionCard: warm-gold border; collapsed shows strikethrough originalExcerpt → suggestedText; Accept/Reject buttons gated by `canReviewSuggestion`; Accept calls `onAcceptedSuggestion` prop (T12 wires to bump `chapterContentVersion`); orphan-accept payload triggers "Suggestion was orphaned" toast. OrphanSection footer pill expands a list with Dismiss buttons. **Action return shape clarification for future consumers:** T6/T7 list actions return `author: { userId, username, avatarUrl, displayName }` and `resolver: {...} | null` as NESTED objects, not flat fields — read `annotation.author.username ?? annotation.author.displayName ?? 'Unknown'`. Replies live in the SAME flat array as parents (parent rows have `parentId: null`); group at the gutter level via `useMemo`; only render top-level rows; replies render inside parent card. Orphan ids only apply to top-level rows. **Action signature inconsistencies caught:** `getChapterAnnotationsAction(chapterId, hiveId)` is positional but `getChapterSuggestionsAction({chapterId, hiveId})` is object — hook adapts. No new shadcn primitives needed. tsc clean. No tests (presentational + thin hook; T20 smoke covers behavior). **T12 studio editor wiring shipped (`2849c88`):** `HiveAnnotationMark` + `HiveSuggestionMark` registered unconditionally in `chapter-editor.tsx`'s TipTap extensions array (safe for non-hive books). `useEditor` keyed on `[activeItemId, chapterContentVersion]` so accept-suggestion success → `reloadActiveChapter().then(() => bumpChapterContentVersion())` → editor recreates with server-fresh content. `BookEditorProvider` context extended with 4 new fields: `chapterContentVersion`, `bumpChapterContentVersion`, `gutterOpen`, `toggleGutter`. **Mutual exclusion** between `gutterOpen` and `historyOpen` wired in BOTH directions (opening either closes the other). **`currentUserId: string | null` added to provider** (threaded from page.tsx via session) — collab UI gates on truthy currentUserId. New VIEW-zone toolbar button (lucide MessagesSquare in `editor-toolbar.tsx`, aria-pressed, gated on `bookHive !== null`). **Layout decision: option (b)** — gutter mounts as a sibling flex column inside chapter-editor's `flex-1 overflow-hidden` container, NOT via `right-panel-slot.tsx` (which stays metadata-or-history). Keeps the TipTap editor instance local (no need to thread it through context) and the gutter's `coordsAtPos` anchoring stays accurate. `right-panel-slot.tsx` got a comment-only update documenting the decision. When gutter + analysis are both open, the chapter pane is 4 columns (editor + gutter + writing-analysis + metadata) — acceptable for now, further squeeze is a follow-up. Studio editor `viewer` constant: `{ id: currentUserId, role: 'OWNER', bookOwnerId: currentUserId }` — book author IS implicitly OWNER for permission purposes; canResolveAnnotation's book-owner bypass returns true regardless. `activeChapter.id` (chapters PK, not binderItem id) is what gets passed to gutter + popover. tsc clean. **T13 hive chapter view shipped (`b57090f`):** New `getHiveChapterView(hiveId, chapterId)` server action (member-scoped, cross-hive escape guard via `chapter.bookId === hive.bookId`, joins binderItems for title since chapters table has no title column, joins userProfiles for chapter author + book owner). Page at `/hive/[hiveId]/chapters/[chapterId]` + `<HiveChapterSurface>` client component: read-only TipTap (`editable=false`) with both H3 marks registered, scoped `[data-slot="hive-chapter-pane"] .ProseMirror` styling (Newsreader + brand-yellow underlines for annotation/suggestion marks since this lives on dark walnut canvas not cream paper). Mounted `<SelectionPopover>` (selectionUpdate still fires in read-only mode) + `<CollaborationGutter>`. Header byline variant: "Written by @submitter — chapter contribution to {book} by @owner" when `chapter.authorUserId !== null AND !== book.userId`. **Known caveat:** `router.refresh()` doesn't actually re-mount client surfaces in Next 16, so the gutter's internal `useCollabData` may not auto-refetch after a new annotation is created from this page. Worth wiring a real `refresh-nonce` prop on `<CollaborationGutter>` if smoke shows missed updates. **T14 public reader sub-byline + H3 marks shipped (`1dfb504`):** Reader's chapter projection extended with `authorUserId` + leftJoin(userProfiles) for both chapter contributor + book owner. New `<ChapterContributionByline>` renders between title + prose, only when chapter has a non-null authorUserId distinct from book.userId. **Public reader doesn't use TipTap's `generateHTML` or `useEditor`** — it has a custom server-side serializer at `lib/export/tiptap-to-html.ts` (switch on node + mark types → emit HTML strings → dangerouslySetInnerHTML). "Registering" the H3 marks meant extending the serializer's mark switch with `hiveAnnotation` + `hiveSuggestion` cases. `.public-reader` wrapper added around the prose div so T3's `.public-reader .hive-annotation { background: transparent; border-bottom: none }` reset hides marks for logged-out viewers. tsc clean across both. **T15+T16+T17 submissions UI shipped (`6e32e03`):** Combined commit for the full submissions sub-feature (3 pages + 5 components + new `getHiveChapterListAction` helper in hive-content.actions.ts — 10 files, +1211 LOC). `/hive/[hiveId]/submissions` list with 3 role-scoped sections (My drafts / My submissions / All in this hive, last gated on `canReviewSubmissions`). `+ New Submission` CTA disabled with tooltip when `!canSubmitChapter`. Status pills use `--status-idea / warning / success / error` tokens. `/hive/[hiveId]/submissions/new` composer (server-redirects BETA_READER to list per `canSubmitChapter`) — sheet-style with title contenteditable + target-order native `<select>` + StarterKit TipTap (no H3 marks — drafts don't carry annotation/suggestion marks) + 800ms debounced auto-save via `saveSubmissionDraftAction`. **Auto-save → router.replace dance** in 'new' mode: first save returns the new id, stash via `submissionIdRef`, then `router.replace` to `/submissions/[id]` so future saves carry the id (editor instance survives the URL change because Next preserves the client component tree when the route renders the same shape). Submit button: flush pending save → `submitSubmissionAction({id})` → toast + push to canonical URL. `/hive/[hiveId]/submissions/[submissionId]` dynamic page branches: DRAFT+owner → SubmissionComposer (mode=existing); PENDING+canReview → SubmissionReview (Approve/Reject CTAs, approve routes to `/studio/[bookId]` simple route per plan); else → SubmissionRead (read-only with status-aware metadata — APPROVED links to public reader via `createdChapterId`, REJECTED shows review-note). Reject opens ConfirmDialog with required reviewNote textarea. SubmissionMetaHeader + body styles factored into `submission-shared.tsx`. `getSubmissionAction` is positional `(id: string)` (not `{id}`) — adapted at call site. **T18 + T19 dispatched in parallel** (suggestions bulk-review + discussions list/thread/compose) — separate routes, no file overlap. Waiting on completion. **Next concrete step:** monitor T18/T19 completion, then T20 activity event wiring audit (in-tx event firing + payload comments) and T21 final AGENTS.md write-up + ship. After T21, Chris runs the manual smoke checklist (will be provided at handoff).

> **The audit** is a 6-sub-project effort to make the book editor at
> `/[locale]/studio/[bookId]` fully operational.
>
> 1. ~~**SP1 Stability Pass**~~ DONE.
> 2. ~~**SP2 Binder UX**~~ DONE.
> 3. ~~**SP3 Specialized Editors**~~ DONE — Front/Back Matter, Outline, Research notes.
> 4. ~~**SP4 Toolbar + modes**~~ DONE — ambient sounds removed; lucide icons; three-zone Format/Status/View; semantic tokens; Cmd+F/Cmd+S scoped to editor; editor light-mode toggle; studio columns fill viewport.
> 5. ~~**SP5 Metadata + persistence**~~ DONE — bottom status bar (save indicator + word count + inline-editable word goal); word goal moved to `chapters.wordGoal` DB column with lazy migration helper; Publishing details labeled as book-level; Scene Planner hidden on Front/Back Matter.
> 6. ~~**SP6 New surfaces**~~ **DONE** (2026-05-26) — Snapshot UI (right-side drawer, preview-then-confirm restore, premium-gated with upsell); aria-labels on all icon-only editor buttons; `?` keyboard cheatsheet modal (Ctrl+/ + Help button trigger). Mobile/tablet responsive deferred — Claude Design pass will repaint the studio. 119/119 tests, tsc clean.
>
> All six audit sub-projects complete. **Next: Claude Design visual pass**, then Phase 8 (Stripe monetization) resumes.
>
> **Chris's working preferences (confirmed across SP1–SP4):**
> - Commits go straight to `main`, no feature branches.
> - Per-task manual verification (don't batch).
> - Subagent-driven execution preserves context window.
> - Push to GitHub when asked.
>
> **Bug-fix posture:** the global error boundary at `app/[locale]/error.tsx` logs errors with stack + message + digest. Always start with the console error before guessing causes.
>
> **Specialized-editor pattern (now load-bearing):** FM/BM, Outline, and Notes all use `binderItems.content` jsonb + a render-branch in `chapter-editor.tsx`'s `!isChapterType` block. New specialized editors for other types should follow the same shape.
>
> **SP4 light-mode gotcha:** `[data-editor-theme="light"]` rules in `globals.css` didn't apply to descendants (root cause unclear — other rules in the same file work). The working approach: inline styles on the wrapper in `corkboard-or-editor.tsx` + a React-injected `<style>` tag in the same file. Anything that needs to flip per editor theme should be added inside that `<style>` tag.
>
> **Studio layout note:** the studio page's outer flex now uses `h-[calc(100vh-56px)]` (nav is `h-14`) instead of `h-full`, because the parent `(app)/layout.tsx` uses `min-h-screen` not `h-screen`. Other (app) routes are unaffected.
>
> **SP5 word-goal pattern:** word goal lives on `chapters.wordGoal` (int, default 0 = "no goal"). The bottom status bar (`editor-status-bar.tsx`) owns the UI; `lib/word-goal-migration.ts` is a pure helper that ports pre-SP5 `wcg:<binderItemId>` localStorage keys to DB on first chapter load, then deletes the key. Future per-chapter settings should follow the same shape (DB column + chapter action + status-bar inline edit) rather than localStorage.
>
> **SP6 snapshot-preview pattern:** `previewSnapshotId` in the provider gates `updateChapterContent` so autosave can't clobber the live draft while previewing a snapshot. Exit re-renders live content via a `wasPreviewingRef` so typing isn't reset every keystroke.
>
> **SP6 right-panel slot pattern:** `RightPanelSlot` client component (in `_components/right-panel-slot.tsx`) switches between `MetadataPanel` and `VersionHistoryDrawer` based on `historyOpen`. Future right-side overlays should follow the same shape.
>
> **SP6 cheatsheet trigger gotcha:** Bare `?` doesn't work in writing apps because the editor surface is contenteditable and `?` types into prose. We use Ctrl+/ (modifier) + a Help button (HelpCircle) in the toolbar that dispatches a `beehive:toggle-cheatsheet` custom event the modal listens for.
>
> **SP6 dev premium override:** Set `DEV_FORCE_PREMIUM=true` in `.env.local` to simulate premium status without modifying `userBilling`. Guarded to `NODE_ENV !== 'production'`. See `lib/premium.ts`.
>
> **Community/Discover boundary:** /community lives in (app) — authenticated-only personal feed. /discover lives in (public) — unauthenticated browsing. Same data (books, sparks, hives) shown differently. Don't duplicate features across both surfaces — Community shows YOUR follows' activity; Discover shows everything.
>
> **DP2 design-port pattern:** Studio chrome ported surface-by-surface (status bar → binder → metadata → toolbar → editor body → audit). Brand yellow restrained to 5 sanctioned uses across the touched surfaces. Sprint timer relocated from floating overlay into the bottom status bar's right cluster (resolves a live overlap bug). Newsreader serif wired as the prose face; container max-width 720px; light mode flips body to paper-ink (not paper-ink-strong) for long-prose readability.
>
> **DP3 specialized-surfaces pattern:** Non-chapter binder items each get their own renderer. FM/BM uses WYSIWYG inline-edit page previews (5 subtypes) with shared `PageWrapper` chrome — book pages always cream paper, surrounding pane theme-aware via `--sheet-canvas`. Outline replaces Kanban with vertical sortable beat-sheet; legacy `{columns, cards}` data flattened at render time via `readBeats()`. Character uses sheet-style with theme-aware ink (paper-ink-strong on cream in light mode for crisper readability). Notes restyled in-place; ruled-paper background lines removed per Chris. Generic textarea fallback removed — every binder type has a specialized renderer.
>
> **DP1 design-port pattern:** Claude Design's tokens.css ported into `app/globals.css` `:root` as oklch primitives (chrome/paper/canvas scales, status, type colors). Shadcn semantic tokens (`--card`, `--background`, etc.) bridge to the new chrome scale so existing components inherit walnut automatically. The SP4 light-mode workaround in `corkboard-or-editor.tsx` references `--paper-*` tokens directly. Source of truth for future updates: `designs/claude/studio-shell/tokens.css`. The bonus pages (Landing / Sign In / Sign Up) Claude Design produced separately are deferred.
>
> **DP4 overlays/modes/modals pattern:** Transient surfaces ported. Two new shared components: ConfirmDialog (built on shadcn Dialog primitive — used for destructive flows like binder delete) and EmptyState (studio-scoped, theme-aware via `onEditorCanvas` prop). Modals (cheatsheet/export/sprint-setup), overlays (find/replace), drawers (history), banners (preview), and panels (writing analysis) each got the new visual treatment with theme-aware ink where they cover the editor canvas. Corkboard pixel-perfect with paper index-cards + alternating ±1° rotation on warm desk-surface bg. Focus mode gained 200ms width/translate/opacity transitions. Sprint finished plays a one-time CSS pulse-glow (`@keyframes sprintFinished` in globals.css).
>
> **Light-mode editor default (2026-05-26):** Editor theme defaults to `light` (cream paper) for all new sessions. Users with `localStorage['editor-theme'] === 'dark'` keep dark mode. The change reflects the on-brand "writer's desk by day" experience the Claude Design pass established. Dark mode remains accessible via the toolbar Moon icon.
>
> **Studio Library pattern:** `/[locale]/studio` is the user's bookshelf — Continue-Writing hero + 3-tile vertical stats stack header (spines/sparkline/ring viz per tile), search + sort + view-switch + filter chips, paper-warm book cards with status stripe + always-visible info band (no hover overlay). Hero/stats data via `getStudioStatsAction`; book projection extended with `summarizeBookStatus()` helper that rolls chapter statuses up to a single book label (Drafting/Revised/Published). Same helper drives card status pills AND filter chip counts so they stay in sync.
>
> **Library v2 design pattern (2026-05-27):** Page bg locked to `#1E1E1E`; lifted surfaces use `--canvas-dark-100/150/200/300` directly via inline `style={{ background: 'var(--canvas-dark-100)' }}` (chrome shadcn-bridge isn't pixel-perfect for this page). Brand yellow restrained to 4 uses: hero Resume CTA + active filter chip + hover title accent + empty-state Start CTA. Status colors variate the page via per-card stripes + per-tile accents. AppNav now owns the only chrome on the page — no page-level top bar. JetBrains Mono added via `next/font/google` → `--font-jetbrains-mono` → wired into `--font-mono`. `--canvas-dark-150` added to globals.css as the search-focus surface tone.
>

**When you finish meaningful work in a session** (any commit, any phase progress, any decision the user agreed to):
1. Update the "📍 Resume Here" block: bump `Last updated`, refresh `Current focus`, `Last commit`, and `Next concrete step`.
2. If a phase completed, move it from "What's Next" into "What Has Been Built" with the same level of detail as existing phases.
3. If new patterns / file conventions / gotchas emerged, add them under "Key Patterns".
4. Commit the doc update **with** the code change, not as a separate commit.

This file is the handoff contract. If "read AGENTS.md and continue project" doesn't get the next session to the right spot, this file failed.

## 🎨 Design System (load-bearing — read this for any new UI)

The app ships a **dark, iOS-inspired, soft-rounded** aesthetic. Locked design spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-editor-aesthetic-refresh-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-editor-aesthetic-refresh-design.md). Read it before touching ANY chrome surface, new or old.

**TL;DR for new components:**
- **Tokens** are all in `app/globals.css` `:root`. Never introduce new ones unless the spec gets amended.
   - Cool-gray chrome scale: `--canvas-dark-100/-150/-200/-250/-300/-350/-400` (light at top, dark at base).
   - Ink: `--canvas-dark-ink-muted` (secondary), `--canvas-dark-ink` (body), `--canvas-dark-ink-strong` (emphasized).
   - Brand: `--brand` (yellow), `--brand-ink` (dark text on brand).
   - Paper (cream): `--paper-100/-200/-300` + `--paper-ink/-strong/-muted` — **studio chapter editor only**, nowhere else.
- **Radius**: `--r-card` 20px (panels), `--r-row` 14px (rows/inputs), `--r-btn` 12px (square buttons), `--r-pill` 999px (pills).
- **Depth** (every panel): vertical gradient + `--br-card` hairline top highlight + `--sh-card` multi-layer shadow.
   - Panel bg: `linear-gradient(180deg, var(--canvas-dark-250), var(--canvas-dark-200))`.
   - Tile bg (rows / buttons): `linear-gradient(180deg, var(--canvas-dark-350), var(--canvas-dark-300))` + `--sh-tile`.
   - Inputs: flat `var(--canvas-dark-100)` + `--sh-inset` for recessed feel.
- **Brand-yellow usage** is RESTRAINED — 12-place map in the spec. Use for headings (h1/h2/h3 + panel titles), active states, premium badge, progress fills, save indicator dot, + Add tile text. NEVER for chrome borders, hover states, neutral text.
- **Pure black is BANNED** from chrome. Darkest legitimate surface = `--canvas-dark-100`.
- **Cream paper** appears in exactly ONE place: the studio chapter editor's ProseMirror surface. Wiki entries, hive chapter view, submission composer, character/notes/outline panels, and every other prose-adjacent surface uses dark walnut chrome + scoped `[data-slot="X"] .ProseMirror { color: var(--canvas-dark-ink) }` overrides.
- **shadcn `Dialog` primitive** already carries the chrome — any new modal automatically inherits. Don't re-skin the primitive.
- **Headings** use Comfortaa (font-bold, brand color). Body uses Geist. Prose uses Newsreader. Labels use JetBrains Mono uppercase tracking-wider muted.
- **Light mode in the editor surface only**: cream paper with `[data-editor-theme="light"]` scoped overrides in `corkboard-or-editor.tsx`. Light-mode rules must use `!important` to win over inline-style gradients.
- **Active nav / active row treatment**: gradient + tile shadow only. NO left brand-yellow stripe (per Chris's smoke feedback).

The detailed shipping history is in two "What Has Been Built" entries: **Studio Editor Aesthetic Refresh** + **Hive Routes Aesthetic Refresh**. Read those for surface-specific patterns and known follow-ups.

---

## What This Is

Beehive Studio is a solo-developer writing platform: rich-text book editor, Hive collaboration groups, and a community discovery feed. Dark-only, bee-themed. Built with Next.js 16 App Router, React 19, TypeScript, Tailwind v4, shadcn/ui (New York style), Drizzle ORM on Neon Postgres.

## What Has Been Built

### Public Book Reader Page Redesign ✅ COMPLETE (2026-06-02)

Overhauls the public book overview page at `/[locale]/books/[bookId]` from a cramped 2-col layout on `bg-[#141414]` (160px cover hero + chapters-left/comments-right grid) into a centered `max-w-5xl` stacked-section page on `bg-[#262728]` matching the iOS-inspired design system established by the editor + hive aesthetic refreshes. Six new colocated components ship under `app/[locale]/(public)/books/_components/` + the page orchestrator gets a full rewrite + new `chapter_reads` join table replaces the linear-cursor read-derivation with true per-chapter mark-as-read. 13 tasks shipped (T1-T13), 16 commits including 1 carry-forward test fix + 1 mid-task concurrency-bug fix from code review + 1 cleanup commit deleting 307 LOC of superseded legacy components.

Spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-02-public-book-reader-page-redesign-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-02-public-book-reader-page-redesign-design.md). Plan at [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-02-public-book-reader-page-redesign.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-02-public-book-reader-page-redesign.md).

**Task SHAs:** T1 [1983e7e](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/1983e7e) `chapter_reads` schema + idempotent migration runner · T2 [0d7aa71](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/0d7aa71) reading actions reshape + new `unmarkChapterReadAction` · carry-forward [ca51b28](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ca51b28) test fix (dynamic `await import()` was timing out at the 5s vitest default under full-suite parallel load) · T3 [78bb164](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/78bb164) `<ShareBookDialog>` · T4 [2d50497](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/2d50497) `<BookHero>` · T5 [70a7f5f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/70a7f5f) `<BookTabStrip>` · T6 [d7e1983](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/d7e1983) `<ChaptersPanel>` + fix [e1c6797](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/e1c6797) concurrent-rollback bug · T7 [8d8c45f](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/8d8c45f) colocated `<CommentsPanel>` · T8 [cf4ca5a](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/cf4ca5a) `<AboutSection>` · T9 [c37d236](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/c37d236) `<AccessDenied>` chrome refresh · T10 [9d91fe4](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/9d91fe4) `<SeriesFooter>` chrome refresh · T11 [96e7586](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/96e7586) page orchestrator rewrite + new `<ReaderPageShell>` client wrapper · T12 [73b61ea](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/73b61ea) deleted 3 legacy `(public)/_components/{chapter-list,comments-panel,social-actions}.tsx` files (307 LOC).

- **Schema change** (`db/schema/social.ts`, `scripts/migrate-reader-redesign.ts`): new additive `chapter_reads` table — `(userId text NOT NULL CASCADE, bookId text NOT NULL CASCADE, chapterBinderItemId text NOT NULL CASCADE, readAt timestamp DEFAULT now() NOT NULL)`, composite PK `(userId, chapterBinderItemId)`, denorm `bookId` covered by `chapter_reads_user_book_idx`. `readingProgress` is preserved unchanged — it still drives `lastChapterId` for the "Continue Reading" cursor + auto-mark from `/read/[chapterId]`. Migration runner is idempotent via `IF NOT EXISTS` + `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`; ran twice on dev DB to prove idempotency. Two tables → two separate concerns: `chapter_reads` is the read-set truth (per-chapter manual toggle), `readingProgress` is the navigation cursor.
- **Server actions** (`lib/actions/reading.actions.ts`): `markChapterReadAction(bookId, chapterBinderItemId)` — second arg renamed from `chapterId` (the `chapters.id` PK) to `chapterBinderItemId` (the `binderItems.id` PK) so the API matches how reads are tracked everywhere else. Resolves `chapters.id` from `chapters.binderItemId` for the cursor upsert, inserts into `chapter_reads` ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING (idempotent), AND still upserts `readingProgress.chapterId` so the cursor stays warm. New sibling `unmarkChapterReadAction(bookId, chapterBinderItemId)` deletes from `chapter_reads` only — never touches the cursor (read set + cursor are separate concerns; unmarking a chapter shouldn't reset "Continue Reading"). `getReadingProgressAction` return shape unchanged (`{ lastChapterId, readChapterBinderItemIds }`) but `readChapterBinderItemIds` now sourced from `chapter_reads` (was: at-or-before-cursor linear derivation). Auto-mark call site at `app/[locale]/(public)/books/[bookId]/read/[chapterId]/page.tsx:93` updated to pass `current.binderItemId` from the existing `allChapters` projection — no new schema lookup. Surface-shape tests at `lib/actions/__tests__/reading-actions.test.ts` use top-level static `import * as readingActions` (NOT per-test dynamic `await import()` — that pattern times out at the 5s vitest default under full-suite parallel load even though solo runs at ~2.7s pass; lesson encoded in `ca51b28`).
- **`<ShareBookDialog>`** (`books/_components/share-book-dialog.tsx`): wraps shadcn `Dialog` (chrome inherited from the primitive — no new styling work). Props `{ url, visibility: 'PUBLIC'|'FRIENDS'|'PRIVATE', trigger: ReactNode }`. Title `Share this book`, read-only URL `<input>` with focus-select-all + `aria-label="Share URL"`, brand-yellow `Copy` button calls `navigator.clipboard.writeText` → flips to `Copied ✓` for 2s + sonner `toast.success('Link copied')`; catch fires `toast.error('Could not copy link')`. Trigger via `<DialogTrigger asChild>` so the hero supplies its own styled button. Privacy note below URL: `Lock` icon + "Only people you invite can open this link." for PRIVATE; `Users` icon + "Only your friends can open this link." for FRIENDS; no note for PUBLIC. `aria-live="polite"` on the Copy button.
- **`<BookHero>`** (`books/_components/book-hero.tsx` ~230 LOC, client): panel-gradient card, `[grid-template-columns:200px_1fr]` on sm+ (mobile stacks). Left = 200px 2:3 cover img + fallback gradient. Right = title (Comfortaa bold 28px brand-yellow) + author row (24px avatar + `by` + `<Link>` to `/${locale}/u/${authorUsername}` with `displayName ?? @username`) + series line (mono uppercase, both `Book N of <italic>` and `Part of <italic>` branches) + pill row (genre conditional · privacy pill always with `Globe`/`Lock`/`Users` lucide icon via `VISIBILITY_META` record · `tags?.slice(0, 5)`) + 4-stat row (`Chapters` shows `${readCount} / ${totalChapters}` only when authed AND total > 0, else just total; `Words` humanized to `Xk` ≥1000; `Likes` live; `Comments`) + `line-clamp-3 max-w-xl` synopsis + CTA row (left to right: brand-pill `Start/Continue Reading →` Link using `continueReadingHref ?? startReadingHref` precedence with label conditional on continue truthiness, renders nothing when both null; `Favorite` tile button with Heart icon + `aria-pressed` + optimistic flip of BOTH `liked` AND `likeCount` with full rollback on action failure + sonner error toast; Share button as `<ShareBookDialog>` trigger via `asChild`; `Bookmark` tile button same optimistic shape; `Edit in studio →` text link to `/${locale}/studio/${book.id}` gated on `isAuthor`). Guest clicks on Favorite/Bookmark fire `toast.info('Sign in to ...')` early-return — intentional UX shift from legacy that hid buttons entirely. Heart/Bookmark active-state: `fill-current text-[var(--brand)]` (lucide pattern). Props widen `PublicBook & { visibility: 'PUBLIC'|'FRIENDS'|'PRIVATE'; commentCount: number }` because `getPublicBookAction` doesn't return those two — page orchestrator fills via sibling fetches.
- **`<BookTabStrip>`** (`books/_components/book-tab-strip.tsx` ~60 LOC, client, no props): `<nav aria-label="Book sections">` with three `<a>` pills (Chapters/Comments/About) — `position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 10` with the panel chrome inline style. IntersectionObserver with `rootMargin: '-80px 0px -60% 0px'` and threshold `[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1]` watches `#chapters`/`#comments`/`#about` section roots by id — most-intersecting (highest ratio) sets the active pill. Active pill: `bg-[var(--brand)] text-[var(--brand-ink)] font-semibold` + `aria-current="page"`. Click handler `e.preventDefault()` + `getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' })`. `TabId` type derived via `(typeof TABS)[number]['id']` so const + runtime narrowing stay in sync.
- **`<ChaptersPanel>`** (`books/_components/chapters-panel.tsx` ~150 LOC, client): root `<section id="chapters" className="scroll-mt-20 ..." style={panel chrome}>`. Header: Comfortaa bold brand-yellow `Chapters` + `{readCount} / {totalCount} read` subtitle (gated `isAuthenticated && totalCount > 0`); thin brand-yellow progress bar below on inset rail (`var(--canvas-dark-100)` + `var(--sh-inset)`), same gate. Author sees all chapters; non-author filters via `isChapterReaderVisible(status)` (REVISED or FINAL only). Empty state: italic muted "No chapters yet." Rows are tile-gradient `<li>`s with: mark-as-read toggle (`<CheckCircle2 text-[var(--brand)]>` when read or `<Circle text-[var(--canvas-dark-ink-muted)]>`; `h-5 w-5` spacer span for guests + locked rows to preserve column alignment; `aria-pressed` + `aria-label`); 1-based index; title cell (`<Link>` to `${readerBasePath}/read/${chapterId}` when reader-visible, italic muted `<span>` when locked); right meta (`Updated Mon DD` from `toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })` when reader-visible, `Draft — coming soon` uppercase tracking-wider when locked). **No per-row word count** (book total lives in hero stats). Guest click on toggle fires `toast.info('Sign in to track your progress')` and does NOT call the server action. `onReadSetChange?: (next: Set<string>) => void` prop fires on every toggle (optimistic flip AND rollback) so `<ReaderPageShell>` lifts the read-set into the hero's `Chapters X/Y read` stat.
- **`<CommentsPanel>`** (`books/_components/comments-panel.tsx` ~150 LOC, client): root `<section id="comments" scroll-mt-20 ...>`. Header: Comfortaa bold brand-yellow `Comments` + count subtitle. Composer (authed): viewer avatar + recessed `<textarea>` (`--canvas-dark-100` bg + `--sh-inset` boxShadow + `--r-row`, `rows={2}`, `maxLength={1000}`) + right-aligned brand-yellow `Post` button with `disabled={isPending || !draft.trim()}`. Guest fallback: muted line with `<Link>` to `/${locale}/sign-in` (`Sign in` brand-yellow). Empty state: italic muted `No comments yet.` Comment rows: avatar + clickable `@username` (or displayName fallback or `Anonymous`) Link to `/${locale}/u/${authorUsername}` + `relTime` muted + body with `whitespace-pre-wrap`. `Load more` tile-button calls `getBookCommentsAction(bookId, page+1)` inside `startTransition`. Post is optimistic: clears draft on submit, restores it + `toast.error('Could not post comment')` on failure. No threading, no per-comment likes (v1).
- **`<AboutSection>`** (`books/_components/about-section.tsx` ~95 LOC, pure SERVER component, no `'use client'`): root `<section id="about" scroll-mt-20 ...>`. Three blocks: (1) Synopsis non-null splits on `/\n\n+/` into `<p>` paragraphs inside `max-w-prose space-y-3`; null renders italic muted `The author hasn't written a description yet.` with `&apos;` JSX escape. (2) 2-col `<dl>` metadata grid with uppercase `text-[10px]` labels (`First published` / `Last updated`) + strong-ink values formatted via `fmt(d: Date | string)` helper using `toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })` (e.g. `March 14, 2026`). Helper handles string OR Date to survive Next.js's server→client Date serialization. (3) Tile-gradient author card with 48px avatar + identity column (truncate Comfortaa display name with `displayName ?? username ?? 'Unknown'` fallback chain + truncate mono `@username` line gated on username) + brand-yellow `View profile →` `<Link>` with `<ArrowRight>` lucide gated on username (no broken `/u/null` href).
- **`<AccessDenied>`** chrome refresh (`books/_components/access-denied.tsx`): Props unchanged (`{ reason: 'PRIVATE' | 'FRIENDS_ONLY', locale: string }`); pure chrome. Page backdrop flipped `bg-[#141414]` → `bg-[#262728]`. Centered `max-w-md` card with panel chrome; tile-gradient `h-14 w-14` rounded-full icon chip with brand-yellow lucide icon (`Lock` for PRIVATE, `Users` for FRIENDS_ONLY). Heading: Comfortaa bold `text-xl text-[var(--brand)]`. CTA: plain brand pill `<Link>` to `/${locale}/discover` reading `Discover other books` — NO drop-shadow glow per sanctioned CTA usage map.
- **`<SeriesFooter>`** chrome refresh (`(public)/_components/series-footer.tsx`, NOT colocated — stays at the shared path because other surfaces may consume it): early-return null when both neighbors absent preserved. Was a flat 2-col grid with `border-t` hairline; now a panel-gradient card containing two tile-gradient prev/next link tiles with `<ChevronLeft size={12}>` / `<ChevronRight size={12}>` + uppercase muted `Previous in series` / `Next in series` labels + `Book N: Title` (when `seriesNumber !== null`) or bare-title rendering branches preserved. Next column has `text-right` on the outer wrapper.
- **Page orchestrator** (`app/[locale]/(public)/books/[bookId]/page.tsx`, server component, full rewrite): centered `max-w-5xl px-4 py-6 sm:px-6` on `bg-[#262728]`. `← Back` link (`← Editor` for author, `← Discover` else) with no top-bar chrome. `<ReaderPageShell>` wraps `<BookHero>` + `<BookTabStrip>` + `<ChaptersPanel>` + 3 children (`<CommentsPanel>` + `<AboutSection>` + `<SeriesFooter>`). Data plumbing: `canReadBook` gate → `notFound()` on NOT_FOUND or `<AccessDenied>` on PRIVATE/FRIENDS_ONLY; `getPublicBookAction` for the base book; supplemental `db.select` on `books` for `visibility` + `createdAt` + `updatedAt` (not on `PublicBook`); `count(bookComments)` for comment count; `chapterRows` projection (`binderItemId, chapterId, title, order, status, updatedAt` — `wordCount` deliberately dropped); `Promise.all` parallel-fetches 5 things (comments page 1, reading progress if authed, social state if authed, series neighbors, viewer profile avatar if authed). CTA precedence: `continueReadingHref ?? startReadingHref` both computed from `visibleChapters` so non-author CTAs never point at locked chapters. `shareUrl` composed from `x-forwarded-proto` + `host` headers with `http` + `localhost:3000` fallback — works in dev and prod without env-var coupling. `headersList` hoisted out of two awaits so `auth.api.getSession` and the share URL share one fetch.
- **`<ReaderPageShell>`** (`books/_components/reader-page-shell.tsx` ~30 LOC, client): owns the shared `readSet` state via `useState<Set<string>>(() => new Set(chapters.initialReadSet))`. Derives `readCount = chapters.chapters.filter((c) => readSet.has(c.binderItemId)).length` on every render. Renders `<BookHero {...hero} readCount={readCount}>` + `<BookTabStrip>` + `<ChaptersPanel {...chapters} onReadSetChange={setReadSet}>` + `{children}`. Prop types via `Omit<ComponentProps<typeof X>, 'readCount' | 'onReadSetChange'>` so the wrapper stays in sync with the children without manual prop duplication.
- **Legacy cleanup**: `app/[locale]/(public)/_components/{chapter-list,comments-panel,social-actions}.tsx` — 3 files, 307 LOC, deleted in T12 after zero-caller audit. The legacy `ChapterList` symbol grep hits in `hive/[hiveId]/chapters/page.tsx` were unrelated `getHiveChapterListAction` references; the lone reader-page `CommentsPanel` import had migrated to `../_components/comments-panel` (colocated under `books/`); `SocialActions` had zero references anywhere.

**Bugs caught + fixed mid-stream:**
- T6 first cut captured `readSet` by closure for rollback. Walking the scenario: user clicks A (snapshot `{}`, optimistic `{A}`) → user clicks B before A resolves (snapshot `{A}`, optimistic `{A, B}`) → A's action fails → rollback `setReadSet({})` wipes B silently. **Fix:** [e1c6797](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/e1c6797) switched BOTH the optimistic flip AND the rollback to functional updaters `(prev) => ...`. Rollback inverts only `binderItemId`'s flip against `prev` instead of stomping the entire set. `wasRead` still captured at click-time because it represents user INTENT, not global state. `onReadSetChange?.(set)` lives inside the updater body so the parent always sees the same value the state lands on — pragmatic violation of React's "updater must be pure" guideline accepted for the live-stat-sync contract.
- T2 surface-shape tests used `await import('@/lib/actions/reading.actions')` inside each `it()` block. Solo runs passed in ~2.7s; under full-suite parallel load the module-graph parse exceeded the 5s vitest default and timed out — T2 reported 427/427 green when full-suite was actually 426/427. **Fix:** [ca51b28](https://github.com/Cremacious/beehive-studio/commit/ca51b28) hoisted to a single top-level static `import * as readingActions` after the `vi.mock` declarations. Tests now resolve in ms once the module graph is parsed.

**Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
1. **`chapter_reads` is the per-chapter read truth.** Never derive reads from `readingProgress.chapterId` linearly — that table is the cursor only. Any new read-related query should join `chapter_reads`, not walk binder item order.
2. **`markChapterReadAction` is the ONE place that bridges read-set + cursor.** Both writes happen in the same call so the auto-mark from `/read/[chapterId]` keeps populating both tables atomically. Don't add a separate "set cursor" action.
3. **Server actions take `chapterBinderItemId` everywhere.** The chapters PK (`chapters.id`) is now exclusively a `readingProgress` cursor implementation detail. UI + new actions must pass `binderItems.id`.
4. **Optimistic-toggle rollback uses functional updaters, never captured snapshots.** Pattern: `setReadSet((prev) => invertOnlyTheOneRowYouChanged(prev, id))`. Captured `setX(state)` for rollback stomps concurrent successful toggles.
5. **Sticky scroll-spy via IntersectionObserver** uses `rootMargin: '-80px 0px -60% 0px'` + threshold `[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1]` + section-id-keyed observation. Future stacked-section pages should reuse this exact tuning.
6. **`<ReaderPageShell>` pattern** — when two stacked sibling sections need to share optimistic state (e.g. a stat in one updates from a toggle in another), wrap them in a client component that lifts state via `useState` and threads `readCount` / `onReadSetChange` callbacks. Use `Omit<ComponentProps<typeof X>, ...>` for prop typing so wrappers stay in sync.
7. **Share URL composed from headers (not env vars)** — `${proto}://${host}${path}` from `x-forwarded-proto` + `host` works in dev (localhost) AND prod without coupling.
8. **`PublicBook & { extra1; extra2 }` intersection** is the pattern for client components that need fields not on the source type — the page orchestrator supplements via sibling fetches and passes the widened object.
9. **Surface-shape tests use top-level static imports** after `vi.mock` declarations. Dynamic `await import()` per test times out at the 5s vitest default under full-suite parallel load.
10. **Test pattern for refactor commits:** mirror `lib/actions/__tests__/create-hive-action.test.ts` shape — `vi.mock` `@/lib/require-auth`, `@/lib/books/can-read`, `@/db`, then assert exports + arity. Behavior tests are gated by manual smoke per Chris's preference.

**Awaiting manual smoke** — Chris runs the 17-scenario T13 checklist before fully closing the loop (see Resume Here next step).

### Phase 1 — Foundation ✅ COMPLETE
- Full DB schema: `users`, `userProfiles`, `userBilling`, `books`, `binderItems`, `chapters`, `chapterSnapshots`, `hives`, `hiveMembers`, social tables, `exportPresets`, `bookTemplates`
- Auth: better-auth v1 (email/password + Google OAuth; Apple pre-wired for when creds are ready)
- Middleware: locale routing (next-intl, `localePrefix: 'always'`), auth guard, onboarding gate
- Route groups: `(public)` (landing, legal), `(auth)` (sign-in, sign-up, forgot-password, reset-password, onboarding), `(app)` (studio, discover, community)
- Onboarding actions: `checkUsernameAvailableAction`, `completeOnboardingAction`
- Cloudinary image upload wiring
- Rate limiting: 7 Upstash limiters
- Seed scripts: export presets + book templates

### P8A — Stripe Foundations ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-27)
First of four Phase 8 sub-projects (P8A → P8D). Lands the Stripe infrastructure.

- **Schema:** `userBilling` extended with `stripeCustomerId`, `stripeSubscriptionId`, `subscriptionStatus` (enum), `currentPeriodEnd`. `premium: boolean` dropped — entitlement now derives from `subscriptionStatus IN ('active', 'trialing')` via `getUserPremiumStatus()`.
- **SDK:** `stripe` npm package installed; `lib/stripe/client.ts` is a singleton with pinned `apiVersion` + runtime key-prefix sanity check (fails loud if test key in prod or live key in dev).
- **Server actions:** `createCheckoutSessionAction({ priceKey, locale })` and `createBillingPortalSessionAction({ locale })` in `lib/actions/billing.actions.ts`. Lazy customer creation on first checkout.
- **Webhook scaffold:** `/api/webhooks/stripe` with signature verification + no-op handlers (P8C wires real entitlement sync). DO NOT configure Stripe dashboard webhook URL until P8C ships.
- **Env vars:** `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `STRIPE_PRICE_ID_MONTHLY`, `STRIPE_PRICE_ID_ANNUAL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` documented in `.env.example`.
- **`DEV_FORCE_PREMIUM=true` preserved** — short-circuits before DB read in non-production builds.

No UI ships in P8A. P8B will build the pricing page; P8C wires real webhook handlers; P8D wires Settings → Billing portal.

**Next:** P8B Pricing page + checkout flow.

### P8B — Pricing Page + Checkout Flow ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-27)
Second of four Phase 8 sub-projects. Builds the public `/[locale]/pricing` page wired to P8A's `createCheckoutSessionAction`, routes logged-out users through sign-up first, lands a `/welcome` celebration page Stripe redirects to post-checkout.

- **Public pricing page** at `app/[locale]/(public)/pricing/page.tsx`: server component, `revalidate: 3600` ISR, fetches live Stripe prices via `stripe.prices.retrieve()` for monthly + annual. Single Premium tier with monthly/annual toggle pill (`PlanCard` client component). Dynamically computed annual savings percentage. Premium feature list with brand-y framing (Never lose a draft, Publish your book, Build your library, Grow your circle). Free-tier callout pinned below.
- **CTA flow:** logged-in users → `createCheckoutSessionAction` → Stripe-hosted Checkout. Logged-out users → `Link` to `/${locale}/sign-up?next=/${locale}/pricing` (sanitized via `safeNextPath` — same-origin paths only).
- **Sign-up `?next=` plumbing:** `safeNextPath()` helper in `lib/url-helpers.ts` validates same-origin paths (rejects protocol-prefixed, double-slash, and external URLs). Sign-up form sanitizes `?next=` via `safeNextPath` but Path 2 was taken — onboarding always redirects to `/studio`. The follow-up commit also added a server-side check on `/sign-up` that bounces already-authed users to `?next=` (so logged-in users clicking Upgrade get straight to /pricing).
- **Welcome page** at `app/[locale]/(app)/welcome/page.tsx`: celebration page Stripe redirects to on successful checkout (`success_url` updated to point here). One-time confetti CSS animation + "Continue to Studio" CTA.
- **Studio upsell href audit:** only one studio reference to `/pricing` (`version-history-drawer.tsx` free-tier Premium card) — already correctly locale-prefixed. No bare `/pricing` hrefs in app code.
- **Live Stripe checkout test deferred** — Chris will exercise the end-to-end flow manually with his own Stripe account. Until P8C wires real webhook handlers, paid users are technically not premium until P8C catches up (Stripe retries events for up to 3 days).
- 121/121 tests, tsc clean.

**Next:** P8C Webhook handlers (real entitlement sync).

### P8C — Webhooks + Entitlement ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-27)
Third of four Phase 8 sub-projects.

- **Subscription event handler** (`lib/stripe/handle-subscription-event.ts`): processes `customer.subscription.{created,updated,deleted}` events. Upserts `userBilling.subscriptionStatus`, `stripeSubscriptionId`, `currentPeriodEnd`. Idempotent by construction (same event re-applied = same final state).
- **Race-recovery branch:** if `userBilling` row is missing for a `stripeCustomerId`, the handler fetches the Stripe customer to read `metadata.userId` (set by P8A's `ensureStripeCustomer`) and upserts. Self-healing.
- **Hard failure modes:** unknown subscription status (Stripe added a value we haven't enumerated) or missing customer metadata → throws → webhook returns 500 → Stripe retries up to 3 days. Logs the customer ID for triage.
- **Webhook route** (`app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts`): now dispatches to the handler. Other events still logged + ignored.
- **Schema:** `subscription_status` enum extended with `paused` (Stripe SDK v20 includes it in the type union; missed in P8A's enumeration). Applied via `npm run db:push`.
- **Premium audit:** 9 call sites of `getUserPremiumStatus`/`requirePremium` reviewed across snapshot/publishing/book/hive/chapter actions. All correctly awaited, gated before writes, using right error codes (`PREMIUM_REQUIRED:<feature>` or `FREE_LIMIT_REACHED`). No drift found.
- **Unit tests:** 4 new tests for the handler (happy path, race recovery, missing metadata, unknown status). Total: 125 (was 121).

No new server actions.

**Post-deploy (NOT in code — Chris does in Stripe dashboard):**
1. Configure webhook endpoint at `https://{prod-domain}/api/webhooks/stripe`.
2. Subscribe to: `customer.subscription.created`, `customer.subscription.updated`, `customer.subscription.deleted`.
3. Copy signing secret → Vercel env `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`.
4. Test from dashboard's "Send test webhook" UI.

**Next:** P8D — Settings → Billing portal + downgrade UX (soft-lock when premium loss pushes user >FREE_BOOK_LIMIT or >FREE_HIVE_LIMIT).

### P8D — Billing Portal + Downgrade UX ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-27)
Fourth and FINAL Phase 8 sub-project. Closes Phase 8.

- **`/settings/billing` page:** server component with 5 state branches (free / active+trialing / past_due / canceled / other). Hero status display + Manage button (opens Stripe Portal via P8A's createBillingPortalSessionAction). past_due shows a warning card; canceled shows "Subscription ended" + Resubscribe CTA. dynamic='force-dynamic' so the page always reflects current state.
- **Soft-lock for overflow books:** `lib/billing/book-overflow.ts::isBookOverflow(userId, bookId)`. Non-premium users with >FREE_BOOK_LIMIT books get the oldest 3 active; 4th+ become read-only. Gated at saveChapterAction + all binder write actions (create/update/delete/reorder binderItem). OverflowBanner mounts in chapter-editor.tsx (brand-yellow band + Upgrade CTA); TipTap editor set to non-editable via setEditable(false) effect. createdAt ASC for stability across edits.
- **Threading:** bookOverflow computed in studio page server component → BookEditorProvider prop → exposed via useBookEditor() context → ChapterEditor consumes.
- **Hive invite gate:** existing `inviteAction` + `joinHiveByLinkAction` already check member count vs FREE_HIVE_MEMBER_LIMIT — confirmed in P8C audit + re-verified in P8D Task 4. Existing members in an over-limit hive keep editing; new invites/joins are blocked.
- **Premium semantics:** `PREMIUM_STATUSES` set in `lib/premium.ts` extended to `{active, trialing, past_due}`. Stripe's grace period (~3 weeks of payment retries) preserves access; once Stripe gives up the retry, the webhook flips status to 'canceled' and the user becomes free-tier.

No DB schema changes. Tests at 126 (+1 past_due test).

**Phase 8 (Stripe monetization) COMPLETE.** End-to-end flow:
- /pricing → Stripe Checkout → /welcome → subscription syncs via webhook → /settings/billing for management → Stripe Portal for plan changes/cancellation → downgrade triggers soft-lock if user is over free-tier limits.

**Post-deploy reminders:**
1. Configure Stripe dashboard webhook at `https://{prod-domain}/api/webhooks/stripe`.
2. Subscribe to: `customer.subscription.{created,updated,deleted}`.
3. Copy signing secret → Vercel env `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`.
4. Test the live flow with a real test-mode subscription.

**Next:** Phase 9 — TBD. Candidates: referral codes, growth analytics, plan-upgrade nudges, polish.

### Book Creation Wizard Redesign ✅ COMPLETE (2026-06-01)

Re-skins `/[locale]/studio/new` to the Apple-warm, helper-text-forward variant captured in the spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-book-creation-wizard-redesign-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-book-creation-wizard-redesign-design.md); plan at [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-book-creation-wizard-redesign.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-book-creation-wizard-redesign.md). 4-step structure preserved (Basics / Discovery / Structure / Sharing). Pure visual + UX-copy refresh — no new tokens, no new server actions, no DB changes. Inherits the entire token system from the editor refresh. 424/424 tests stayed green; tsc clean throughout.

**Task commits (11/11 shipped):** T1 `f7a759f` stage shell + 4-pill progress bar · T2+T3 `1fce158` shared `<StepHeader>` + `<WizardField>` + `<HelperText>` + `<ExampleChips>` + recessed input + textarea-bg one stop lighter · T4-T8 `b97fedc` Steps 1-4 full refresh + `<SharingControls>` re-skin (parallel-dispatch racing meant the T8 agent's `git add -A` swept up T4-T7's uncommitted work; functionally complete, history just collapsed) · T9 `6c70b7e` `<WizardFooter>` + per-step Next-button copy + submit flow · T10 `27c36fc` feature-integrity + a11y sweep (clean — no fixes needed) · T11 ship.

Highlights:
- 1040px stage panel with a 4-pill progress bar at the top (`aria-current="step"` on active; checkmark icon when done; clickable backwards-jump to any reached step; forward-jump blocked when title is empty).
- 2-col body in Steps 1 + 3 (cover-on-left / fields-on-right; structure-on-left / template-on-right).
- Multi-sentence helper text under every field; clickable italic-serif example chips on the Title field (Step 1) + Comparable Titles (Step 2). `<ExampleChips>` chips render as `<button>` with `aria-label="Use example: …"`.
- New `<CoverPicker>` component (`cover-picker.tsx`) — drag-drop + click-to-browse + URL paste, all visible at once. Cloudinary upload via the existing `useCloudinaryUpload('covers')` hook when `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME` is set; falls back to `FileReader.readAsDataURL` data-URL embed otherwise. PNG/JPG/WEBP only; 5 MB cap; sonner error toasts on validation fail. Dropzone is keyboard-accessible (Enter/Space triggers picker).
- Apple-warm tone: reassuring lede on every step ("You can change any of this later", "Most writers start private and switch later"); per-step Next-button copy ("Next: tell us how to find it →", "Next: shape your manuscript →", "Next: who can see it →", "Create your book ✨").
- Brand-pill Next button — NO drop-shadow glow per Chris's explicit call.
- `<SharingControls>` re-skinned once and the change cascades to both consumers (wizard Step 4 + Details page Sharing section); both verified intact. 3-layer discoverable defense preserved (checkbox disabled when visibility ≠ PUBLIC; client force-clear on visibility change; server-side `.transform()` coercion in `createBookSchema`).
- Stale `wizard-progress.tsx` deleted earlier in the epic (already absent before T1).
- Textarea bg derived inline as `oklch(0.245 0.003 256)` — one stop lighter than other recessed inputs. **Follow-up:** promote to `--textarea-bg` token in `app/globals.css` if a second consumer needs it.

**Patterns now load-bearing:**
1. **`<WizardField>` + `<HelperText>` + `<ExampleChips>`** are the canonical pattern for any future multi-step form. Helper text gets an `id`, the input gets `aria-describedby` pointing at it. Example chips are presentational pure: take an array of strings + an `onPick(value)` callback.
2. **`<CoverPicker>`** is the canonical drop-zone + URL combo (Cloudinary primary, data-URL fallback). Reusable for any future image-upload field where Cloudinary may not be configured.
3. **The 4-pill `aria-current="step"` progress bar** in `book-creation-form.tsx` is the canonical multi-step indicator for the studio surfaces.

**Known follow-ups:**
- Promote `oklch(0.245 0.003 256)` to `--textarea-bg` token if a second consumer arises.
- Future plan tasks should avoid `git add -A` when working in parallel batches — T4-T8 fused into one commit because the T8 agent swept the working tree clean.

Dropped the unused 🐝 emoji prefix on the hive name header.
- **Dashboard** (`hive/[hiveId]/page.tsx`): welcome card + linked-book card (paper-warm cover preserved) + standalone fallback. Stat tiles consolidated into the welcome panel meta line. "Read the book" CTA visible to all members; "Open in studio" CTA gated to book author.
- **Wiki** (`hive/[hiveId]/wiki/*` — 6 files, +323/-169): shell + 3-tab view switch (By Category / By Folder / Notes) + entry editor + folder renderer + entry card + category picker. Per-category `--wiki-{CATEGORY}` accents preserved as left-stripe / pill / tag accent. 14 categories + tag chips + entry/folder editors + read-only mode all preserved. **Notable deviation:** the entry editor dropped its old `--wiki-canvas` / `--wiki-card-bg` cream-on-cream tokens entirely; rebuilt scoped `[data-slot="wiki-entry-pane"] .ProseMirror` against `--canvas-dark-ink*` so wiki entries are uniformly dark walnut regardless of editor theme.
- **Outline** (`hive/[hiveId]/outline/*`): beat sheet surface with act groups + chapter-link popover (hive-cloned variant) + standalone-hive variant + BETA_READER read-only footer. Drag-between-acts + status cycling + per-act and ungrouped "+ Add a beat" affordances all preserved.
- **Chapters** (`hive/[hiveId]/chapters/*`): index + chapter view. **Notable deviation:** the chapter view's dark prose surface is preserved (H2 T17's original design — only the studio chapter editor uses cream paper). The plan's "cream paper sheet preserved byte-for-byte" phrasing was aspirational; the hive chapter view always shipped dark prose. Collab gutter integrates cleanly into the dark walnut frame.
- **Annotations + Suggestions bulk views** (`hive/[hiveId]/annotations/*` + `suggestions/*`): outer panel + per-chapter group headers + inline `Row` components got the new tile gradient + per-layer left border. Stays flat (deep-link is the engagement path).
- **Submissions** (`hive/[hiveId]/submissions/*` — 6 files, +485/-297): all 4 modes (list + composer + review + read). Status pills use `--status-{state}` tokens. **Notable deviation:** the composer dropped its cream paper sheet in favor of dark walnut panel+tile chrome (mirrors T3 wiki precedent — only studio chapter editor still uses cream paper). SaveStatusBadge hidden in non-editable modes.
- **Discussions** (`hive/[hiveId]/discussions/*`): list + thread + reply composer + compose modal (Dialog cascade). Reply composer reordered BELOW replies (was above); thread title derived from `post.body.split('\n')[0].slice(0, 80)`.
- **Word Goals** (`hive/[hiveId]/word-goals/*` + sidebar badge): page + active goals strip + 3 sub-panels (Contributors / Recent Activity / History) + new/edit modals (Dialog cascade). Sidebar progress badge re-skinned in T1's layout pass. Progress bars use brand-yellow fill on inset track.
- **Buzz Board** (`hive/[hiveId]/buzz/*`): feed + post card + link card + like button (muted idle, brand-yellow active) + compose/edit modals (Dialog cascade) + empty state. TEXT + LINK posts + like flow all preserved; LinkCard URL line + icon promoted to brand-yellow.
- **Members** (`hive/[hiveId]/members/page.tsx` + `_components/hive-members.tsx`): invite link sub-panel (brand-yellow Copy CTA + recessed link input + member-count badge) + member rows with role dropdown (OWNER) or plain-text role (non-OWNER) + remove button (ConfirmDialog cascade). Pre-existing username-invite form kept (dropping would be a regression). Added a member-count progress bar vs `FREE_HIVE_MEMBER_LIMIT`.
- **Settings** (`hive/[hiveId]/settings/page.tsx` + `_components/hive-settings-form.tsx` + `_components/coming-soon.tsx`): form sections as sub-panels with brand-yellow labels + recessed inputs + 3-card visibility picker + discoverable checkbox + Save brand-yellow CTA + Danger Zone (destructive-tinted) + ComingSoon stub re-skinned for any future-deferred subroute.
- **Invite accept** (`hive/invite/[token]/page.tsx`): centered card on backdrop with three state branches (valid → accept; invalid/expired → destructive; over-limit → muted Users). Premium-owned hives show `count / ∞ members` and skip the over-limit branch; already-member case server-redirects to bypass the accept screen entirely.

**Post-ship UX flags worth knowing (out-of-scope, deferred):**
- `hive_invites` table doesn't track inviter — invite-accept uses hive owner as proxy in the "Invited by @x" line.
- Pre-existing `disabled={type !== 'TOTAL' && false}` always-false bug in `new-goal-modal.tsx` (out of scope for this refresh).
- `getHiveAction` members-page projection still joins `users.name` / `users.image` not `userProfiles.username` / `avatarUrl` — known AGENTS.md cleanup item, intentionally left.

**Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
1. **Same design system serves studio editor AND hive routes.** Single source of truth at `app/globals.css`. Any future surface (auth pages, landing, /community, /discover, /settings) should consume the same token scale.
2. **shadcn Dialog primitive carries the chrome.** Every modal across the app inherits new chrome from `components/ui/dialog.tsx`. Future modals should reach for shadcn Dialog rather than rolling custom overlays.
3. **Sidebar progress badge pattern.** Server-fetch via React `cache()` in the layout, thread as a prop to the client sidebar. Reusable for any future sidebar widget that needs live data.
4. **Cream paper is studio-chapter-editor-only.** Every other surface (wiki entries, hive chapter view, submission composer, character/notes/outline panels) uses dark walnut + scoped `.ProseMirror` against `--canvas-dark-ink*`. When adding a new specialized renderer that consumes TipTap, scope the ProseMirror color rule rather than relying on globals defaults.

**Brand-yellow usage map in hive (sanctioned uses):** chrome headings, active sidebar nav entry icon, active wiki view-switch tab, active visibility picker card border, "+ New Entry" / "+ New Submission" / "+ New Discussion" / "+ New Goal" / "+ New Post" triggers, "Go to Hive" footer text in studio binder, Approve submission CTA, Save settings CTA, Accept invite CTA, Post Reply / Post Buzz CTA, Copy invite link CTA, like-button active heart + count, word-goals progress fill, sidebar word-goals badge title + fill, BACK-link hover, error-state "Back to studio" link, invite-accept member-count progress fill.

**Out of scope (deferred):** Light-mode chrome variant. Auth pages, landing page, /community, /discover, /settings (untouched unless they share a re-skinned primitive like `dialog.tsx`).

### Studio Editor Aesthetic Refresh ✅ COMPLETE (2026-06-01)

Re-skins every studio editor chrome surface to a warmer, cool-gray, iOS-modern stacked-depth aesthetic. Presentation-only — no DB, no feature changes, no IA changes. 424/424 tests stayed green; tsc clean throughout. Approved mockup at `.superpowers/brainstorm/29735-1780335541/content/full-editor.html`. Spec at [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-editor-aesthetic-refresh-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-01-editor-aesthetic-refresh-design.md); plan at [docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-editor-aesthetic-refresh.md](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-01-editor-aesthetic-refresh.md).

**Task commits (15/15 shipped):** T1 `811a964` tokens · T2 `fa5ce62` app nav · T3 `bb36f31` binder · T4 `8902e96` toolbar · T5 `9291c87` chapter canvas frame · T6 `ce35abc` status bar · T7 `a63b716` metadata panel · T8 `65a7831` modals · T9 `1d9d73e` drawers + find/replace + gutter chrome · T10 `c75503e` specialized renderers · T11 `f3fa46a` collab gutter cards · T12 `d5308cc` banners · T13 `8632594` empty states + writing analysis · T14 feature-integrity audit (no code changes) · T15 ship (this commit).

- **Token additions in `app/globals.css` (T1, additive — never rename DP1–DP4 tokens):** new cool-gray mid-stops `--canvas-dark-150 / -250 / -350 / -400` (the -150 was already present from Library v2; -200 and -300 got tiny lightness bumps within visual tolerance), radius scale `--r-card` (20px) / `--r-row` (14px) / `--r-btn` (12px) / `--r-pill` (999px) / `--r-nav` (18px), depth shadows `--sh-card / -tile / -inset`, hairline border `--br-card`.
- **Stacked iOS depth philosophy:** every chrome surface is a vertical gradient (lighter top → darker bottom) + an inner top highlight + multi-layer drop shadow. Outer panels use `linear-gradient(180deg, --canvas-dark-250, --canvas-dark-200)` + `--r-card` + `--sh-card` + `--br-card`. Inset tiles use `linear-gradient(180deg, --canvas-dark-350, --canvas-dark-300)` + `--r-btn` (or `--r-row`) + `--sh-tile`. Recessed inputs use `--sh-inset`. Pure black is **banned** from chrome — darkest legitimate surface is `--canvas-dark-100`.
- **Brand-yellow on headings:** chrome section headings + dark-theme TipTap H1 (via scoped `[data-editor-theme="dark"] .ProseMirror h1` override) use `color: var(--brand)`. Sanctioned usage map (12 places, restrained): chrome headings · app logo · active nav link · active toolbar tile · active status pill · premium badge · word-goal progress fill · word-goals page progress fill · + Add binder trigger text · Go to Hive footer text · unsaved-changes dot · annotation layer accents.
- **Cream prose surface byte-for-byte unchanged** (T5 explicit non-touch). The chapter editor's cream paper sheet, Newsreader serif, paper-ink prose color, FM/BM page sheets, note paper, and character identity card all live in their own paper-aware surfaces — chrome rules never restyle them.
- **All existing affordances preserved (T14 audit confirmed):** binder + Add tile present in BOTH the header (text-link delegating to the footer trigger via `data-binder-add-trigger` querySelector) AND the footer (the canonical `<BinderAddMenu>` dropdown — 10 types across Manuscript / Worldbuilding / Planning sections + wiki category picker). Chapter-editor toolbar's 19 buttons all render and dispatch (Bold/Italic/Strike, Heading▾ dropdown, List▾ dropdown, Quote, HR, Undo/Redo, Underline/Highlight/Link, Align▾ dropdown, Preview, Find, History, Gutter [conditional on `bookHive`], Theme, More▾ dropdown, Focus). Status bar word-goal inline edit still works (the `·` separators were dropped; the word-goal % text became the inline-edit trigger directly). Metadata panel still has all 5 sections (Status pills / Synopsis / Scene Planner / Notes / Publishing). Modals (export, cheatsheet, sprint setup, ConfirmDialog) all rendered via the shadcn `Dialog` primitive — single chrome update cascades app-wide. Drawers (history) + find/replace + gutter chrome + snapshot-preview + overflow banner + all specialized renderers (wiki entry, wiki folder, character profile, outline beat-sheet, NoteEditor, FM/BM PageWrapper, container view) re-skinned with cream sub-surfaces intact. Collaboration gutter cards retain per-layer accent colors for differentiation.
- **Notable scope deviations from the plan:**
  - **Notes renderer needed a structural change** — an inner `paper-100` div was added inside `NoteEditor` to recreate the cream sheet inside the new dark panel (the plan's pure-CSS approach would have lost the paper feel).
  - **Corkboard step was a no-op** — the corkboard view was deleted in `8ddf3a9` before this refresh epic; no surface remained to restyle.
  - **Light-mode overrides needed `!important`** to win over inline-style gradients — when a `data-slot` panel uses an inline `style` attribute for the gradient, the `[data-editor-theme="light"] [data-slot="X"] { background: ... !important }` pattern is required.
  - **Writing analysis 44px headline wraps to two lines** at the 288px panel width — flag if it should be scaled (28–32px would fit).
  - **Active app-nav link is now pure brand-yellow text** (background pill dropped — flag if you want it back).
  - **Status bar `·` separators removed;** the word-goal `%` text itself became the inline-edit trigger (smaller hit target, but cleaner visually).
  - **Per-layer annotation card accents** stayed as literal `oklch(...)` values — the plan's `--annot-layer-*` tokens never existed; promote to CSS vars if reused in another surface.

**Patterns now load-bearing (read before adding a new chrome surface):**
1. **Token additions in `globals.css` are additive.** Never rename or repurpose a DP1–DP4 token; add a new one if needed.
2. **Inline-style gradients require `!important` overrides for theme variants.** Pattern: `[data-editor-theme="light"] [data-slot="X"] { background: linear-gradient(...) !important }`.
3. **shadcn `Dialog` primitive carries the chrome.** Consumers (export modal / cheatsheet / sprint setup / ConfirmDialog) get the new look for free — touch `components/ui/dialog.tsx` rather than each modal.
4. **Specialized renderers wrap a cream-paper inner sheet inside the dark panel chrome.** The new `NoteEditor` is the canonical example — outer dark `data-slot` panel + inner `paper-100` div with paper-ink prose color.
5. **Brand-yellow is allowed in exactly 12 places** (see the usage map above and the spec). Any new chrome surface must respect it — don't reach for brand-yellow as a generic accent.

**Out of scope (deferred):** Hive routes (separate spec — next brainstorm). Light-mode chrome variant. Auth pages, landing page, /community, /discover, /settings (untouched unless they share a re-skinned primitive like `dialog.tsx`).

**Next:** Brainstorm + spec + plan the Hive aesthetic refresh — the tokens shipped today are the source of truth; the hive routes need a parallel pass.
