### SP-A — Reader Route + Privacy/Discoverable ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-28)

Public-facing book reader route accessible from the studio editor. First of two sub-projects (SP-A reader + SP-B friendships); SP-B deferred.

- **Schema** (commit `7e180bb`): `book_visibility` enum extended with `FRIENDS`; existing-but-unused `books.explorable` column renamed to `discoverable` (saved a column add); new composite index `books_discoverable_visibility_idx` on `(discoverable, visibility)`; backfill `UPDATE books SET discoverable=true WHERE visibility='PUBLIC' AND status='PUBLISHED'` ran (0 rows in dev). Migration applied via one-shot tsx script (drizzle-kit push requires TTY; same trade-off as Phase 7 — drizzle snapshot history is out of sync with live DB).
- **`canReadBook()` helper** (`7e180bb`/`acf7948`): single source of truth at [lib/books/can-read.ts](lib/books/can-read.ts). Resolution order: NOT_FOUND → author wins → PUBLIC → FRIENDS=FRIENDS_ONLY (placeholder for SP-B) → PRIVATE. 6 unit tests.
- **Discover refactor** (`a8567ce`): `getDiscoverFeedAction` + `getDiscoverWritersAction` now filter on `discoverable=true AND visibility='PUBLIC'`; `getPublicBookAction` stripped to bare PK lookup (privacy now caller-owned via `canReadBook`). Six `status='PUBLISHED'` refs audited and intentionally left — they're author-label / feed-event queries, not access gates.
- **Reader components hoisted** (`fba2c1a`): `ChapterList`, `CommentsPanel`, `SocialActions` git-moved (100% similarity) from `discover/_components/` to `(public)/_components/` for sharing.
- **New reader at `/[locale]/books/[bookId]`** (`4c43ab3`, `ab01e81`): server component pair — book overview page + chapter reader at `read/[chapterId]`. canReadBook gate at top: NOT_FOUND → `notFound()`; PRIVATE/FRIENDS_ONLY → `<AccessDenied>` (Lock icon for private, Users for friends-only, brand-yellow "Discover other books" CTA). Author back-link goes to studio; everyone else gets discover. ChapterList refactored to take `readerBasePath` prop.
- **Redirects** (`72bfa76`): old `/discover/book/[bookId]` + `read/[chapterId]` pages are now 8-line `permanentRedirect` shims (HTTP 308); 4 production `<Link>` hrefs migrated to `/books/` directly so internal nav skips the 308 hop. Code dupe between discover and books readers fully collapsed.
- **Reader-write action gating** (`950aec1`): `markChapterReadAction`, `getReadingProgressAction`, `addCommentAction` gated after `requireAuth()` via `canReadBook`; `getBookCommentsAction` (which lives in `discover.actions.ts` and supports anon viewers) gated via `getOptionalUserId`. All return `{ success: false, error: 'FORBIDDEN' }` on denial. Closed a silent bug — `getBookCommentsAction` had no visibility filter before.
- **Validation schemas** (`2eb43e2`): `createBookSchema` + `updateBookDetailsSchema` accept `visibility` (incl. FRIENDS) + `discoverable` with `.transform()` coercing discoverable→false when visibility≠PUBLIC; `updateBookSchema` widened to FRIENDS + optional discoverable (no transform; partial-update semantics). 4 new coercion tests + 1 repurposed sibling test now rejects `'EVERYONE'` and accepts FRIENDS.
- **Wizard Sharing step** (`ea888a3`): new `SharingControls` presentational component (three-card privacy radio Private/Friends/Public with Lock/Users/Globe lucide icons + discoverable checkbox, brand-yellow active state). Wizard now has 4 steps (Step 4 = Sharing, owns Create Book submit); Step 3 "Create Book" became "Next →". 3-layer discoverable defense (checkbox disabled + client force-clear on visibility change + server coercion).
- **Details page Sharing section** (`e7751bd`): `SharingControls` shared between wizard and Details form (`components/book/sharing-controls.tsx`); Details form gained a fifth Section after Publishing; `Visibility` type exported from the shared module, imported by both consumers.
- **Editor entry points** (`70daf4e`, `c1b1817`): toolbar Preview button (Eye icon in VIEW zone) + binder header book title wrapped in `<Link>` to the reader (double-click still triggers rename via `e.preventDefault()` — known minor UX race on the dblclick, deferred).

**Tests:** 132 → 137 (+5 net: +6 canReadBook + 4 schema coercion + 1 sibling repurposed - lost). tsc clean across the run.

**Mental model:** three independent axes on a book — `privacy` (who can open the reader, via `visibility` column extended with FRIENDS) / `discoverable` (whether it appears in /discover listings) / `status` (DRAFT/PUBLISHED — author's done-label only, no longer gates access).

**Deferred to SP-B (Friendships subsystem):** symmetric friend request/accept table + actions + UI + notifications. Until SP-B ships, `FRIENDS` visibility is settable but `canReadBook` returns `FRIENDS_ONLY` (author-only access).

### Linked Series ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-28)

The "Series" choice in book creation now does something. Before: `seriesName` + `seriesNumber` were written to the DB and never displayed. Now: series metadata surfaces across the reader, library, discover, and profile; the library has a "By series" sort that clusters; the book reader page shows previous/next navigation between books in the same series; the wizard + Details page explain what the choice controls.

- **Matching helper** (`lib/books/series-key.ts`): `normalizeSeriesKey(name)` — lowercase, drops leading "The ", collapses whitespace, returns null for empty. 7 unit tests. Display name in UI is always the raw `seriesName`; this helper is matching-only.
- **Server-side neighbor query** (`lib/books/get-series-neighbors.ts`): `getSeriesNeighbors({ currentBook, viewerUserId })` returns `{ previous, next, total }`. Author-scoped (same `userId`), normalized-key match, gaps allowed (next jumps over missing numbers), every candidate filtered through `canReadBook` so PRIVATE/FRIENDS-locked books in the same series silently omit. 6 unit tests with mocked DB + canReadBook.
- **Projection extensions:** `BookSummary` (library), `DiscoverBook` (discover), `ProfileBook` (profile), `PublicBook` (reader page) all extended with `seriesName: string | null` + `seriesNumber: number | null`. SELECTs updated correspondingly.
- **Shared `SeriesLine` component** (`components/book/series-line.tsx`): renders "Book N · *Series*" (mono 11px, 0.05em letter-spacing) when both fields set, "Part of *Series*" when only seriesName is set, null when no series. Used by 3 surfaces: library card, discover card, profile tile.
- **Book reader hero:** "Book N of *Series*" line between the author meta row and the tags row, only when seriesName is set.
- **Library "By series" sort** (`book-grid.tsx`): new option in the existing SORT_LABELS. When active, the `clusterBooks` builder groups by normalized key with the first-encountered raw `seriesName` as the cluster displayName. Within-cluster sort is `seriesNumber` ascending with nulls last. Standalone books cluster under a "Standalone (N)" subheader at the end. Operates on the already-filtered `visible` list so filter chips remain accurate. Cluster order = insertion order (not alphabetical) — flag if you want it alphabetical later.
- **Reader page footer** (`(public)/_components/series-footer.tsx`): `<SeriesFooter>` renders prev/next links (ChevronLeft / ChevronRight + "Book N: Title"). Returns null when both neighbors are null. Author always sees full neighbors; non-author viewers don't see locked next-books because of the canReadBook filter inside `getSeriesNeighbors`.
- **Wizard Step 3 (`step-three.tsx`) + Details Structure section**: identical explanatory paragraph above the Standalone/Series toggle: "Is this book part of a series? If so, the series name and number will appear on your book's reader page, library card, and any discoverable surface — and readers will be able to jump between books in the series."

**Pattern note:** When adding a new card surface that displays a book, mount `<SeriesLine seriesName={book.seriesName} seriesNumber={book.seriesNumber} />` from `@/components/book/series-line`. Single component, consistent visual across all surfaces.

**Path correction:** AGENTS.md previously stated the legacy `create-book-wizard/` directory was unused (no callers). That's incorrect — the active wizard at `studio/new/_components/book-creation-form.tsx` imports `StepThree` from `studio/_components/create-book-wizard/step-three.tsx`. The `step-three.tsx` file IS the live wizard surface for Step 3; only `index.tsx` in that legacy directory is fully unused.

No DB / schema changes. 175/175 tests (162 baseline + 7 series-key + 6 get-series-neighbors), tsc clean.

### Chapter Status — Publish-Readiness Gate ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-28)

Chapter status now controls reader visibility on the public book reader. Readers see full prose only for chapters at `status IN ('REVISED', 'FINAL')`; everything below renders as a "Draft — coming soon" locked teaser. Author always sees full content (gate is bypassed when `viewerUserId === book.userId`). Solves the "what does chapter status DO?" problem by giving the feature load-bearing user-visible consequences.

- **Pure helper** (`lib/books/is-chapter-reader-visible.ts`): `isChapterReaderVisible(status)` — true for REVISED/FINAL, false otherwise. Exports `ChapterStatus` union matching the `chapter_status` pgEnum. 5 unit tests. Single source of truth so the threshold is changed in one place if it ever moves.
- **Reader chapter list** (`(public)/_components/chapter-list.tsx`): widened `ChapterItem` with `status` + `updatedAt`; new `isAuthor` prop. Non-author + non-visible chapters render as a non-clickable `<div>` with "Draft — coming soon" badge + `aria-disabled="true"`. Visible chapters render the link with an "Updated MMM DD, YYYY" label sourced from `chapters.updatedAt`. `formatUpdatedLabel(Date | string)` normalizes Next.js server→client serialization.
- **Reader page** (`books/[bookId]/page.tsx`): chapter projection includes `status` + `updatedAt`. `isAuthor` computed once and threaded down. Hero "Start/Continue Reading" CTA + `lastReadChapter` both gated through `readerVisibleChapters` for non-authors — no broken links to locked chapters from the hero or the progress-bar Continue button.
- **Chapter reader page** (`books/[bookId]/read/[chapterId]/page.tsx`): direct-URL access guard. After `canReadBook` + chapter fetch, if viewer is not author AND `!isChapterReaderVisible(chapter.status)`, render `LockedChapterPlaceholder` (Clock icon + "This chapter is still being drafted" + back-link to `/books/[bookId]`). Gate placed BEFORE `markChapterReadAction` so locked direct-URL hits don't get logged as reads. Chapter query extended with `status: chapters.status`; book query extended with `userId: books.userId`.
- **No auto-demote on edit.** Status only changes by explicit author action; editing a REVISED/FINAL chapter just bumps `updatedAt` (which the freshness label reflects).
- **Binder color dots** (`binder/binder-item.tsx`): 6px dot using `--status-*` tokens renders between chapter icon + title for `type === 'chapter'` rows. `BinderItemRow` widened with `chapterStatus: ChapterStatus | null`; `getBinderTreeAction` projection includes `chapters.status`. Provider's `updateChapterStatus` optimistically syncs `chapterStatus` onto `binderItems` + rolls back on failure (live UI update). 3 literal `BinderItemRow` construction sites updated (chapter-editor, binder-item-menu, binder-add-menu) — new chapters default to `chapterStatus='FIRST_DRAFT'` matching schema default.
- **Metadata panel** (`metadata/metadata-panel.tsx`): explanatory paragraph above the Status pill bar ("Readers can only see chapters marked Revised or Final…"); per-pill subtitle ("Visible to readers" / "Not visible to readers") in 9px mono uppercase. Pills switched `rounded-full` → `rounded-md` to accommodate the two-line label+subtitle layout. Active-state subtitle inherits the status color; inactive sits at muted foreground.

No DB / schema changes. 162/162 tests (157 baseline + 5 new helper tests), tsc clean. The threshold logic centralized in `isChapterReaderVisible` so moving it to FINAL-only or extending it to FIRST_DRAFT is a one-file edit if priorities change.

**Pattern note:** when adding new reader-side gates, place them AFTER `canReadBook` (book-level access) and AFTER data fetches (you need the row), but BEFORE any side effects like `markChapterReadAction` — otherwise the side effect fires on locked surfaces.

### Drag-into-Folder ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-28)

Extends the binder's existing dnd-kit setup with nesting. Dropping a document onto a folder row nests it; existing sibling-reorder behavior preserved.

**Accept rules** (`lib/binder/drop-rules.ts`):
- `part` (collection) accepts only `chapter`
- `research_folder` accepts every non-chapter document type: `research_note`, `research_folder` (sub-folders), `character`, `outline`, `front_matter`, `back_matter`
- Non-folder types are top-level only
- Cycle guard walks `parentId` upward from the target — rejects if active.id appears in the chain (with a `seen` Set defense against corrupt data)
- Pure helpers (`getAcceptedChildTypes`, `canNest`, `classifyDropZone`) unit-tested in isolation in `lib/binder/__tests__/drop-rules.test.ts`

**Mechanism** (lessons learned from a hairy debugging session — see `ff3e711` commit message):
- **NO `verticalListSortingStrategy`** on SortableContext. The strategy makes rows shift out of the way during drag to preview the new position — which made folder rows vacate when the user tried to hover them as nest targets. Sortable still tracks reorder logically; items just snap to the new position on release rather than animating during drag.
- **Custom collision detection** (`pointerWithin` → `rectIntersection` composition) using real pointer Y from a global `pointermove` listener. `closestCenter` doesn't work here because the dragged ghost's center is offset from the user's pointer by their grab offset, so it consistently resolved `over` to a neighbor instead of the folder under the pointer.
- **Separate `:nest` droppable overlay** on folder rows (NOT in SortableContext, so it doesn't participate in sortable reorder). Overlay element is absolutely positioned at `top:3, bottom:3, inset-x-0, pointer-events-none` — covers ~87% of the row. When the pointer is over the overlay, custom collision detection prefers the `:nest` id (suffixed `${node.id}:nest`) over the sortable's id. `handleDragOver` / `handleDragEnd` strip the suffix to recover the logical folder id.
- **`setNodeRef` on the row only**, not on a wrapper containing the row + children. Otherwise an expanded folder's droppable rect spans its entire subtree and `closestCenter` (or any rect-based collision) picks children instead of the folder itself.

**Three drop zones per row** (`classifyDropZone`): folders get top 3px = reorder before, middle = nest into (via `:nest` overlay), bottom 3px = reorder after. Non-folder rows split at vertical midpoint (before/after only). The 3px edges make the nest band ~87% of the row, so users don't need precise alignment.

**Visual indicators** (`binder-item.tsx`):
- Reorder before/after: thin brand-yellow horizontal line above/below the row (`h-0.5 bg-brand rounded-full mx-2`)
- Nest target: row gets `ring-2 ring-brand bg-brand/20` + a 4px brand-yellow halo via `shadow-[0_0_0_4px_oklch(from_var(--brand)_l_c_h_/_0.25)]`. The halo makes the locked-on folder visually pop forward.
- All rendered via a `dropZone: { overId, zone } | null` field on the existing `BinderTreeContext`.

**Auto-expand** (`binder-tree.tsx`): middle-body hover on a collapsed folder for 500ms expands it. Implemented with two `useRef`s (timer + target id) so re-hovering the same folder doesn't reset the countdown. Timer canceled on drag end, on invalid hover, and on any guard-return path inside `handleDragOver`.

**Drop behavior** (`handleDragEnd`): branches on the captured `dropZone`. `middle` → `parentId = folderId, order = maxOrderInFolder + 1` (no sibling reshuffle). `before`/`after` → adopts `overItem.parentId`, splices at the correct insertion index, recomputes orders. All updates flow through the existing `reorderBinderItemsAction` — no server-action changes.

**Pattern notes for future dnd-kit work in this binder:**
- Don't use `verticalListSortingStrategy` when you also need "drop ON" semantics. The strategy and "drop ON" are fundamentally incompatible.
- Use `pointerWithin` + a tracked `pointermove` ref when the dragged ghost's center may be offset from the user's pointer (which is almost always).
- For "drop ON" targets that overlap with sortable items, use a SEPARATE `useDroppable` NOT in SortableContext, with an overlay element covering the target band. Prefer the overlay id in collision detection. The non-sortable nature of the overlay is what stops the underlying sortable from reordering.
- `setNodeRef` MUST go on the visual click target itself, never on a wrapper that also contains descendants — otherwise the droppable rect doesn't match the visual target.

No DB / schema changes. 157/157 tests (137 baseline + 17 helpers + 3 net updates), tsc clean. Two non-blocking concerns noted: timer cleanup on unmount (low risk; would require `useEffect` cleanup hook), and `.find()` perf on very large binders (Map lookup would be cheaper). Both deferred.

### Character Profile Redesign ✅ COMPLETE (2026-05-28)

Single-file presentational rewrite of `character-profile.tsx`. The body region's 2-column section grid (which caused an Appearance↔Personality row-stretch bug) collapsed into a single continuous "specimen sheet" — sections separated by thin `var(--sheet-rule)` horizontal lines instead of individual paper cards. Per-section background / shadow / border-radius removed; the dossier feel now comes from the sheet's own paper tone + the mono index labels (`01 · Description`, etc.) + the display headings.

- **Layout:** outer container `grid + gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr'` → `flex flex-col`. `SectionCard.full` prop removed (every section is full-width now). `<section>` semantic tag replaces `<div>` in both SectionCard and the Relationships outer wrapper.
- **Dividers:** `pt-8 first:pt-2 border-t border-[var(--sheet-rule)] first:border-t-0` provides the divider rule + top-padding pattern. Tailwind's `first:` variant handles the first-section exception cleanly. `--sheet-rule` already theme-aware via the in-file `<style>` block (`canvas-dark-300` dark / `paper-300` light).
- **Typography refinements:** section heading 17→16px, body line-height 1.65→1.7.
- **Relationships section:** outer card chrome (background/shadow/radius) dropped, joins the divider flow. Inline row pills (avatar + name + arrow + relation chip + remove `×`) preserved verbatim — they provide their own structure.
- **Preserved verbatim:** identity header card (avatar + name + meta), breadcrumb head, theme-token `<style>` block, MetaPill, MetaText, save-status badge, all logic (CharacterContent type, readContent legacy migration, scheduleSave debounce, setField helper, removeRelationship, commitTitle).

+11 / -39 lines. No DB / schema / test changes. 137/137 tests, tsc clean.

### Hives Redesign — H4 Motivation ✅ COMPLETE (2026-06-01)

Fourth of 5 sub-projects. Lands the team-momentum layer on top of H2's binder mirror + H3's collaboration surfaces: shared **Word Goals** that progress automatically as any hive member saves chapters on the linked book, plus a lightweight **Buzz Board** for TEXT/LINK posts with likes. End-to-end: any hive member's chapter save in the studio editor auto-flows a `hive_word_logs` row via `logHiveWordDelta` (outside-tx best-effort hook, 60s throttle per `(user, chapter)`, zero-delta skip — mirrors snapshot precedent); OWNER/MODERATOR creates goals at /hive/[hiveId]/word-goals (DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY/CUSTOM with auto-derived end dates); progress + per-contributor breakdown aggregate across all members within `[startDate, endDate)`; goals lazy-auto-archive on end-date expiry (sweep on list, no cron); the hive sidebar shows a live progress badge under "Word Goals" (server-fetched in layout via React `cache()`, passed to client sidebar as prop). Buzz Board at /hive/[hiveId]/buzz with TEXT/LINK posts, denormalized `like_count` bumped in-tx alongside the like row for atomicity, per-call state-correct toggle (not strict HTTP idempotent), Edit/Delete kebab gated by `canEditBuzz`. `buzz_posted` fires into /community with the row reading "posted: '<excerpt>...'" for TEXT and "shared a link to <hostname>" for LINK. Milestones / achievements deliberately DROPPED from the redesign — do not reintroduce. 15 tasks across schema → predicates → helpers → save-hook → server actions → UI → audit → ship.

**Task SHAs:** T1 `0bef5c4` schema migration · T2 `182cf6d` permission predicates · T3 `0276b74` word-delta helper · T4 `8bc95bc` `logHiveWordDelta` hook into `saveChapterAction` · T5 `e94dce7` goal-progress helpers · T6 `3fd2ede` word-goal actions · T7 `bbfd160` word-logs action · T8 `d6891f2` buzz actions · T9-T11 `a385f7d` word-goals page + modals + sidebar badge · T12-T13 `e16c78e` buzz page + modals + like flow · T14 `25d2c17` activity audit + buzz_posted payload refinement · T15 (this ship commit).

- **Schema migration** (`scripts/migrate-h4.ts`, `db/schema/hive.ts`, T1): two new pgEnums (`word_goal_type` ∈ DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY/CUSTOM; `buzz_post_type` ∈ TEXT/LINK), four new tables (`hive_word_goals`, `hive_word_logs`, `hive_buzz_posts`, `hive_buzz_likes`). Partial-unique index `hive_word_goals(hive_id, type) WHERE is_active = true` enforces one-active-per-type. CHECK constraint on `hive_buzz_posts` enforces `type='TEXT' → link_url IS NULL` and `type='LINK' → link_url IS NOT NULL`. Composite indexes on `hive_word_logs(hive_id, logged_at DESC)` for hive-feed queries + `(hive_id, user_id, chapter_id, logged_at DESC)` for the 60s throttle lookup. Migration is idempotent via `IF NOT EXISTS` / `DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS` / `DO $$ EXCEPTION`.
- **Permission predicates** (`lib/hive/permissions.ts`, T2): `canSetWordGoal` (OWNER + MODERATOR), `canPostBuzz` (all members), `canLikeBuzz` (all members), `canEditBuzz({ authorId }, role, viewerId)` (author OR OWNER/MOD). 84 permission tests green (67 H3 baseline + 17 new for the 4 predicates × 4 roles + author-bypass variants).
- **Pure helpers** (T3 `lib/hive/word-delta.ts`, T5 `lib/hive/goal-progress.ts`): `computeWordDelta(current, priorSum)` = `current - priorSum` (signed; deletes can be negative). `computeGoalEndDate(type, start)` for DAILY=+1d / WEEKLY=+7d / MONTHLY=+30d flat / CUSTOM=null (caller supplies). Timezone-naive ms math; half-open `[start, end)` intervals so a goal's "today" is `[startDate, startDate+1d)`. `pickPrimaryActiveGoal` precedence DAILY > WEEKLY > MONTHLY > CUSTOM. `aggregateGoalProgress(logs, goal)` sums `wordsDelta` where `loggedAt ∈ [startDate, endDate)`. `aggregateContributorBreakdown` returns per-userId sum sorted desc. 13/13 unit tests across both helper files.
- **`logHiveWordDelta` save-hook** (`lib/hive/log-word-delta.ts`, T4): `saveChapterAction` calls it AFTER the chapter tx commits + after the snapshot block, **outside any tx, wrapped in try/catch** so a logging failure cannot break the user's chapter save (mirrors snapshot precedent). 60s throttle per `(hive_id, user_id, chapter_id)` via the composite index — if the most recent log within 60s exists, skip. Delta = `currentWordCount - SUM(wordsDelta) over prior logs for that user+chapter` (signed). Zero-delta rows skipped entirely. Resolves the chapter's hive via H1's `getBookHive(bookId)` (React `cache()`-memoized) so books with no hive are no-ops.
- **Word-goal server actions** (`lib/actions/hive-word-goals.actions.ts`, T6): six actions — `createWordGoalAction` / `updateWordGoalAction` / `archiveWordGoalAction` / `listHiveWordGoalsAction` / `getWordGoalProgressAction` / `getActiveWordGoalSummaryAction`. **`createWordGoalAction` runs `UPDATE … SET is_active=false WHERE hive_id=$1 AND type=$2 AND is_active=true` BEFORE the INSERT inside the same tx** to avoid the partial-unique index violating on the upsert path (single-writer safe). Defensive Postgres 23505 catch returns `GOAL_ALREADY_ACTIVE` for the concurrent-writer race. **`listHiveWordGoalsAction` runs the lazy auto-archive sweep** (`UPDATE hive_word_goals SET is_active=false WHERE hive_id=$1 AND is_active=true AND end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date < now()`) BEFORE the SELECT — no cron. `getActiveWordGoalSummaryAction` wrapped in React `cache()` so the hive layout's sidebar badge + the Word Goals page share one query per request.
- **Word-log action** (`lib/actions/hive-word-logs.actions.ts`, T7): `getRecentWordLogsAction({ hiveId, limit, cursor })` — cursor-paginated by `(loggedAt DESC, id)` tuple for stable pagination across same-millisecond rows. Joins `userProfiles` for actor display + `binderItems.title` for chapter title (chapters table has no title column).
- **Buzz server actions** (`lib/actions/hive-buzz.actions.ts`, T8): five actions — `createBuzzPostAction` / `updateBuzzPostAction` / `deleteBuzzPostAction` / `listBuzzPostsAction` / `toggleBuzzLikeAction`. `createBuzzPostAction` wraps INSERT + `recordHiveActivityTx({ type: 'buzz_posted', payload: { type, bodyExcerpt, linkUrl } })` inside the same tx (atomic). **`toggleBuzzLikeAction` is per-call state-correct** (not HTTP idempotent — double-click serializes to like→unlike): looks up existing like row, INSERTS-or-DELETES + UPDATEs `hive_buzz_posts.like_count` (denorm) in the same tx. `listBuzzPostsAction` uses **two-queries-with-Map** for `viewerLiked` — fetch posts page → IN-list query for likes by viewer → assemble in JS (matches H1's `getHiveActivityFeed` precedent; the codebase has no correlated `EXISTS` subquery precedent).
- **Word-goals page + modals + sidebar badge** (T9-T11): server `/hive/[hiveId]/word-goals/page.tsx` parallel-fetches `listHiveWordGoalsAction` + `getRecentWordLogsAction`. **`<WordGoalsPageShell>`** client component composes active goals strip (up to 4 cards) + contributors panel (per-member word-delta breakdown within the primary goal's `[startDate, endDate)` window) + recent activity (last 20 logs, "Load older" via `useTransition`) + collapsible History accordion at bottom. **`<GoalCard>`** with progress bar + words/target + relative time-remaining + kebab (Edit/Archive) gated by `canSetWordGoal`. **`<NewGoalModal>`** with 4-card type picker, target words input, start date (defaults to now), end date (auto-derived for non-CUSTOM via `computeGoalEndDate`, editable). **`<EditGoalModal>`** pre-fills `targetWords` + `endDate` only (type + startDate locked since they'd change the aggregation window). Archive via shared `ConfirmDialog`. **Sidebar badge** in `/hive/[hiveId]/layout.tsx`: server fetches `getActiveWordGoalSummaryAction` (React `cache()`-deduplicated with the page below it), passes `progressPct: number | null` as a prop into the client sidebar; a 2px brand-yellow fill on a chrome track renders under the "Word Goals" nav row when `progressPct !== null`.
- **Buzz page + modals + like flow** (T12-T13): server `/hive/[hiveId]/buzz/page.tsx` calls `listBuzzPostsAction`. **`<BuzzBoardShell>`** client component renders reverse-chron `<BuzzPostCard>`s + sticky "+ New Buzz" CTA gated by `canPostBuzz`. **TEXT cards** preserve line breaks. **LINK cards** render the URL as a clickable link with the hostname shown beneath the caption (`rel="noopener noreferrer"`). Like button has filled/unfilled heart + count; uses `useTransition` to disable during the optimistic flip + rollback on action failure. Kebab Edit/Delete gated by `canEditBuzz`. **`<ComposeBuzzModal>`** two-tab (Text/Link) — LINK URL validates HTML5 `type=url` + client `new URL()` + `protocol === 'https:'` check, mirroring server Zod. **`<EditBuzzModal>`** pre-filled with type locked + "(edited)" indicator appears on cards once `updatedAt > createdAt` (matches discussion-thread convention). Delete via shared `ConfirmDialog`.
- **Activity event policy** (T14): **`buzz_posted` is the ONLY H4 activity event** — word-goal create/archive/update and word-log inserts deliberately DO NOT fire activity events (feed-clutter avoidance). The buzz event is fired INSIDE the create tx (atomic with the post insert). Payload shape `{ type: 'TEXT' | 'LINK', bodyExcerpt: string, linkUrl: string | null }` documented inline at the `recordHiveActivityTx` call. **`activity-event-row.tsx`** got a `buzzVerb(payload)` narrowing helper: TEXT renders "posted: '<bodyExcerpt-truncated-to-80>...'", LINK renders "shared a link to <hostname>" via `new URL(linkUrl).hostname`, with safe fallbacks for malformed payloads.

**Known follow-ups (deferred, non-blocking):** `EditGoalModal` and `NewGoalModal` could fold into a single component with a `mode` prop. LINK URL validation lives in three places (Zod, HTML5, client `new URL()`) which is intentional defense-in-depth but slightly duplicative.

**Patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
1. **Outside-tx best-effort logging.** Both `saveChapterAction`'s snapshot block and H4's `logHiveWordDelta` write outside the source tx, wrapped in try/catch — a logging failure must never break the user's save. Future async side-effects on save should follow this posture.
2. **Lazy-archive sweep on list.** Goals whose `end_date < now()` get `is_active=false` via an UPDATE that runs BEFORE the SELECT in `listHiveWordGoalsAction` — avoids needing a cron. Any future "expires automatically" pattern should follow this.
3. **In-tx archive-before-insert for partial-unique constraints.** Postgres partial-unique indexes (like `hive_word_goals(hive_id, type) WHERE is_active`) need the prior winner archived in the SAME tx as the new insert; otherwise the index throws. Defensive 23505 catch handles the concurrent-writer race.
4. **React `cache()` for shared server-side fetches between layout + page.** When both the layout and the page need the same query (e.g. `getActiveWordGoalSummaryAction` for sidebar badge + page tile), wrap in `cache()` so it dedupes per request.
5. **Counter denorm with in-tx update.** `hive_buzz_posts.like_count` is bumped in the same tx as the `hive_buzz_likes` row insert/delete — denorm and source row stay atomic.
6. **Two-queries-with-Map for correlated joins.** Codebase has no `EXISTS` subquery precedent — `listBuzzPostsAction`'s `viewerLiked` resolution follows H1's `getHiveActivityFeed` pattern (fetch primaries → IN-list secondaries → assemble in JS).

**Test count:** 424/424 (398 H3 baseline + ~13 word-delta/goal-progress + ~13 permission additions). tsc clean throughout.

**Carry-forward smoke checklist for Chris:**
1. **Word goal create flow:** as OWNER/MOD → `/hive/[hiveId]/word-goals` → `+ Add Goal` → pick DAILY, target 500 words, default endDate → Save → confirm tile appears with 0% progress.
2. **Word-log auto-flow:** open studio editor on the hive's book → type some new words → wait for autosave → reload `/hive/[hiveId]/word-goals` → confirm progress bar advanced + your username appears in the contributor breakdown + recent activity feed shows a row.
3. **Sidebar badge:** open the hive sidebar → confirm a small progress bar appears under "Word Goals" with the current %.
4. **Lazy auto-archive:** set `endDate` to a past timestamp via direct DB edit (or wait, or temporarily edit your local clock) → reload `/hive/[hiveId]/word-goals` → confirm the goal moves to History section without manual intervention.
5. **Archive button:** archive an active goal → confirm it disappears from the active tile + reappears in History.
6. **Buzz TEXT post:** as any member → `/hive/[hiveId]/buzz` → `+ New Buzz` → type a TEXT body → Post → confirm the card appears at top of feed + `/community` shows a `buzz_posted` event row reading `"posted: '<excerpt>...'"`.
7. **Buzz LINK post:** same flow but pick LINK type + paste a URL (e.g. https://anthropic.com) + optional caption → confirm card renders the URL as a clickable link with the hostname displayed + `/community` event reads `"shared a link to anthropic.com"`.
8. **Like flow:** click the heart on a buzz post → confirm it fills + count increments → click again → confirm it un-fills + count decrements. Rapid double-click should disable briefly via `useTransition`.
9. **Edit + delete:** as author or OWNER/MOD → kebab → Edit → change body → save → confirm `(edited)` indicator appears. Delete via kebab → `ConfirmDialog` → confirm card disappears.

### Hives Redesign — H3 Collaboration Core ✅ COMPLETE (2026-06-01)

Third of 5 sub-projects. Lands the four collaboration surfaces — annotations, edit suggestions, chapter submissions, and topic-tagged discussions — on top of H2's binder mirror. 21 tasks across schema → predicates → TipTap marks → mark helpers → server actions → UI → audit → ship. End-to-end: a CONTRIBUTOR can draft a chapter in the hive submissions composer → submit for review → OWNER/MODERATOR approves on a privileged binder-create path that lands a new chapter in the linked book with `chapters.author_user_id = submitter` (which then surfaces as a "Written by @submitter" sub-byline on the public reader). Any hive member can highlight prose in the studio editor or hive chapter view and either annotate (5-layer enum) or propose a suggested edit; both paint TipTap marks that survive subsequent author edits. OWNER/MOD bulk-review pending suggestions with inline diffs + Accept (surgical `applySuggestionToDoc` mutation) / Reject. Discussions support topic-pill filtering + flat one-level replies. Eight new activity event types fire into /community via a new tx-aware `recordHiveActivityTx(tx, opts)` writer — replies skip events to avoid feed flood.

**Task SHAs:** T1 `9eba5a5` schema migration · T2 `bb62c83` permission predicates · T3 `3e0e5ad` TipTap marks · T4 `0c8f5de` mark scanning helpers · T5 `c26560f` applySuggestionToDoc · T6 `504ab1d` annotation actions · T7 `cfe940f` suggestion actions · T8 `27410b6` submission actions · T9 `48621e7` discussion actions · T10 `a2d392b` selection popover + modals · T11 `3c14f1e` collaboration gutter · T12 `2849c88` studio editor wiring · T13 `b57090f` hive chapter view · T14 `1dfb504` public reader sub-byline + marks · T15+T16+T17 `6e32e03` submissions UI (list/composer/dynamic page) · T18 `0ba57d0` suggestions bulk-review · T19 `53741a7` discussions list/thread/compose · T20+T21 SHIP commit.

- **Schema migration** (`scripts/migrate-h3.ts`, T1): 7 ✓ idempotent steps. `hive_comments` → `hive_annotations` with new columns (`layer annotation_layer NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GENERAL'`, `parent_id` self-FK CASCADE, `selected_text`, `resolved_by`, `resolved_at`, booleanized `resolved`, `content` → `body` rename, integer cast on `selection_start`/`end`); `hive_suggestions` reshaped (drop `original_text`/`suggested_text`/`diff`/`status`, add `selection_start`/`end`/`original_excerpt`/`suggested_text`/`parent_id`/`resolved`/`resolved_by`/`resolved_at`/`accepted_at`); `hive_submissions` reshaped to carry draft content inline (drop `chapter_id`/`status`/`reviewer_note`/`submitter_id`; add `user_id`/`title`/`content jsonb`/`word_count`/`target_chapter_order`/`draft_status` CHECK ∈ DRAFT/PENDING/APPROVED/REJECTED + `created_chapter_id`/`reviewed_by`/`reviewed_at`/`review_note`); `chapters.author_user_id` added (NULL = book owner, non-null = submitter); `hive_discussion_posts.topic discussion_topic` added with `topic_only_on_top_level` CHECK; `hive_chapter_locks` dropped (unused). Two new pgEnums (`annotation_layer` GRAMMAR/PLOT/TONE/CONTINUITY/GENERAL, `discussion_topic` GENERAL/WORLDBUILDING/FEEDBACK/OFF_TOPIC). **T9 schema fix shipped in same milestone:** `hive_discussion_posts.parent_id` FK switched from ON DELETE SET NULL → ON DELETE CASCADE (the SET NULL combined with topic CHECK would have errored on parent delete since the reply's `topic IS NULL` would violate CHECK after `parent_id` became null).
- **Permission predicates** added to `lib/hive/permissions.ts` (T2): `canPostDiscussion` (all members), `canReviewSuggestion` (OWNER/MODERATOR), `canResolveAnnotation` (book owner OR annotation author), `canEditDiscussionPost` (post author OR OWNER/MOD). 67/67 permission tests green. Dead `lib/actions/hive-collab.actions.ts` (zero callers — legacy lockChapter/createHiveComment etc.) deleted in the same pass. **Known caveat:** existing `canSubmitChapter` is strict equality `=== 'CONTRIBUTOR'`, so MODERATOR can't draft/submit chapters today — likely intentional but flagged for predicate-widening if MODERATOR should also submit.
- **TipTap marks** (`lib/tiptap-extensions/`, T3): `HiveAnnotationMark` (attrs: `annotationId`, `layer`) and `HiveSuggestionMark` (attrs: `suggestionId`). Both `inclusive: false`, `excludes: ''`, parseHTML on `span[data-…]`, renderHTML emits `<span class="hive-annotation|hive-suggestion" data-…>`. Custom commands via TS module augmentation on `@tiptap/core`. Per-layer CSS in `app/globals.css` (5 layer accent colors via `[data-layer]` selectors). `.public-reader .hive-annotation, .public-reader .hive-suggestion { background: transparent; border-bottom: none }` reset so marks ride along in saved docs but stay invisible to logged-out viewers. Round-trip tests via `@tiptap/html` (devDep, exact-pinned at 3.23.1 to match the tiptap ecosystem — future bumps must move all packages together).
- **Mark scanning helpers** (`lib/tiptap-extensions/mark-scanning.ts`, T4): `findMarkRanges(doc, markName)` + `findOrphanMarks(doc, markName, attrKey, dbIds)` — pure ProseMirror JSON walkers (no `@tiptap/pm` runtime import). Coalesce adjacent text nodes with same mark + same attrs into a single range; block-element boundaries break ranges. Used by the gutter UI's orphan section and by `getChapterAnnotationsAction` / `getChapterSuggestionsAction`.
- **Server-side suggestion application** (`lib/tiptap-extensions/apply-suggestion.ts`, T5): `applySuggestionToDoc(doc, suggestionId, replacementText) → { doc, found }`. Carries sibling marks (bold/italic) onto the replacement text. Handles drifted marks correctly — replacement lands at the mark's CURRENT range, not the original `selection_start`. Multi-block suggestions: only the first block's run is replaced (subsequent blocks left untouched via `found` flag). Orphan short-circuits with `found: false`.
- **`patchDocWithMark`** (`lib/tiptap-extensions/patch-mark.ts`): pure JSON walker that mutates a doc to add a mark over `[selectionStart, selectionEnd]`. Uses a transient `__split__` sentinel for text nodes straddling the range boundary; sentinel flattens in the parent's content array and never escapes. **Pattern note:** this helper lives in `lib/tiptap-extensions/` (not co-located with annotation/suggestion actions) because `'use server'` modules require every export to be async — sync helpers must live in a non-'use server' sibling.
- **Annotation actions** (`lib/actions/hive-annotations.actions.ts`, T6): 5 actions — `createAnnotationAction` (in-tx: insert row → `patchDocWithMark` mutates chapter content → fires `annotation_added`), `replyToAnnotationAction` (no event, no mark — inherits parent range; selection cols null), `resolveAnnotationAction` / `unresolveAnnotationAction` (permission via `canResolveAnnotation`), `getChapterAnnotationsAction` (rows + orphanRowIds + nested `author` / `resolver` author-profile objects). Tx-aware `recordHiveActivityTx(tx, opts)` writer appended to `lib/hive/record-activity.ts` (with `DrizzleTx` type extracted via `Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0]`).
- **Suggestion actions** (`lib/actions/hive-suggestions.actions.ts`, T7): 6 actions — `createSuggestionAction` (in-tx: insert + `patchDocWithMark` + fires `suggestion_proposed`), `replyToSuggestionAction`, `acceptSuggestionAction` (in-tx: `applySuggestionToDoc` → recompute wordCount → bump book.updatedAt → mark resolved+accepted → fires `suggestion_accepted`; snapshot OUTSIDE tx via existing 60s throttle gated on BOOK OWNER's premium status), `rejectSuggestionAction` (no doc change; fires `suggestion_rejected`), `getChapterSuggestionsAction`, `getPendingSuggestionsForHiveAction` (joins suggestions ↔ chapters ↔ binderItems ↔ userProfiles; orders by `binderItems.order ASC`; computes `hasReplies` via secondary `inArray(parentId, topLevelIds)` query). **Orphan path on accept:** when `applySuggestionToDoc` returns `found: false`, the suggestion is resolved without doc mutation AND the activity event is fired as `suggestion_rejected` with `payload.orphan: true` — consumers must read the payload, not the event type, to distinguish rejection-vs-orphan.
- **Submission actions** (`lib/actions/hive-submissions.actions.ts`, T8): 6 actions — `saveSubmissionDraftAction` (upsert; no id → create DRAFT row, id present → update only if user owns row + draftStatus still DRAFT; BETA_READER blocked), `submitSubmissionAction` (DRAFT→PENDING; fires `chapter_submitted`), `approveSubmissionAction` (**the privileged binder-create path** — BYPASSES `requireBinderWritePermission` because it's the reviewer creating a chapter on the submitter's behalf, in-tx: shift sibling `binder_items.order` by +1 at-or-above target, insert binderItem with `authorId+lastEditedBy=submitter`, insert chapter with `authorUserId=submitter`, mark submission APPROVED, fires `chapter_submitted_approved`), `rejectSubmissionAction` (requires non-empty review note via Zod min(1); fires `chapter_submitted_rejected`), `getSubmissionAction` (positional `(id)`, viewer must be submitter OR reviewer), `listHiveSubmissionsAction` (3-section payload role-scoped: myDrafts / mySubmissions / allInHive — last gated on `canReviewSubmissions`).
- **Discussion actions** (`lib/actions/hive-discussions.actions.ts`, T9): 6 actions — `createDiscussionPostAction` (top-level only; topic required; fires `discussion_posted`), `replyToDiscussionPostAction` (one level only — returns `REPLY_DEPTH_EXCEEDED` if `parent.parentId !== null`, checked BEFORE membership so unauthenticated probing can't differentiate), `editDiscussionPostAction` / `deleteDiscussionPostAction` (permission via `canEditDiscussionPost`), `listDiscussionPostsAction` (multi-topic filter; reply counts + last-activity computed via two-query stitch-in-JS — top-level posts query → inArray reply query → aggregate in a Map), `getDiscussionThreadAction`. Body stored as PLAIN TEXT not TipTap JSON; titles derived at read time via `body.slice(0, 80)` (no title column). Legacy `getDiscussionPostsAction` / `createDiscussionPostAction` / `deleteDiscussionPostAction` deleted from `hive-content.actions.ts` in the same commit (zero external callers).
- **Selection popover + modals** in `components/hive/collab/` (T10): `lib/hooks/use-selection-popover.ts` returns `{from, to, text, anchorRect, close}` on non-empty TipTap selection (listens to `selectionUpdate`/`blur`, DOMRect via `editor.view.coordsAtPos(from)`). `<SelectionPopover>` is a portaled fixed-position floating element (Radix Popover skipped — selection ranges aren't anchorable elements). `<AnnotateModal>` shadcn Dialog with 5 horizontal layer-radio cards + body textarea + selected-text snippet. `<SuggestModal>` same shape with strikethrough preview + replacement textarea + optional rationale. Selection state captured into modal-local state on open so the dialog focus shift doesn't wipe `{from, to, text}` mid-submit.
- **Collaboration gutter** `<CollaborationGutter>` (T11): 320px expanded / 32px collapsed (controlled by parent). Cards positioned absolutely via `editor.view.coordsAtPos(row.selectionStart)` inside `useLayoutEffect` (recomputed on update/selectionUpdate/resize/scroll, try/catch around the call, ESTIMATED_HEIGHT=84px collision-avoidance heuristic for dense ranges). Flow-layout fallback when editor is null. `<GutterFilterStrip>` 8 pills (All + 5 layers + Suggestions + Resolved), multi-select, localStorage key `collab:filter:<chapterId>`. AnnotationCard: paper-100 chrome + per-layer left-edge border. SuggestionCard: warm-gold border + inline diff + Accept/Reject gated on `canReviewSuggestion`. OrphanSection footer pill expands a list with Dismiss buttons. Hook `useCollabData({chapterId, hiveId})` Promise.alls `getChapterAnnotationsAction` + `getChapterSuggestionsAction`, exposes `refresh` + `mutate.{...}`.
- **Studio editor wiring** (`chapter-editor.tsx`, T12): H3 marks registered unconditionally in TipTap extensions array (safe for non-hive books). `useEditor` keyed on `[activeItemId, chapterContentVersion]` so accept-suggestion success → `reloadActiveChapter().then(() => bumpChapterContentVersion())` → editor recreates with server-fresh content. `BookEditorProvider` context extended with `chapterContentVersion`, `bumpChapterContentVersion`, `gutterOpen`, `toggleGutter`, `currentUserId`. **Mutual exclusion** between `gutterOpen` and `historyOpen` wired in both directions. VIEW-zone toolbar button (lucide MessagesSquare, aria-pressed, gated on `bookHive !== null`). **Layout decision:** gutter mounts as a sibling flex column inside chapter-editor's `flex-1 overflow-hidden` container — NOT via `right-panel-slot.tsx` (which stays metadata-or-history) — so the TipTap editor instance stays local and `coordsAtPos` anchoring remains accurate.
- **Hive chapter view** at `/hive/[hiveId]/chapters/[chapterId]` (T13): new `getHiveChapterView(hiveId, chapterId)` server action (member-scoped, cross-hive escape guard via `chapter.bookId === hive.bookId`, joins binderItems for title + userProfiles for chapter author + book owner). `<HiveChapterSurface>` is read-only TipTap (`editable={false}`) with both H3 marks registered + scoped `[data-slot="hive-chapter-pane"] .ProseMirror` styling. Mounts `<SelectionPopover>` (selectionUpdate still fires in read-only mode) + `<CollaborationGutter>`. Header byline variant: "Written by @submitter — chapter contribution to {book} by @owner" when `chapter.authorUserId !== null AND !== book.userId`. **Known caveat:** `router.refresh()` doesn't re-mount client surfaces in Next 16, so the gutter's `useCollabData` may not auto-refetch after a new annotation is created from this page — wire a real `refresh-nonce` prop on `<CollaborationGutter>` if smoke shows missed updates.
- **Public reader sub-byline + H3 marks** (T14): reader's chapter projection extended with `authorUserId` + leftJoins for chapter contributor + book owner profiles. New `<ChapterContributionByline>` between title and prose, only when chapter has a non-null `authorUserId` distinct from `book.userId`. **Public reader doesn't use TipTap's `generateHTML` or `useEditor`** — it has a custom server-side serializer at `lib/export/tiptap-to-html.ts` (switch on node + mark types → emit HTML strings → `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`). "Registering" the H3 marks meant extending the serializer's mark switch with `hiveAnnotation` + `hiveSuggestion` cases. `.public-reader` wrapper added around the prose div so T3's transparency reset hides marks from logged-out viewers.
- **Submissions UI** at `/hive/[hiveId]/submissions/` (T15-T17, `6e32e03`): list page with 3 role-scoped sections (My drafts / My submissions / All in this hive — last gated on `canReviewSubmissions`); `+ New Submission` CTA disabled with tooltip when `!canSubmitChapter`; status pills via `--status-idea / warning / success / error`. `/submissions/new` composer (server-redirects BETA_READER per `canSubmitChapter`): title contenteditable + target-order native `<select>` + StarterKit TipTap (no H3 marks — drafts don't carry annotation/suggestion marks) + 800ms debounced `saveSubmissionDraftAction`. **Auto-save → router.replace dance** in 'new' mode: first save returns the new id → `router.replace` to `/submissions/[id]` so future saves carry the id (editor instance survives the URL change because Next preserves the client component tree when the route renders the same shape). `/submissions/[submissionId]` dynamic page branches: DRAFT+owner → SubmissionComposer; PENDING+canReview → SubmissionReview (Approve/Reject); else → SubmissionRead.
- **Bulk suggestion review** at `/hive/[hiveId]/suggestions` (T18): server gates on `canReviewSuggestion` — non-reviewer sees ComingSoon (`phase` prop, not `message`). Reviewers get `<SuggestionsByChapter>`: header with total pending count; per chapter group (clickable to collapse) a chapter title linked to T13's view + per-suggestion row (avatar + @author + relTime + inline diff + "Has replies" badge + Accept/Reject/Open buttons). Accept calls `acceptSuggestionAction(id)` → toast "Accepted" or "Was orphaned" depending on `result.data.orphan`. Reject opens inline custom Dialog (NOT ConfirmDialog — that has no body slot) with optional reviewNote textarea. Open button is a deep link `/hive/[hiveId]/chapters/[chapterId]#sug-{id}` — T13's hive chapter view doesn't yet scroll-to-fragment (future follow-up).
- **Discussions UI** at `/hive/[hiveId]/discussions/` (T19): list page with topic-pill multi-select + reverse-chron feed of top-level posts; thread page at `/discussions/[postId]` with flat one-level replies + reply input + `@username ` prepend on reply button; `DiscussionComposeModal` (topic radio default GENERAL + optional title + StarterKit TipTap body, no headings); `canEditDiscussionPost` gates edit/delete kebab.
- **Activity event wiring audit** (T20): the 8 H3 event types all fire from the right actions, in-tx, top-level only. T20 audit landed 5 payload fixes to match the spec: `chapter_submitted_rejected` now includes `title` (was missing); `annotation_added` now includes `annotationId` (was missing); `suggestion_proposed` now sends `{ suggestionId, chapterId, originalExcerpt, suggestedText }` (was `{ chapterId, excerpt }`); `suggestion_accepted` now sends `{ suggestionId, chapterId }` (was `{ chapterId, excerpt }`); `suggestion_rejected` (explicit reject path) now sends `{ suggestionId, chapterId, note }` (was `{ chapterId, excerpt, note }`); the orphan-on-accept path (also typed `suggestion_rejected`) now sends `{ suggestionId, chapterId, note: null, orphan: true }` for consumer distinguishability. Inline payload-shape comments added near each `recordHiveActivityTx` call.

**H3 patterns now load-bearing across the codebase:**
1. **Tx-aware activity writes** use `recordHiveActivityTx(tx, opts)` (sibling to legacy `recordHiveActivity`) so the activity row commits atomically with the source-row insert. Any new collab action that fires an event must use the tx-aware variant.
2. **Server-side TipTap mutators** (`apply-suggestion.ts`, `patch-mark.ts`) are pure JSON walkers with no `@tiptap/pm` runtime dependency — sibling pattern to `mark-scanning.ts`. The walkers operate on ProseMirror JSON shapes directly.
3. **`'use server'` modules cannot export sync helpers** — every export must be async. Pure helpers must live in a non-'use server' sibling module. This forced `patchDocWithMark` out of the actions file and into `lib/tiptap-extensions/patch-mark.ts`.
4. **Public reader uses `lib/export/tiptap-to-html.ts`** (custom server-side serializer with `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`), NOT TipTap's `generateHTML` or `useEditor`. Any new TipTap mark must extend that file's mark switch to render in public surfaces.
5. **`.public-reader` wrapper class** is the gate for hiding H3 marks from logged-out viewers. Add new annotation/suggestion-like mark CSS with a matching `.public-reader .<mark-class>` transparency reset.
6. **Top-level only fires activity events.** Replies on annotations / suggestions / discussions emit nothing. Inline comments near every `recordHiveActivityTx` call document the policy.
7. **Privileged binder-create on submission approval.** `approveSubmissionAction` does NOT route through `createBinderItemAction` or `requireBinderWritePermission` — it writes directly to `binder_items` + `chapters` in its own transaction, with `chapters.author_user_id` set to the submitter. This is the documented exception to H2's permission gate.
8. **Doc-as-source-of-truth for ranges.** Annotation + suggestion rows store an initial `selection_start`/`selection_end` for display, but the AUTHORITATIVE range is the TipTap mark on the chapter doc — survives author edits naturally. `acceptSuggestionAction` uses the mark's CURRENT range via `applySuggestionToDoc` → `findMarkRanges`, not the stored `selection_start`.

**Known follow-ups / inconsistencies (deferred, not blocking):**
- `getChapterAnnotationsAction(chapterId, hiveId)` is positional but `getChapterSuggestionsAction({chapterId, hiveId})` is object — normalize later.
- Replies on `hive_suggestions` use sentinel `''`/`0`/`0`/`''` values for `selectionStart`/`End`/`originalExcerpt`/`suggestedText` because T1 schema declared those NOT NULL. Projection layer casts back to null when `parentId !== null`. T1 follow-up: make those four columns nullable.
- `getSubmissionAction(id)` is positional, not `{id}`. Adapted at call site but inconsistent with object-arg neighbors.
- `canSubmitChapter` strict equality blocks MODERATOR from submitting chapters — flag for predicate-widening if intended.
- T13 hive chapter view + T18 suggestions bulk-review's `router.refresh()` may not auto-refetch the gutter's `useCollabData`; wire a real `refresh-nonce` prop on `<CollaborationGutter>` if smoke shows missed updates.
- T13 hive chapter view has no scroll-to-fragment for `#sug-<id>` deep links yet.

**Carry-forward smoke checklist for Chris:**

H3 is server-clean (tsc + 342/342 tests) but every UI surface still needs Chris-eyes-on. Suggested smoke order — each step is independently meaningful, can be skipped:

1. **Submission happy path:** as a CONTRIBUTOR member of a hive linked to a book → `/hive/[hiveId]/submissions/new` → title + body + target-order → wait for auto-save status → Submit → confirm toast + redirect to canonical URL → switch to OWNER account → `/hive/[hiveId]/submissions/[id]` → Approve → confirm new chapter appears in the book's binder at the target order + chapter's author byline reads "@submitter" in the studio binder row + `/community` shows the `chapter_submitted_approved` event row.
2. **Submission reject path:** repeat (1) but Reject with a review note → submitter's `/submissions/[id]` shows REJECTED status + review-note rendered → `/community` shows the `chapter_submitted_rejected` event row.
3. **Annotation flow:** open the studio editor on a chapter that's part of a hive → select prose → selection popover should appear → Annotate → pick a layer + body → Submit → confirm gutter card appears with the chosen layer color and your handle as author + `/community` shows `annotation_added` event row.
4. **Annotation from hive chapter view:** `/hive/[hiveId]/chapters/[chapterId]` as a non-author hive member → same flow as (3) → confirm card appears in the gutter (if it doesn't auto-refresh, that's the known T13 caveat — reload the page).
5. **Suggestion happy path:** select prose in the studio editor → Suggest → enter replacement → Submit → switch to OWNER → `/hive/[hiveId]/suggestions` → confirm pending suggestion appears in the grouped-by-chapter view → Accept → confirm: (a) chapter content reflects the replacement, (b) word count recomputed, (c) `/community` shows `suggestion_accepted` event row.
6. **Suggestion orphan path:** create a suggestion → author edits the chapter to delete the surrounding paragraph → as reviewer click Accept → confirm toast says "Suggestion was orphaned" and the chapter content is unchanged → confirm `/community` event row is `suggestion_rejected` with the orphan distinguishing payload (visible only via DB inspection, not on the row itself).
7. **Suggestion explicit reject:** create a suggestion → reviewer clicks Reject + adds note → confirm dialog closes + suggestion disappears from list + `/community` shows `suggestion_rejected` event row.
8. **Discussions:** `/hive/[hiveId]/discussions` → + New Post → pick a topic + body → confirm post appears at top of list and `/community` shows `discussion_posted` event row → open the thread → reply → confirm reply appears below the top post + the parent post's reply count bumped → as a NON-author non-MOD member, confirm edit/delete kebab is hidden on the top post.
9. **Topic filtering:** with multiple posts across topics, multi-select topic pills → confirm list filters correctly.
10. **Reply depth:** try to reply to a reply (via the API if no UI surface exposes the affordance) → confirm `REPLY_DEPTH_EXCEEDED` error.
11. **Public reader sub-byline:** open `/books/[bookId]/read/[chapterId]` for a chapter that came via submission approval → confirm "Written by @submitter — chapter contribution to *Book* by @owner" appears between title and prose → confirm annotation/suggestion marks are invisible (no underlines/highlights) to logged-out viewers.
12. **Studio editor gutter toggle:** open a hive-linked book in the studio → toolbar MessagesSquare button → confirm gutter opens + history drawer closes (and vice versa).

If any of these reveal a bug or rough edge, log it in the AGENTS.md "Next concrete step" — H3 ship is binding only on server correctness; UI smoke gaps roll forward as follow-ups.

342/342 tests, tsc clean.
